# DONE

- [2026-04-08] **Self-host GIR lowerer: 552/898 (61.4%) function signatures match**: Generic monomorphization — scan call sites with explicit type args, collect function/equip templates, emit monomorphized instances with mangled names (`identity[int]` → `identity__int64_t`). Type substitution via `type_subs` field on GirModule. Generic equip blocks monomorphized for all discovered type instances (supports multiple equip blocks per generic type for derives). Trait-prefix naming for explicit trait impls (`From__int64_t_for_Pair__from`), derive-generated traits use short form (`Point__eq`). Static methods in trait impls skip self param. Type variable filtering prevents emitting template stubs with unresolved `T`. 898/898 fixtures process without crash, 0 regressions. Remaining: 117 imported functions (need reachability), 76 error tests (self-host processes them), 35 `*mut` generic String params, 26 trait `*unit` self types.
- [2026-04-08] **Fix collection reassignment leak (P6)**: `v = new_v` now drops old `v` before assigning. Root cause: `needs_drop` for collections used `ctx.drops.is_moved()` which was always false for non-moved locals. Fixed to use `ctx.type_registry.needs_drop()` (same as other types). The RHS is fully computed and cloned before the Drop fires, so self-referential RHS like `v = v.slice(...)` is safe. Test fixture baseline adjusted to capture `mem_live()` after initial allocation. 3 remaining leak patterns → 2.
- [2026-04-08] **Self-host GIR lowerer: 474/896 (52.9%) function signatures match**: Tuple return types (`Tuple__int64_t__int64_t`), &self/!self MutPtr distinction, resource type scanning (struct+enum field analysis), equip method emission with correct self ownership, ownership numbering fix (self-host OWN_MUTABLE=1 vs Rust MutableBorrow=2). Fixed compiler bug: return inside tail match expression overwritten by jump. 896/896 fixtures process without crash.
- [2026-04-08] **Ptr-scrutinee pattern extraction widened + Option[Ptr(T)]→Option[T] return conversion**: (A) `patterns.rs`: enum field extraction on borrowed scrutinees now produces `Ptr(field_type)` for all resource types except Box (which needs value for `*` deref). (B) `stmts/mod.rs`: `lower_return` detects `Option__Ref_T` → `Option__T` mismatch and auto-clones: branch on tag, deref+clone Some payload, rewrap. Prevents buffer overflow from sizeof mismatch. Json.get()/at() still use explicit `.clone()` — removing it blocked on Ptr method dispatch for pattern-extracted locals.
- [2026-04-08] **Json.get()/at() return Option[Json] with owned clones**: Changed from `Json` by-value (shallow copy sharing internal data) to `Option[Json]` with explicit `.clone()` inside. Callers use `.unwrap()` for the value, `.unwrap_or(Null())` for fallback. Fixes double-free when document drops cascade into values returned by get(). Updated all callers in json.gg (JsonDeserializer), jsonpath.gg (14 sites), and test fixtures (json_parse, json_edge_cases, httpserver_json). 943/943 tests pass.
- [2026-04-08] **Fix formatter f-string double-brace escaping**: `format_string_escape` didn't re-escape `{`/`}` as `{{`/`}}` in Format-kind strings, breaking formatter idempotency for f-strings with literal braces.
- [2026-04-08] **Fix array literal elem_drop — synthesize original_name for gorget_array_new**: Array literals (`[]` and `[a, b, c]`) called raw `gorget_array_new` without element type info, so the C backend couldn't set `elem_drop`/`elem_clone`. Fix: in `emit_extern_call`, when `original_name == "gorget_array_new"` and GIR args contain `SizeOf(type_id)`, synthesize `Vector__{type_name}__new` as the original_name. Restricted to string/collection element types — user types with Recursive drops have shallow clone semantics that break when elem_drop cascades. Fixes `Vector[String]`, `Vector[Vector[int]]` element leaks.
- [2026-04-08] **Revert match destructuring widening (is_resource_type → owned_string_type)**: Widening from `owned_string_type` to `is_resource_type` caused use-after-free: (1) user types with Recursive drops (Json) cascade into shared data, (2) extracted values that are returned aren't move-zeroed, so the drop frees the returned value. Reverted to string-only. Collection/struct patterns remain as known leaks, blocked on proper move-zero tracking.
- [2026-04-07] **Fix Result[String, String] double-free — Ok/Some variant construction skipped clone for borrowed params**: `Ok(name)` and `Some(name)` used raw `builder.enum_init` bypassing `emit_enum_init_owned`, so BareParam resource args (borrowed strings) were shallow-copied into enum fields without cloning. The caller then freed the original, leaving the enum field as a dangling pointer. The `Error()` path already used `emit_enum_init_owned` correctly. Fixed all three (`Ok`, `Some`, both `Ok` branches) to use `emit_enum_init_owned` which includes `clone_resource_args_for_init`. Added `result_string_string.gg` regression test.
- [2026-04-07] **Self-host GIR lowerer produces output**: `gir.gg` (data structures), `lower.gg` (AST→GIR), `format_gir.gg` (text formatter) — 716 LOC. Correctly lowers `int add(int a, int b): return a + b` + `void main(): print(x)`. Two compiler fixes unblocked it: (1) empty array `[]` now infers element size from expected_type via struct field propagation, (2) struct literal lowering sets expected_type per field. Processes all 860+ fixtures without crashing.
- [2026-04-07] **String reassignment clone elision + closure capture move optimization**: Two optimizations: (1) `s = expr` skips `gorget_string_clone_to_owned` when the RHS is a `fresh_string` (user function call result — independent buffer, cannot be a view into the old LHS). Eliminates redundant allocation for `s = compute()` patterns. (2) Closure captures of CoW Ptr(T) aliases skip cloning when the captured variable is dead after capture (`is_last_use_at`). The closure takes ownership via deref+move instead of clone. Added `closure_span` parameter to `lower_closure` for liveness checking. 975 unit + 908 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **Fix match arm move-zero leak — is_moved guard skipped across exclusive arms**: `mark_moved` from arm 1 caused arm 2+ to skip `move_zero` of the original scrutinee variable. Arms are mutually exclusive at runtime — each must independently zero. Removed `is_moved` guard. Combined with self-host Gorget fixes (removed `!` on pattern bindings, direct Dict.put), type checker goes from 406 crashes → 0 crashes, 36 matches → 845/861 (98.1%). 975 unit + 908 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **Pattern extraction clone elision for dead scrutinees**: When a match scrutinee identifier is the last use (dead after match), pattern extraction of string fields skips `gorget_string_clone_to_owned`. The shallow copy from `enum_field_load` takes ownership directly. Both the scrutinee copy AND the original variable are MoveZeroed after extraction, preventing double-free at scope exit. Excludes Ptr scrutinees (borrowed parameters) and guarded arms. Also fixed liveness analysis to track `Is`, `Rethrow`, `Await`, `As`, `Catch`, `Spawn`, `ListComprehension`, `SetComprehension`, `OptionalChain`, `MutableBorrow`, and `Deref` expressions — previously missing, causing false "last use" reports. 975 unit + 904 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **CoW return clone elision for named locals with no borrowers**: Return clone skipped for named owned string locals when no other local shares their data via string Borrow assignment (`String b = a`). Tracks `string_borrow_sources` set — Borrow assignment marks the source local; return path checks the returned local is not in the set. Combined with Phase 1 (fresh string temps), this eliminates the return clone for `String result = f(); return result` and similar patterns. 975 unit + 903 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **CoW return clone elision for fresh string temps + flow-sensitive loop borrow**: Two optimizations: (1) String return clone skipped for unnamed temps from user function calls (fresh allocations not shared with any other variable). Uses `fresh_string_locals` set, marked in `call_tracked` for non-builtin calls (distinguished via `fn_sigs` — builtins are in fn_sigs, user functions are not). Saves 1 clone + 1 free per `return f()` / `return a + b` pattern. (2) Loop borrow propagation now uses flow-sensitive `is_cow_unsafe_at(name, stmt_span)` instead of whole-function `cow_reassigned_names` — allows CoW borrow propagation in loops when the variable is only reassigned before, not after, the borrow site. Phase 2 (pattern extraction clone elision) and Phase 3 (named-local return optimization via invisible alias tracking) deferred to TODO — require scrutinee copy chain tracking and string borrow alias tracking respectively. 975 unit + 898 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **Self-host type checker restored: 794/857 (92.6%)**: Two fixes in self-host Gorget code: (1) Remove `!` moves on pattern bindings in `typecheck.gg` — `!ptypes` in match arms double-freed the scrutinee copy's vector. Plain assignment clones correctly. (2) Replace extract-modify-putback Dict pattern in `types.gg` with direct `self.defined_cache.put()` — the `!self.defined_cache` move permanently zeroed the field. Type checker went from 36/857 to 794/857, crashes from 406 to 41.
- [2026-04-07] **Fix loop-carried variable drop — CoW Ptr→owned upgrade registered in wrong scope**: Variables starting as `Ptr` (from `.get().unwrap()` borrows) don't need dropping, so they're never registered in any drop scope. When CoW materializes them to owned inside a loop body, `update_or_register_type` couldn't find them and fell through to `register_local` — registering in the Loop scope instead of the Function scope. At loop body exit, the variable was dropped (use-after-free). Fix: fallback path in `update_or_register_type` now uses `register_local_at_function_scope`. Self-host resolver restored from 0/856 to 852/856. 975 unit + 903 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **Self-host comparison re-measurement (856 fixtures)**: Parser: 851/856 (99.4%). Resolver: 852/856 (99.5%, restored from 0%). Type checker: 436/856 (50.9%, up from 36, still 405 crashes from related struct-with-String double-free).
- [2026-04-07] **Closure return type inference for multi-line closures**: `return` inside a closure body was being checked against the enclosing function's return type, not the closure's own. The closure body block then evaluated to `void`. Fix: save/restore `current_return_type` around closure body checking, use a fresh type variable unified with the expected return type from `decl_type_hint` (if available). Non-void body types (expression closures, tail expressions) take priority; resolved type variable used for block bodies with `return`. 975 unit + 902 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **Trait-bounded generic functions monomorphize correctly**: `void print_sum[Summable T](T val)` — originally a linker error (monomorphized instance never emitted, added 2026-03-23). Fixed as side effect of IR lowering pipeline improvements since then. The generic collector discovers trait-bounded templates correctly; method dispatch resolves `Trait_for_Type__method` names via fn_sigs. Added 4 regression tests: trait method call on generic param, multiple bounds with `&`, transitive bound propagation, return value computation.
- [2026-04-07] **All ownership-boundary string clones use clone_to_owned + CoW clone auditing**: LIR IndexLoad element clone (`calls.rs:395`) and C backend deref clone (`emit_call_extern.rs:1669`) upgraded from `gorget_string_clone` to `gorget_string_clone_to_owned`. CoW materializations now emit `ImplicitCloneReason::CoWMaterialization` warnings — visible via `--show-clones` (audit), suppressed in default output (not actionable). Threaded span through `cow_before_mutation`, `cow_before_field_mutation`, `cow_sever_all_aliases_from` (10 call sites). 975 unit + 898 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **Struct clone upgraded to clone_to_owned**: `emit_recursive_struct_clones` and `deep_clone_resource_fields` now use `gorget_string_clone_to_owned` for string fields instead of view-preserving `gorget_string_clone`. CoW materializations produce independently-owned copies. Safe now that the MoveZero gap is fixed (consuming calls zero the source). 975 unit + 898 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **String audit — 7 fixes, 0 new implicit clones**: (1) MoveZero gap for callee-Move + bare-callsite: `calls.rs:123` now pushes to `pending_move_zeros` — both the Borrow and Move paths correctly zero after consuming calls. (2) `pending_move_zeros` drain in methods.rs: save baseline before arg lowering, drain after call — fixes orphaned MoveZero entries for equip method args. (3) `gorget_array_clear` calls `elem_drop` on all elements. (4) `gorget_array_remove` calls `elem_drop` on removed element. (6) `gorget_array_extend` made consuming via LIR post-call zeroing (no elem_clone, source move-zeroed). (7) `gorget_array_slice` propagates `elem_drop`/`elem_clone` and deep-clones resource elements. Struct clone view-preserving workaround deferred to IndexLoad reference semantics. 975 unit + 898 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-08] **LLVM IR backend — 310/896 fixtures pass (34.6%)**: `src/backend/llvm/mod.rs` (~2,060 LOC). Sessions pushed from 171→310 (+139). Key fixes: (1) **aarch64 ABI mismatch**: C compiler passes >16B structs by indirect reference, LLVM 14 was using registers — extern declarations now use `ptr` for aggregate params. Fixed ~50 segfaults in gorget_str_cat, gorget_string_clone, etc. (2) **Runtime struct padding**: GorgetArray (4 LIR fields, 7 C fields = 56B) had wrong LLVM size (32B), corrupting stack — added computed_c_size padding with LLVM alignment awareness. Fixed ~65 segfaults. (3) **fprintf_stderr fd skip**: args[0]=fd (null), args[1]=fmt — was dereferencing null. (4) **Void→ptr in call args**: closure env params. (5) **Option/Result unwrap type inference**: payload type from struct definition + PtrTo propagation through SlotAddr. (6) **Division-by-zero format**: fprintf(stderr,...) + exit(1) matching C backend. (7) **Store PtrTo memcpy**: Store of pointer-to-aggregate now does memcpy instead of storing the pointer value — fixes tuple/struct field initialization. (8) **Overflow trap messages**: fprintf(stderr, "gorget: integer overflow\n") + exit(1) matching C backend. (9) **Auto-declare sret for runtime aggregates**: gorget_array_clone, gorget_str_*, etc. now correctly declared with sret. (10) **StrLit PtrTo type propagation**: StrLit returns PtrTo(GorgetString), PtrCast preserves PtrTo — enables Store→memcpy for string fields in structs/tuples. (11) **InlineC fatal detection**: InlineC blocks with gorget_panic/exit/abort emit exit(1) instead of being skipped. (12) **Aggregate PtrTo in val_types**: Struct→PtrTo preserving struct identity. (13) **Cmp width mismatch**: trunc/zext for i8 vs i64 comparison operands. (14) **SlotStore scalar-to-aggregate**: store with value's actual type when extern returns wrong type. Printf bool format rewriting moved from C backend to LIR lowering. Shared `fix_printf_format()` in `src/lir/lower/calls.rs`. Remaining: 295 link errors (monomorphized wrappers), 40 llc type errors, 32 crashes, 66 mismatches.
- [2026-04-07] **Fix ref_locals not remapped after dead local elimination**: `eliminate_unused_locals` in GIR optimization remaps LocalIds in instructions and terminators but was not remapping `func.ref_locals`. After optimization removed dead locals and renumbered survivors, stale ref_local IDs aliased unrelated locals — causing the LIR to emit `SlotLoad { ty: Ptr }` (void*) for struct-typed slots, producing invalid C assignments like `void* v = struct_val`. Root cause of gorget-arena `FaceDrawRange` and `Option[int]` C compilation errors. Fix: remap ref_locals in eliminate_unused_locals using the same old→new mapping. 975 unit + 898 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-07] **CowBorrow for field-access receivers**: `self.data.get(i).unwrap()` now gets zero-cost CowBorrow provenance, same as direct local receivers. Collection identity uses `CollectionId` enum — `Local(LocalId)` for direct locals, `FieldPath(String)` for dot-separated field paths (e.g., "self.data", "game.entities"). New `extract_field_path_string` AST helper extracts paths from `FieldAccess` chains. Mutation-side calls (`cow_before_field_mutation`) added to method dispatch, index assignment, and field assignment for field-access receivers. 975 unit + 898 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-06] **Fix Ptr→void* type mapping in LIR lowering**: `map_gir_type_with_structs` now resolves ALL `Ptr(Named(X))` types to `LirType::PtrTo(sid)` — not just GorgetString. Fixes type-unsafe C codegen where pointer-to-struct slots were declared `void*` (e.g., `gpu.diffuse_textures.get(idx).unwrap() <= 0` through `&` borrow). Also fixed C backend Store emitter to treat `PtrTo` as pointer type alongside `Ptr` for direct pointer stores (was incorrectly falling through to memcpy, causing segfaults in dynamic dispatch). gorget-arena inline `.get().unwrap()` workaround reverted. Removed duplicate "CowBorrow for field-access receivers" TODO entry. 975 unit + 897 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-06] **CowBorrow with provenance — typed bindings borrow like `auto`**: `Entry e = vec.get(i).unwrap()` now produces a zero-cost borrow (same as `auto`), with clone deferred to mutation/ownership boundaries. Implementation: `CowBorrow` ownership state on unwrap results from `Option__Ref_*`; `cow_borrow_sources` side-channel maps CowBorrow locals to their source collection LocalId; provenance propagated through `.get()` → Option → `.unwrap()` chain; VarDecl recognizes CowBorrow with provenance and sets `CollectionRef` on the typed binding so `cow_before_mutation` materializes correctly. Only named-local receivers (not field-access temps) get provenance — library code like Heap stdlib still clones at typed bindings (safe). Fixed copy-on-write.md doc. 973 unit + 897 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-06] **`!self` consuming self — borrow checker enforcement**: Borrow checker now marks named receivers as moved after `!self` method calls. Uses existing `check_move` infrastructure via `method_resolutions` → `function_info.param_ownerships`. Use-after-move on named vars is correctly rejected. Temps (method chains) have no def_id and pass through safely. Loop constraint still TODO. 973 unit + 895 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-06] **`!self` consuming self for equip methods**: New parameter mode `!self` — callee receives MutPtr, resource-type field loads use MoveZeroSource + explicit field zeroing. Caller MoveZeros receiver after call. Temps auto-move (method chains). Eliminates builder-pattern clones (`struct_builder_pattern.gg` 4→0 with `!self`). 973 unit + 894 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-06] **CoW clone optimization — 3 implicit clone reductions**: (1) **VarDecl borrow propagation in loops**: bare param borrows now propagate into loop bodies when the variable is never reassigned; `cow_before_mutation` handles mutation-through-method-call correctly. (2) **Named-in-loop refinement**: locals declared inside a loop body (fresh each iteration) can now be auto-moved on last use at struct/enum init sites; `LoopInfo.pre_loop_local_count` tracks the boundary. (3) **Flow-sensitive prescan**: replaced function-wide `cow_reassigned_names` check at VarDecl aliasing site with `cow_reassigned_after` reverse-walk analysis — variables reassigned only before the alias point now get zero-cost CoW aliasing. Added `compute_cow_reassigned_after()` (same algorithm shape as liveness analysis). Remaining clone source: builder-pattern "struct field from borrow" (~16 fixtures), requires `!self` consuming-self language change. 3 new test fixtures. 973 unit + 893 integration tests pass.

- [2026-04-06] **Leak reduction — 5 bugs fixed, 5/6 stress tests leak-free**: Fixed 5 distinct memory leaks across 4 files. (1) **String reassignment AssignMode::Borrow→Move** (assigns.rs): unregistered clone intermediate was never freed; Move transfers ownership without extra clone. (2) **Function return clone_to_owned original leaked** (stmts/mod.rs): `did_clone_return` path unregistered+move-zeroed the original source, losing its allocation; now scope-exit drops free it. (3) **Dict.remove() freed key/value** (c_runtime.rs): `gorget_map_remove` now calls key_drop/val_drop before tombstoning. (4) **F-string interp Ptr deref copy untracked** (calls.rs): deref copies of Ptr params for string interpolation were not registered for drops; C backend cloned them (allocating), but the allocation was never freed. (5) **Closure expression-body missing scope-exit drops** (closures.rs): expression-body closures had no Function drop scope and no emit_early_exit_drops; added save/restore of outer drops, push Function scope, Move assignment for resource return types. stress_alloc: strings ✓, dicts ✓, closures ✓, vectors ✓ (already), structs partial (Option payload), mixed partial (Option + collection reassign). 972 unit + 890 integration tests pass.

- [2026-04-06] **CoW materialization point 7 — field store from borrow**: Fixed UAF/double-free when assigning a borrowed parameter to a struct field (`self.pickup_text = text`). Without `!`, the callee receives a `Ptr(GorgetString)` borrow — a plain memcpy aliases the heap allocation, so when the caller drops its copy the field becomes dangling. Fix: `lower_field_assign` in `assigns.rs` now detects `Ptr`-typed sources via `pointee_type()` and emits `clone_fn_for_ptr()` to produce an independently-owned copy. Handles all resource types (String, Vector, Dict, Set, user structs). Documented as 7th CoW materialization point in `copy-on-write.md`, `ownership-ir.md`, and `language-reference.md`. 972 unit + 890 integration tests pass.
