- [2026-04-06] **Backend trait enrichment**: Added `BackendFeatures` struct and `features()` + `emit_function()` methods to Backend trait. CLirBackend implements both (hot_reload + per-function emit).
- [2026-04-06] **Extract runtime selection from generate_c_inner()**: Two runtime inclusion blocks (~626 lines) moved from c_lir/mod.rs to emit_types.rs. c_lir/mod.rs: 3,045 → 2,435 lines.
- [2026-04-06] **EmitContext struct**: Consolidated 13 parallel-array parameters on emit_inst() and emit_call_extern() into a single struct. Cleaner signatures, easier to extend.
- [2026-04-05] **LocalOwnershipState consolidation (Phase 4a)**: Replaced 6 separate tracking maps (`ref_locals`, `owned_locals`, `cow_alias_sources`, `cow_alias_targets`, `cow_ptr_params`, `cow_collection_refs`) with a unified `LocalOwnershipState` enum and single `local_ownership: FxHashMap<LocalId, LocalOwnershipState>`. Five variants: `Owned`, `Alias { source }`, `CollectionRef { collection }`, `BareParam`, `Ref`. Reverse maps (`cow_alias_targets`, collection refs) are now derived queries (O(n) scan, n small). Helper methods: `is_ref_local()`, `is_owned_local()`, `is_bare_param()`, `set_ref()`, `set_owned()`, `set_bare_param()`, `set_collection_ref()`, `derive_ref_locals()`. Updated 68 reference sites across 9 files. 988 unit + 914 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-05] **`gg build --show-clones` structured report (Phase 4c)**: Added `--show-clones` flag that emits a compact table of all CoW materialization points with file:line:col, type name, and reason. Builds on existing `ImplicitCloneWarning` infrastructure. Deduplicates by source location, separates user vs library clones (env `GORGET_SHOW_LIB_CLONE_WARNINGS=1` to include libraries). Added `span_location()` to ErrorReporter for structured span-to-location resolution.
- [2026-04-05] **Mutation trait on method declarations (Phase 4d)**: Replaced hardcoded `is_mutating_collection_method` name lists in borrow checker (`check_expr.rs:337`) and IR lowering (`methods.rs:990`) with `is_mutating_builtin_method()` query derived from the protocol table in `builtins.rs`. Single source of truth — adding `is_mutating: true` to a `BuiltinMethodDecl` automatically propagates to both borrow invalidation and field-zeroing. Also fixes missing `get_or_put` (mutating Dict method absent from old hardcoded list). 988 unit + 914 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-05] **Post-StringView dead code cleanup (−309 lines)**: Deleted `last_error_returns_cstr` + `wrap_cstr` wrapping logic (dead since StringView removal), `view_method_vals` bitmap + entire pre-pass infrastructure + `_view` suffix dispatch in C backend, 11 `_view` runtime functions in c_runtime.rs, `is_str_dst` cap/alloc zeroing blocks in lir/lower.rs (2 blocks), and `block_move_zero_targets` field + pre-scan loop. All were StringView-era mechanisms made dead by the unification.
- [2026-04-05] **StringView removal — all 27 regressions fixed (914/914 pass)**: Three root causes: (1) `last_error_returns_cstr` incorrectly marked socket/tls/udp functions as returning `const char*` — they return `GorgetString` via `gorget_string_adopt`. Before unification, `wrap_cstr` was dead code (ok_ty_c was GorgetStringView, not "Str"); after unification it incorrectly fired. Fix: `last_error_returns_cstr` returns false (24 httpserver + async_socket_echo tests). (2) `gorget_string_clone` preserves views (cap=0) which can dangle after source freed. Enum clones (`TomlValue__clone`) need owned copies. Fix: enum clone generators use `gorget_string_clone_to_owned` (toml_stringify test). (3) `stress_alloc_vectors` expected `leaked=true` but improved ownership means no leak — updated expected output.
- [2026-04-05] **Delete `directive explicit-clone`**: Removed the directive, `is_error` field on `ImplicitCloneWarning`, `explicit_clone` field on `LoweringContext`, `clone_errors` counter in `main.rs`, and `"explicit-clone"` match arms in `semantic/mod.rs` and `ir/lowering/mod.rs`. Incompatible with CoW philosophy — clones are correct by design. Replaced by future `gg build --show-clones` observability tool.
- [2026-04-05] **CoW Phase 1f liveness analysis**: Full-function span-based reverse walk with branch union and two-pass loops. `is_last_use_at(name, span)` provides precise per-use last-use queries. Integrated at push/put and struct-init call sites. Phase 2a (unified `Type__drop`) also done (`emit_type_drop_fns` exists, `Type__drop` called at scope exit).
- [2026-04-05] **Fix closure capture of CoW Ptr aliases**: Closures capturing CoW alias variables (Ptr(T)) now clone through the Ptr at capture time, producing an independent owned T in the closure struct. Previously the raw Ptr was copied, causing stale reads after source mutation (`snap.len()` returned 0 instead of 2). Fix in `closures.rs`: resolve Ptr(T)→T for ByValue capture struct fields, clone via `clone_fn_for_ptr` at StructInit emission, and resolve spawn capture types. 914 integration tests pass, zero regressions.
- [2026-04-05] **Revert unwrap auto-clone — return Ptr borrow, let CoW handle cloning**: Reverted eager clone in `Option[Ref_T].unwrap()` for resource-type payloads. The unconditional clone at unwrap time was wasteful for read-only use (`print(x.name)`). Restored original Ptr(T) + ref_local return — CoW fires at ownership boundaries (VarDecl with explicit type, struct init, function args). `auto` bindings stay as Ptr borrows (zero-cost reads). Reverted `drop_struct_collection_fields` expected output and fixture comment.
- [2026-04-05] **Struct string field access returns Ptr(GorgetString) reference**: `s.name` for GorgetString fields now returns `Ptr(GorgetString)` (borrow reference) instead of `StringView` (non-owning view with zeroed cap/alloc). Matches the existing collection field pattern (`Ptr(Vector)`, `Ptr(Dict)`). The LIR FieldLoad cap/alloc zeroing blocks become dead code for string fields — `is_str_dst` matches `GorgetStringView` but field loads now produce `Ptr(GorgetString)`. Zero-cost reads (no allocation), CoW clone-on-mutation for ownership transfer. Updated all 4 field access paths: struct_fields cache, TypeDef fallback, Guard auto-deref cache, Guard auto-deref fallback. 988 unit + 911 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-05] **Match pattern string bindings: clone-on-extract**: Pattern extraction (`case Error(msg)`, `case Text(s)`) creates shallow copies that share heap data with the scrutinee. Previously, string bindings were typed as StringView with no-op drops → leaked. Now: owned string fields from value scrutinees are cloned during extraction, producing independent copies registered for drop. Avoids the shared-pointer problem that caused 16 regressions in the "register for drop without clone" approach — the scrutinee retains its original data (safe for post-extraction use like `child_list.push(child_nd)` in xml.gg), and the clone is independently freed. 988 unit + 911 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-05] **Fix csv_basic UAF + reduce leaks — Error path clone + StringView call upgrade**: Two fixes: (1) `resolve_option_result_variant` Error path now uses `emit_enum_init_owned` — clones non-owned string views (like `p.err` field loads) before storing in Error variant. Root cause: csv library's `Error(p.err)` stored a cap=0 view; CsvParser drop freed the buffer; caller got garbage. (2) `call_tracked` now upgrades ALL StringView call results to GorgetString (owned, with proper gorget_string_free drop), except known view-returning methods (trim, substring, etc. via VIEW_METHODS list). Previously, non-equip/non-extern StringView returns got registered for drop but the drop was a Nop (no TypeDef for GorgetStringView), causing leaks. The 3 old `else if` upgrade branches (equip methods, extern fns, cstr ABI) are subsumed by the unified upgrade. Attempted: making ALL StringView drops effective (infer_drop_strategy returns gorget_string_free) — caused 22 regressions from double-frees with VarDecl-consumed intermediates. The call_tracked-only approach avoids this by only affecting function return values. 988 unit + 909 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-05] **CoW audit pass 2 — set clone double-free, move tracking, for-loop safety**: (1) `gorget_set_clone` now deep-clones resource-type keys (strings) — previously did shallow memcpy causing double-free on `Set[String]` drop. Matches `gorget_map_clone` pattern (lines 5399-5410). (2) Move expression (`!vec`) now cleans up `cow_collection_refs` — previously left stale entries after zeroing. (3) Borrow checker now rejects mutating a collection during for-loop iteration regardless of element type — previously only caught explicit `T &` refs. Added `for_loop_iterables` tracking set. New tests: `cow_set_string_clone.gg`, `for_loop_mutate_error.gg`.
- [2026-04-05] **CoW internals hardening — 2 bugs fixed, 4 tests added**: (1) `cow_sever_all_aliases_from` now cleans up `cow_ptr_params` and `cow_collection_refs` on reassignment — previously stale entries caused incorrect clones. (2) `cow_materialize_collection_ref` rewritten to create a new owned local instead of changing ref_local's type in-place — aligns with `cow_materialize_alias` and respects the LIR invariant that ref_locals/slot types are static per-function. (3) Wired existing `cow_borrow_basic.gg` into integration tests (was dead code). (4) Added 3 new edge-case fixtures: `cow_transitive_alias.gg` (3-level alias chains), `cow_collection_element_mutate.gg` (element borrow + collection mutation), `cow_param_alias_reassign.gg` (bare Ptr param aliasing). Deferred: nested field mutation gap (requires FieldLoad provenance tracking).
- [2026-04-03] **cstr demoted from keyword to contextual identifier**: `cstr` is now only valid inside `extern "C"` contexts (block or inline). Parser rejects it elsewhere. Keyword::CStr removed from lexer — `cstr` lexes as a regular identifier, recognized contextually in type parser when `in_extern_c` is true. All .gg library files use `String` in APIs; `cstr` only appears inside `extern "C":` blocks. Test fixtures updated. Inline `extern "C"` syntax added: `extern "C" int foo(String s) = "symbol"` — single-function equivalent of `extern "C":` blocks, with ABI tag stored in FunctionDef.extern_abi.
- [2026-04-03] **Extern ABI whitelist reduction (-100+ entries)**: Deleted `runtime_fn_str_param` (10 GL/Metal entries, dead after C runtime fix). Removed 30+ redundant entries from `takes_cstr_for_str_param` (covered by fn_extern_abi_kinds from .gg files). Added ABI derivation for equip/standalone extern methods with inline `extern "C"` tag. 5 C runtime functions changed to return Str via gorget_string_adopt (bytes_to_str, bytes_to_hex, regex_match_text, regex_pattern_str, sdl_get_error). All extern function results now owned by definition (`extern_body_fns` tracking). Fixed cstr-return string leaks (~10 leaks eliminated). Whitelist: ~50→~15 entries.
- [2026-04-01] **Extern module migration COMPLETE — all 27 stdlib modules now .gg files**: Thread (generic Thread[T] with Declaration equip) and sync (10 types including generic RWLock[T]/ReadGuard[T]/WriteGuard[T]) migrated. Deleted all gen_*_module() functions, all AST-building helpers (decl_fn, ty_int, opaque_struct, equip_block, etc.), simplified generate_builtin_module to always return None. stdlib.rs: ~4983→1425 lines (-71%). New .gg files: lib/std/thread.gg, lib/std/sync.gg. 988 unit + 905 integration tests pass.
- [2026-04-01] **`is_resource_type = needs_drop` — COMPLETE (905/905)**: GorgetStringView is now a resource type with uniform CoW ownership. PtrTo(StructId) typed pointers preserve pointee info through LIR. Fixed: takes_cstr_for_str_param coverage (regex, crypto, file_write, input, exec, panic), MoveZero for PtrTo slots (zero pointer not pointee), sentinel/nullable-cstr call paths, general Ptr(Str)→Str by-value deref for Str-param functions. Restored accidentally deleted modules_pkg fixture files.
- [2026-04-01] **Refactor: consolidate IR/LIR/codegen boilerplate (-196 lines)**: get_or_register_type() helper (12 sites), emit_i64_const/emit_i32_const (29 sites), map_type() wrapper (17 sites), is_str_struct_id() helper (15 inline checks replaced).
- [2026-03-30] **Remove unregister_gorget_string_args — 99 lines deleted**: The workaround is no longer needed. owned_locals tracking now covers all GorgetString locals that matter. Only 1 unregister fired in yaml_parse. Disabling entirely: zero test failures, zero new leaks. Also fixed `!` string params to use Move (memcpy) instead of Copy (clone) — zero-cost ownership transfer. Parser 761/858 (3 unfixable mismatches). Canonical formatter ..= fix.
- [2026-03-30] **Fix Box(val) for Ptr-wrapped resource types — 31 self-host parser fixes**: Root cause: Box(val) where val is a bare-borrowed resource param (const void*) was boxing the 8-byte POINTER instead of the struct. type_name_for_id(Ptr(T)) returned None → fallback to int64_t. Fix: unwrap Ptr(T) → T + LoadRef before boxing. Parser comparison: 729→760 matched, 35→4 mismatches. Also removed dead code (register_owned_string_for_drop, gorget_str_owned_region, consumed_local_id, mark_consumed_local_by_id).
- [2026-03-30] **IR audit: consolidate ownership tracking, remove string_param_locals**: Deep audit of IR lowering. (1) Marked constructor results (struct_init, enum_init, Some/None/Ok/Error) and gorget_string_format results as owned_locals — single source of truth for ownership. (2) Replaced string_param_locals tracking set with type-based StringView check in MoveZero paths — 15 lines removed. (3) Attempted removing unregister_gorget_string_args from struct_init — reverted (ensure_owned_string clones without MoveZero pairing cause double-ownership). (4) Previous audit batch: local_type() bounds check (47 sites), move_zero_and_mark helper (22 sites), is_string_type consolidation (8 sites), .expect() messages (17 sites), else-arm simplification (14 unwrap sites removed), is_collection_type Deque fix. Net: ~100 lines removed, ~130 sites improved.
- [2026-03-30] **Constructor auto-clone + `!` parity — yaml_parse 374→50 leaks**: Structs/enums auto-clone StringView locals to GorgetString at constructor boundaries (ensure_owned_string). StringView call_tracked results excluded from owned_locals (byte_slice/char_at return views). unregister_gorget_string_args skips owned_locals. `!` string params marked as owned — no double-clone regression. MutPtr deref results propagate owned status. 981 unit + 905 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-30] **Fix return-path string leak — `owned_locals` tracking**: Named locals from function calls leaked on return: C backend cloned (Copy mode) then MoveZero zeroed without freeing. Fixed by tracking `owned_locals` (call results that own their data) and using Move (memcpy) instead of Copy (clone) on return for those locals. Ownership propagated through VarDecl and reassignment chains. Reduced yaml_parse leaks 122→101, toml_parse 232→219. Also wired up `cow_collection_refs` for IndexLoad, preventing dangling pointers from collection mutation after element borrow. 981 unit + 905 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-29] **Fix all 6 remaining library test failures (899→905/905)**: Three bugs fixed: (1) **String clone-on-read from collections**: C backend's Option-wrapping code for `gorget_array_safe_get`/`gorget_map_get` checked `"GorgetString"` for clone but the payload type was `"Str"` (from StringView mapping) → shallow copy → use-after-free. Fixed by adding `"Str"` to clone match in both Option-wrapping and non-Option deref paths. (2) **Move-argument MoveZero for non-Identifier expressions**: `!expr.clone()` (Move of a method call result) got `mark_moved` (for DropIfAlive guard at scope exit) but NO MoveZero was emitted → the DropIfAlive guard found non-zero data → fired the drop → double-free of data already moved into callee. Fixed by adding `pending_move_zeros` on LoweringContext, populated in `lower_call_arg` Move path, drained after Call instruction with baseline tracking to prevent cross-call leakage. (3) **needs_drop("GorgetStringView") = true**: One-line fix making StringView locals droppable, closing the 72-leak provenance class. All 981 unit + 905 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-28] **Fix provenance-driven string leak class**: Root cause: Provenance Pass 4.5 downgrades user-defined function return types from GorgetString (owned, needs-drop) to GorgetStringView (view, no-drop) when all return paths appear to be views. But IR lowering always materializes owned strings on return (CoW clone/clone_to_owned). Callers didn't register return values for drop → leak. Fix: (1) `call_tracked` upgrades StringView results from GIR equip methods to GorgetString + register for drop. (2) VarDecl `GorgetString→StringView` borrow path restricted to named locals (not temps). (3) String fold return type uses owned_string_type. (4) C backend fold codegen wraps string literal accumulators with `gorget_str_from_literal`. 981 unit + 894 integration tests pass (11 pre-existing httpserver/signal failures).
- [2026-03-26] **BuiltinTypeProtocol: delete infer_collection_method_return_type + 11 special-case blocks + EnumCategory**: (1) Deleted `infer_collection_method_return_type` (262 lines, ~60 `starts_with` sites) — replaced by lazy substitution from protocol table via `resolve_builtin_method_return_type`. Added GorgetStringView, Option, Result protocols with full return type closures including resource-aware Option wrapping. (2) Deleted 7 pure-dispatch blocks (AtomicInt, AtomicBool, Barrier, WaitGroup, Semaphore, OnceFlag, TaskGroup — 174 lines) via `is_by_value_receiver` protocol query. (3) Deleted 4 MutBorrow blocks (Channel, Guard, ReadGuard, WriteGuard — 144 lines) via `is_mut_borrow_method` protocol query in generic dispatch path. (4) Added `EnumCategory` metadata (Option/Result) to TypeMetadata — migrated ~20 `starts_with("Option__")`/`starts_with("Result__")` dispatch sites to metadata-first checks. Total: 580 lines of name-dispatch code deleted, 26 type families in protocol table (~190 method declarations).
- [2026-03-26] **Deep drops in enum/struct drop functions**: `Yaml__drop`/`Json__drop`/`TomlValue__drop` now recursively drop collection variant fields (Seq/Arr elements, Map/Obj values) before freeing. `YamlParser__drop`/`JsonDeserializer__drop`/etc. struct drops also deep-drop collection fields. yaml_parse leaks: 374→256 allocations (-32%). No use-after-free (ASan verified on yaml_parse, toml_parse, xml_parse, query_basic, serialize_collections). Zero test regressions.
- [2026-03-26] **Borrow-aware drop ordering + enum auto-upgrade + per-element collection drops**: (1) Enum auto-upgrade: if any variant has droppable fields, enum gets CopySemantics::Resource + DropStrategy::Recursive (Rust-inspired rule). (2) Borrow-aware drop ordering: topological sort (Kahn's algorithm) using borrow deps exported from borrow checker, LIFO tiebreaker. (3) `Yaml__drop`/`Json__drop`/`TomlValue__drop` generated for Recursive enums with variant-dispatch. (4) LIR scope-exit drops emit per-element drop loops for collections with Recursive enum elements (via `compute_drop_actions` recipe system). (5) Fixed `elem_type` extraction from monomorphized names (strip `__new` suffix). (6) Fixed `compute_drop_actions` for Custom drops (don't duplicate field drops). (7) Library fixes: yaml.gg, toml.gg, jsonpath.gg, json.gg, all 4 self-host fixtures — `!` move sigils on resource params consumed by push/put/set. yaml_parse leaks: 386→374 allocations. 981 unit + 904 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-25] **Declarative builtin type protocol table (Phases 1-6)**: Created `src/ir/lowering/builtins.rs` — single source of truth for all builtin type methods (~150 methods across 16 type families). TypeMetadata extended with `collection_kind` and `clone_fn` fields. Type classification functions (`is_collection_type`, `is_resource_name`, `clone_fn_for_ptr`) now check metadata first. For-loop dispatch uses `CollectionKind` enum. LIR backend uses `runtime_callees` table for monomorphized→runtime function mapping. On-the-fly `resolve_builtin_method_return_type` handles late-registered types. All dispatch sites retain name-based fallbacks during migration. Inspired by Rust's `TypeckResults` pattern.
- [2026-03-25] **Fix implicit clone warnings in xtd/p2p.gg — 35 down to 5**: Systematic cleanup of implicit clone warnings in the P2P networking library. Fixes: (1) Changed ~25 `Type x = collection.get(i).unwrap()` to `auto x = ...` for read-only access (zero-cost borrow). (2) Changed `String peer = s.peer_hex` field reads to `auto peer = ...` with `.clone()` at use sites. (3) Added explicit `.clone()` for struct field accesses passed to functions (e.g., `s.local_ephemeral_pub.clone()`, `node.identity.public_key.clone()`). (4) Extracted `seg.data.clone()` into locals before struct constructors. (5) Changed `Result[...]` declarations to `auto` where possible. Added `GORGET_SHOW_LIB_CLONE_WARNINGS=1` env var to `main.rs` for debugging library warnings. Remaining 5 warnings are compiler false positives (spans on comments/struct defs, already-explicit clones).
- [2026-03-25] **`.clone()` method works on all types**: Explicit `.clone()` calls now route to the correct clone function for every type: collections → `gorget_array_clone`/`gorget_map_clone`/etc., user structs/enums with resource fields → generated `{Name}__clone`, copy types → trivial value copy. Added early interception in `methods.rs` before general method dispatch. Uses `clone_fn_for_ptr()` to resolve the clone function. ASan verified: deep clone produces independent heap copies.
- [2026-03-25] **`directive explicit-clone` — opt-in strict ownership mode**: Auto-clone becomes a compile error instead of a warning. Libraries and performance-critical code can opt in to require `.clone()` or `!` at every ownership transfer.
- [2026-03-25] **ImplicitClone warnings at all 6 auto-clone sites**: Compiler now emits warnings when it auto-clones resource types. Covers: VarDecl from borrow (`Vector[int] v = matrix[0]`), named-to-named assignment (`v1 = v2`), return from borrow (`return m[0]`), move parameter from borrow (`f(!ref)`), struct field from borrow (`S{field: ref}`). Warnings include user-friendly type names (`Vector[int]` not `Vector__int64_t`) via `demangle_type_name()`. Added `ImplicitCloneWarning`/`ImplicitCloneReason` to GIR Module, `warn_implicit_clone()` on LoweringContext, `report_implicit_clone_warning()` on ErrorReporter. Phase 1 of explicit clone roadmap complete.
- [2026-03-25] **Unified `needs_drop_for_temp` with `needs_drop` — Recursive types now tracked for call result drop**: Removed the separate `needs_drop_for_temp()` that excluded Recursive-drop types. `call_tracked()`/`call_extern_tracked()` now register ALL droppable types (including Recursive structs/enums like Column, DataFrame, TypedColumn) for scope-exit drop. Three fixes needed: (1) Missing `move_zero_resource_args` on Call-based EnumInit paths in `calls.rs` (meta-expanded variant constructors like `StrCol(call_result)` went through Call, not DotShorthand). (2) `DataFrame.drop(col_name)` user method collides with generated `DataFrame__drop` destructor — added `drop_collision_types` set to LirModule, populated during `populate_recursive_drop_structs`, used by `lower_field_drops` to inline sub-field drops instead of calling the colliding function. (3) Moved `populate_recursive_drop_structs()` before function lowering so collision info is available during LIR generation. 981 unit + 904 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-25] **Ptr pattern extraction for borrowed enum scrutinees**: Pattern match extraction from Ptr(enum) scrutinees now produces Ptr(T) for collection payloads and StringView for string payloads, matching struct field load behavior. Prevents shallow-copy ownership issues when extracting from borrowed enums. Applied in `patterns.rs` (GIR-level type conversion) and `lower.rs` (LIR EnumFieldLoad: Ptr destinations get field address, StringView destinations get cap-zero). Enables the `needs_drop` unification above.
- [2026-03-24] **ALL 904 TESTS GREEN — struct string ownership complete**: FieldLoad of GorgetString into Str destination zeros cap+alloc to create non-owning view. MoveZero on all 7 EnumInit paths (mod.rs×4, calls.rs×2, methods.rs×1) prevents shallow-copy double-free. Return MoveZero generalized for all droppable types. VarDecl Move for unregistered droppable temps. Provenance classifies local field access returns as Owned (auto-clone). StructInit auto-clones Ptr(collection) operands. P2P library 5 use-after-move fixes. Httpserver library `!resp` move semantics. LIR type inference block-param override.
- [2026-03-24] **Struct fields own GorgetString instead of Str views**: Removed String→Str mapping in struct TypeDefs. Structs now OWN their string fields via GorgetString, enabling recursive drop to free them. Field loads return Str views at load time (not type level). The previous design leaked strings by storing non-owning Str views that pointed to external temps kept alive by `should_unregister_string_args`. The "method chain temp lifetime" blocker turned out to be a test fixture bug (double unwrap = use-after-move, correctly caught by GIR validator). 981 unit + 904 integration tests pass, no ASan crashes.
- [2026-03-24] **Eliminate unnecessary string clones in C backend**: Two optimizations: (1) struct-by-value GorgetString stores use memcpy instead of clone+free (the clone+free round-trip was equivalent to memcpy since the source is a C temporary); (2) propagate GIR AssignMode::Move to LIR SlotStore via `is_move` flag so pointer-to-string stores use memcpy instead of clone when the source is being moved. Combined: 66% fewer gorget_string_clone calls (290→100 in yaml_parse). No ASan issues.
- [2026-03-24] **Universal drop registration for call results via call_tracked/call_extern_tracked**: Added `call_tracked()` and `call_extern_tracked()` wrappers on LoweringContext that auto-register call results for drop. Uses narrow `needs_drop_for_temp()` check (Trivial/Custom drop + collections) to avoid registering user structs with Recursive drop that would be double-freed. Replaces 5 explicit `register_local`/`register_owned_string_for_drop` sites in calls.rs, methods.rs, operators.rs, and assigns.rs. 981 unit + 904 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-23] **Collection field loads return Ptr(T) references**: Struct field access on collection-type fields (Vector, Dict, Set) now returns `Ptr(T)` (a reference into the struct) instead of a raw `T` (shallow copy). Prevents double-free from shared heap buffers. LIR FieldLoad stores field address as pointer for Ptr destinations; IndexLoad correctly handles Ptr-typed bases without double-deref (ref_locals already get SlotLoad). `.len()` optimization resolves through Ptr for field-load refs. Strings excluded (GorgetString header copy is safe as a Str view). Also fixed hot-reload `current_exe()` returning `(deleted)` path via `canonicalize()`. 981 unit + 904 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-23] **Fix GorgetString temp leak in C backend SlotStore clone**: C backend cloned GorgetString on every by-value SlotStore but never freed the original transient value. Each gorget_str_cat/format/int_to_display produced a heap temp that leaked after being cloned. Now frees originals immediately after cloning (+224 string frees in gorget-arena, 211→435). 981 unit + 903 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-23] **Fix collection temp leaks — register call results for drop + field assignment drop-before-reassign**: Three fixes: (1) `lower_drop` now resolves GIR type through projection chain (`resolve_gir_place_type`) so field drops like `bot.nav.path = Vector[int]()` free the old array before overwrite (+8 new frees in gorget-arena); (2) collection-returning function/method calls now register their temps for drop at scope exit (+61 new frees in gorget-arena); (3) `try_resolve_field_place` fast path now move-zeroes registered temps after field assignment (prevents double-free). Previously blocked by 8 p2p test failures — resolved by also move-zeroing anonymous collection temps after they're passed as function arguments. 981 unit + 904 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Fix closure f-string capture** — Variables used inside f-string interpolation in closure bodies weren't detected as free variables, so they weren't captured in the closure environment. Root cause: `collect_free_vars` in closures.rs didn't visit `Expr::StringLiteral` segments — interpolated identifiers like `{prefix}` in `f"{prefix} {name}"` fell into the `_ => {}` catch-all. Fix: added `Expr::StringLiteral` handler that extracts leading identifiers from `StringSegment::Interpolation` text and registers them as free variable references. 981 unit + 903 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Fix match guard bindings invisible in arm body** — `case Some(v) if v > 10:` would match correctly but `v` was 0 in the body. Root cause: `emit_pattern_bindings` ran in the guard basic block, creating SSA values there. The body ran in a different basic block where those values weren't defined. Fix: re-emit pattern bindings at the start of the body block. 981 unit + 901 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Fix Set.insert() missing in LIR backend** — `Set.insert()` mapped to `gorget_set_insert` which doesn't exist in the C runtime (the runtime function is `gorget_set_add`). Added `"insert" => "gorget_set_add"` mapping in `map_monomorphized_to_runtime`. Updated `collection_empty_edge.gg` to include Set operations, added `set_insert_contains.gg` fixture. 981 unit + 901 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Fix trait methods with `throws` segfault** — Trait method signatures with `throws` now correctly wrap the return type in `Result[T, E]`. Previously, vtable registration, equip method lowering, and trait method body lowering all used the bare return type (e.g., `int`) instead of `Result[int, String]`, causing the caller to interpret the return value as a Result struct while the callee returned a raw int — segfault via type confusion. Fixed in three places: vtable VTableMethod registration (traits.rs), lower_equip_method (functions.rs), and lower_trait_method_body (traits.rs). Also set `current_throws_result_type` so return/throw statements properly wrap values. 981 unit + 900 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Fix IndexLoad clone-on-read for collection-type elements** — `Vector[Vector[int]]` indexing (`matrix[0]`) now clones the inner vector instead of moving+zeroing it. Previously, reading a resource-type element from a collection via IndexLoad would zero the source slot (move semantics), destroying the original. Now collection-type elements (Vector, Dict, Set, Str) are deep-cloned via `gorget_*_clone()` runtime functions, preserving the original. Non-collection resource types (Task, user structs) retain move+zero semantics for intentional consumption patterns (e.g., `tasks[j].await()`). 981 unit + 897 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Fix nested pattern matching codegen** — `lower_pattern_condition` now recursively checks inner enum discriminants for nested constructor patterns (e.g., `Some(Shape.Circle(r))`). Previously only the outer tag was checked, so `Inner.B(99)` would incorrectly match `Inner.A(n)` and extract `n=0`. Fix uses short-circuit branching: inner field extraction only runs when outer tag matches (avoids reading garbage from wrong union variant). Also fixed ambiguous `resolve_enum_variant` for bare prelude variants (`Some`/`None`/`Ok`/`Error`) when multiple monomorphizations exist — now prefers `type_name(scrut_type)` to select the correct monomorphization. 981 unit + 897 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Fix library/fixture files for bare-param-in-struct restriction** — Updated .gg files to comply with the new borrow checker rule rejecting storage of borrowed resource params. Library files: `lib/xtd/toml.gg` (`start_section !hdr_path`), `lib/xtd/yaml.gg` (`merge_into` refactored from `!` return-pattern to `&` mutable borrow), `lib/xtd/dataframe.gg` (`df_group_by`/`df_group_by_multi`/`df_cast` — copy before struct storage), `lib/xtd/log.gg` (3 constructors `!log_level`), `lib/xtd/db.gg` (`FromRow::from_row !row`), `lib/xtd/math3d.gg` (`Ray.new !origin`). Fixture files: `httpserver_middleware.gg`, `httpserver_router.gg`, `httpserver_static_enhanced.gg`. Self-host files (3 copies each): `parser.gg` (`!lhs`, `!derives` in 3 functions), `scope.gg` (`!span` in define/alloc_def with pre-computed dummy flag). 980 unit tests + 892/893 integration tests pass (1 flaky hot_reload).
- [2026-03-22] **Borrow checker: reject storing borrowed resource params** — Added compile-time restriction: borrowed parameters (bare and `&`) of resource types cannot be stored in struct constructors, enum variant constructors, or return statements. Reuses existing `check_non_owned_param_move()` in `check_expr.rs` (StructLiteral + constructor Call sites) and `check_stmt.rs` (Return + expression-body). Extended `is_copy_type()` to recognize user-defined enums as Copy (eliminates false positives for ordinal enums like LogLevel). Fixed `lib/xtd/toml.gg` (`start_section` → `!hdr_path`) and `lib/xtd/yaml.gg` (`merge_into` → `!yaml_target`). Added 6 unit tests. Documented the rule in `docs/language-design.md` section 3.2. IR unregister-on-pass workaround retained for uncovered paths (method calls on struct fields). 980 unit + 893 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-22] **Phase 6: Collection scope-exit drops via ownership tracking** — Enabled `needs_drop()` for collection types (Vector, Dict, Set, Deque, HashMap, HashSet) using name-based detection in `src/ir/types.rs` — no TypeDef registration, avoiding transitive struct field upgrade. Added `is_collection_type_name()` helper. VarDecl move-zero (stmts/mod.rs): zero source temp when assigned to collection variable. Return move-zero (stmts/mod.rs): zero source local on collection return. Assignment move-zero (assigns.rs): zero source temp on collection reassignment. `gorget_map_free` null-alloc guard (c_runtime.rs): early return for zeroed/freed structs. `lower_field_drops` fix (lower.rs): use `infer_drop_strategy()` instead of `get_type_def()` to detect collection fields for Custom/Recursive drop structs. Unregister collection args passed by value to functions (calls.rs, methods.rs): prevents shared-data-pointer use-after-free when callee stores shallow copy. Drop-before-reassign excluded for collections: RHS may modify target in-place through pointer (e.g., `dict = merge_into(dict, x)`). 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass, 0 double-frees, 0 use-after-free (verified with ASan).
- [2026-03-21] **Phase 6: Ptr-for-all-types in collection element reads** — Changed collection element reads (`get/first/last → unwrap`) to return Ptr references for ALL element types, not just direct collections. Previously, user structs with resource fields (e.g., Container with Vector field) were deref'd to shallow copies → double-free when both copy and collection were dropped. Now ALL unwrapped Ptr values stay as Ptr, matching Rust's `&T` semantics. Fixed `lower_place_addr` to handle Deref vs non-Deref projections correctly for collection_ref_locals (Deref-first → SlotAddr, otherwise → SlotLoad). Removed `is_collection` check (~10 lines). Updated `drop_struct_collection_fields` expected output (no more duplicate drops from shallow copies). Scope-exit drops for collection locals deferred — shallow copy semantics in function returns/assignments still prevent safe drops. 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Phase 6 investigation: collection scope-exit drops** — Attempted registering collection TypeDefs with `CopySemantics::Resource` + `DropStrategy::Trivial` for Vector/Dict/Set. Found that enabling drops causes 42+ double-free crashes because collection element reads create shallow copies sharing heap buffers. Added infrastructure for future enablement: `deep_clone_resource_fields()` in c_lir (handles struct payloads with resource fields), `is_collection_type()` on TypeRegistry (distinguishes direct collections from user structs), `infer_drop_strategy()` fallback in LIR lower_drop. Dict Ref (Ptr payloads for Dict.get()) also attempted and reverted — breaks write-back pattern. Closed "variable shadowing aliases storage" TODO (already working, verified by `shadowing_nested.gg`). 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Fix nested field writes through mutable borrows** — `gs.current_weapon.ammo = x` was silently writing to a discarded local copy. Root cause: `lower_field_assign` called `lower_expr` on intermediate objects, which materialized nested structs via FieldLoad (value copy). Fix: call `try_resolve_field_place` first, which recursively builds the full Place projection chain (e.g. `[Deref, Field(5), Field(2)]`) without intermediate copies. The LIR then emits chained FieldPtr instructions computing the address through the base pointer. File: `src/ir/lowering/stmts/assigns.rs`. 974 unit + 702/703 LIR A/B tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Resolver 100%, type checker 99.2%** — Resolver: removed overly-restrictive trait replacement guard in scope.gg (797/797). Type checker: added RTRef type variant for `.get()` borrow tracking (fixes 4), Ordinal enum derive handler (fixes 1). Type checker: 786→791.
- [2026-03-21] **Self-host parser/formatter/typechecker batch fixes** — Expression-body `=` formatting, bitwise NOT parsing, list comprehension `if` filters, `else if` → elif, f-string format specifier preservation, `str`→`String` normalization in type checker output. Parser: 779→794, Resolver: 794→795, Type checker: 775→786.
- [2026-03-21] **Self-host comment-skip fix + str→String normalization** — Added `self.skip_comments()` after colon in `parse_function_def` across all 3 self-host parsers. Comment tokens before NEWLINE caused block-body functions to be misparsed as expression-body (output `0`), cascading to resolver (Variables→Static) and type checker mismatches. Also normalized `str`→`String` in `describe_type_canonical` for type comparison. Parser: 553→779, Resolver: 565→794, Type checker: 538→775.
- [2026-03-21] **Fix self-host resolver crash: Dict for-loop scalar key SSA bug** — 746/797 resolver fixtures crashed (SIGSEGV) because `for (key, value) in dict:` with scalar keys passed raw integers to `gorget_array_push` (expects `void*`). Root cause: InlineC dst slot was SSA-promoted, removing the `SlotStore` that the C backend's type inference relies on. Fix: mark destination slot as address-taken via `SlotAddr` in `src/lir/lower.rs`. Also confirmed parser (0 crashes) and type checker (0 crashes) were already fixed by prior sizeof union layout fix. Resolver: 35→565 matched. All 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Conditional C runtime inclusion — hello world 4,167→841 lines, 164KB→30KB** — Split monolithic `RUNTIME_PREAMBLE` (2,480 lines) and `RUNTIME_CORE` (1,620 lines) into ~25 conditionally-included sections using the existing `has()` detection pattern. Optional allocators (Arena, Tracking, Pool, TLSF, FixedBuffer, Fallback) only included when used. Core sections (Array, Map, Set, File, Path, Parse, ToStr, Math, IO, Sort, Error) only included when the program references their functions. Extended string methods (Unicode tables, search, split/replace/trim) split from always-included string core. Alloc-report atexit handler moved from always-on to test/bench-only. Struct typedefs (GorgetArray, GorgetMap, GorgetSet, GorgetClosure) and hash utilities (FNV-1a) moved to always-included preamble for forward-reference safety. `gorget_init_args` moved to preamble; `gorget_args()` conditional on usage. Dependencies tracked via `ensure_array!`/`ensure_map!` macros. Special codegen paths (sleep→gorget_sleep_ms, int__parse→gorget_try_parse_int) added to detection. Files: `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/backend/mod.rs`. 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Fix two correctness bugs: closure return types & variable shadowing** — (1) `infer_closure_return_type` in `closures.rs` now walks block bodies for `return` statements and tail expressions instead of returning `UNIT_TYPE` for all `Expr::Block` bodies. Added `find_return_type_in_block` recursive helper. Note: semantic type checker still needs a matching fix for closures with explicit `return` (added to TODO). (2) Variable shadowing in nested scopes no longer aliases storage. Added `save_locals()` to `LoweringContext` and `lower_block_scoped()` helper. Applied save/restore at all inner-scope call sites: if/elif/else, while, loop, named_scope, with, select arms, match arms (including guards and else), and all 7 for-loop variants (string, array, enumerate, dict, set, iterable, range). Fixed `shadowing_nested` expected output from `1\n2\n2` to `1\n2\n1`. Updated `lower_match_binding` unit test to expect scoped (not leaked) bindings. 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **LIR: enum struct sizeof uses union layout** — `c_sizeof_struct_def` now computes union-aware sizes for enum structs (`is_enum == true`): `align8(tag) + max(variant_size)`. Also fixed struct alignment to use max field alignment instead of always 8. Eliminates 4 memset overflow warnings in gorget-arena.
- [2026-03-21] **LIR: const-discard warnings fixed** — `ParamRef` codegen now emits `(void*)` cast when source parameter is `const void*`, suppressing 161 `-Wdiscarded-qualifiers` warnings. Gorget-arena now builds with zero C warnings.
- [2026-03-21] **Gorget Arena: deformVertexes CPU vertex modification** — Wired existing DeformType parsing/eval into the render loop. `identify_deform_faces()` scans faces at load time, `apply_deform_vertices()` runs per-frame to modify VBO via `glBufferSubData`. Supports DeformWave, DeformBulge, DeformMove (22 deforming faces on oa_dm1). AutoSprite deferred.
- [2026-03-21] **Fix three correctness bugs: narrow int parse range check, signed int sort, vector comprehensions** — (1) `uint8.parse("256")` now returns `None` instead of silently wrapping to `Some(0)`. Added range validation for int8/int16/int32/uint8/uint16/uint32 parse in `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`. Also fixed `uint8_t__parse` being mapped to nonexistent `gorget_uint8_parse` by excluding `parse`/`default` from the uint8 runtime name mapping in `src/lir/lower.rs`. (2) `Vector[int].sort()` now correctly sorts negative numbers. Added `gorget_int_compare` (signed comparison) to the C runtime helpers and wired it into `compare_fn_for_elem` for `int64_t` elements. (3) List comprehensions over vector variables (`[x * 2 for x in vec]`) now produce correct results instead of empty arrays. Replaced the placeholder in `src/ir/lowering/exprs/collections.rs` with a full index-based iteration loop matching the `lower_for_array` pattern. 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Removed deprecated `=` expression-body syntax** — Removed the `else if self.match_token(&Token::Eq)` branch in `src/parser/mod.rs` that accepted `int f(int x) = x * 2`. Only `:` syntax remains: `int f(int x): x * 2`. Cleaned up `DeprecatedExpressionBodyEquals` warning variant from `src/errors.rs`. Updated 18 unit test strings in `src/semantic/safety/tests.rs`, 1 in `resolve.rs`, 1 in `typecheck.rs` that used the old syntax. 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Stale TODO cleanup: bench_basic + generic_nested_collections** — Verified both tests pass (844/844). Bench lowering was fixed by earlier commit adding `__bench_*` to DCE root set. Nested collections double-free was fixed by IndexLoad slot zeroing in `src/lir/lower.rs:1653-1686`. Removed stale comment in `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs` about bench lowering not being implemented. Updated self-host parser trait crash TODO with current crash list (9 fixtures). Updated comparison scores.
- [2026-03-21] **Return ownership contract enforcement — implemented then reverted** — Implemented callee-side validation for `T !` / `T &` return sigils, `Owned()` wrapping for allocating string methods, `BareNonCopyReturn` warning, and return sigil documentation. Reverted: the `&` sigil's semantics in return position were unclear (mutable reference? read-only view? into what?), and the compiler already infers all ownership information via Pass 5a (`return_borrows_from`) and origin tracking. The sigils added cognitive overhead without enabling new safety checks. The `Type::Ref`/`Type::Owned` AST/type infrastructure remains (pre-existing from 2026-03-20) but is not enforced or documented as recommended practice.
- [2026-03-21] **Self-host migration: `str` → `String` type annotations** — Replaced ~770 `str` type annotations with `String` across all 34 self-host .gg files (lexer/parser/resolver/typechecker phases). Updated ~15 codegen strings in derive.gg (`"Vector[str] "` → `"Vector[String] "`, `"str "` → `"String "`, etc.). Added `or == "String"` dual checks to 6 string comparison sites (resolve.gg ×2, derive.gg ×3, meta.gg ×1). Preserved `"str"` in keyword mappings (lexer.gg), parser returns (parser.gg), and type-table registrations (types.gg). Restored broken symlink (parser/lexer.gg). 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **GorgetString temp leak: extend void-callee heuristic to unknown/extern functions** — Changed `should_unregister_string_args` fallback for unknown callees (not in `fn_param_abis`) from conservative `true` (leak) to `false` (keep in drop tracking). Sound because: (1) void return already checked (no view escapes via result), (2) C runtime functions take Str by value (no ByMutPtr in Gorget sense), (3) Gorget-defined functions with ByMutPtr params are always in `fn_param_abis`. Eliminates leaks for all void-returning runtime/extern calls. File: `src/ir/lowering/exprs/mod.rs`. 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **GIR copy propagation re-enabled + string temp leak reduction** — Two optimizations. (1) **Copy propagation fix**: `propagate_copies` in `src/ir/transforms/optimize.rs` was disabled due to 143/844 integration test failures. Root cause: `Drop`, `DropIfAlive`, and `MoveZero` instructions destroy a local's value but weren't invalidating copy entries — stale copies were substituted after the source was zeroed/freed (use-after-free). Fix: added explicit match arm to invalidate copies whose source is destroyed. The existing type-equality guard (`dst_type == src_type`) was already correct; the original "TypeId aliasing" diagnosis was wrong. Added 4 unit tests. (2) **Targeted string temp leak reduction**: `unregister_gorget_string_args` unconditionally leaked GorgetString temps passed as call args. Added `should_unregister_string_args` heuristic: for void-returning calls with no `ByMutPtr` params (checked via `fn_param_abis`), the str view cannot escape the callee's stack frame, so temps stay in drop tracking and are freed at block scope exit. Recovers `print(f"...")`, `assert(f"...")`, and similar patterns. Unknown/extern callees conservatively unregister (leak). Files: `src/ir/transforms/optimize.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/mod.rs`, `calls.rs`, `methods.rs`. 974 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **String provenance fixes: extern heuristic, VIEW_METHODS, match arms** — Three correctness fixes to Pass 4.5 provenance inference. (1) **Extern return type heuristic**: Removed the bare-borrow-param clause that incorrectly downgraded bodyless function returns to view — stdlib functions like `regex_escape`, `path_join`, `replace_all` take borrowed params but return owned strings. Only VIEW_METHODS list now drives extern downgrades. Updated 3 borrow-checker tests to expect conservative-owned behavior for bodyless functions. (2) **VIEW_METHODS cleanup**: Removed `"str"` (GorgetString builder method, not a view-returning method), added `"slice"` (has `gorget_str_slice_view` backend variant). Kept `"as_str"` (semantically correct view method). (3) **Match arm provenance**: `check_assignments` now iterates match arm bodies (via `Expr::Block` unwrap) for reassignment tracking; `recurse_into_stmt` now calls `infer_block` on match arm bodies for nested VarDecl provenance. Files: `src/semantic/provenance.rs`, `src/semantic/safety/tests.rs`. 970 unit + 843 integration tests pass (1 flaky).
- [2026-03-21] **String provenance: tuple destructuring view downgrades** — Fixed two bugs where string bindings in tuple destructuring patterns missed provenance downgrades. (1) `collect_string_decls` only handled `Pattern::Binding` — added recursive `collect_string_pattern` for Tuple/Constructor patterns, and extended to handle `Type::Inferred`/`Type::Tuple` AST types. (2) Tuple VarDecl lowering didn't call `provenance_adjusted_string_type` for field bindings — added the adjustment call. Fixes unnecessary GorgetString allocations for `auto host, port = parse_url(...)` patterns. 970 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Union-based enum layout** — Large enums (>4 fields) use C union layout instead of flat sequential fields. Stmt: 39KB → ~1.3KB. Fixes self-host resolver/typechecker stack overflow. Small enums (Option, Result) keep flat layout for C backend compatibility. 970 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-21] **Phase 3b: Ptr payloads for collection borrowing reads** — Collection borrowing methods (`get`/`first`/`last`) on resource-type elements now return `Option__Ref_T` with `Ptr(elem_type)` payload. The collection retains ownership; caller gets a reference (no clone, no drop, no double-free, no leak). GIR unwrap detects `Ref_` prefix and emits `Ptr(pointee)`. For primitives/strings: immediate Deref. For collections: Ptr propagates through var decls, auto-reinference, and function return locals. LIR `lower_place_addr` uses `SlotLoad` for `collection_ref_locals`. C backend `__option_unwrap` handler detects Ptr payload fields. `collection_ref_locals` set on GIR Function, populated at GIR lowering, consumed at LIR lowering. 970 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-20] **T &/T ! return type sigils — Phases 1-5** — Implemented suffix ownership syntax for return types and generic args. (1) **AST+Parser**: `Type::Ref(Box<Spanned<Type>>)` and `Type::Owned(Box<Spanned<Type>>)` variants; `parse_type_with_ownership()` for suffix `&`/`!`; used in generic args and function return types. (2) **Semantic**: `ResolvedType::Ref(TypeId)`/`Owned(TypeId)`; `Ref` is Copy, `Owned` is not; `Ref` is a reference type for lifetime tracking. (3) **IR**: `Type::Ref` → `GirType::Ptr(inner)`, `Type::Owned` → unwrap to inner. (4) **Type checker**: Auto-deref coercion `Ref(T)↔T` and `Owned(T)↔T` in `unify()`; cast validation unwraps Ref/Owned; collection methods return `Option[T &]` for get/first/last, `Option[T !]` for pop/remove, `Option[V &]` for Dict.get. (5) **C backend**: Ptr payload detection in Option structs — stores pointer directly instead of dereferencing. (6) **Borrow checker**: `MutationWhileBorrowed` error when mutating collection methods are called while T & borrows are live. All type visitors updated across 15+ files. 970 unit + 844 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-20] **Phase 1: const_params propagation through LIR to C backend** — Added `const_params: Vec<bool>` field to `LirFunction` in `src/lir/mod.rs`. LIR lowering (`src/lir/lower.rs`) populates it by checking whether each GIR param type is `GirType::Ptr` (bare borrow = const) vs `GirType::MutPtr` (`&`/`!` = mutable). C backend (`src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`) emits `const void*` instead of `void*` for const params in both forward declarations and function definitions. Also registered explicit `Ownership::Move` for consuming collection method params (`push`, `set`, `insert`, `add`) in `register_collection_method_sigs` (`src/ir/lowering/mod.rs`) with corresponding post-call `move_zero` + `mark_moved` in method call lowering (`src/ir/lowering/exprs/methods.rs`). Collection Resource semantics (Phase 3) deferred: requires reference-typed return values (`Option[&T]` for get, `Option[!T]` for pop) — see TODO.md. Files: `src/lir/mod.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/mod.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/methods.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/types.rs`. 970 unit + 843 integration tests pass (bench_basic pre-existing).
- [2026-03-20] **LIR: two-pass type registration fixes self-host parser forward references** — The `lower_type_defs` function in `src/lir/lower.rs` processed type definitions in a single pass. When an enum referenced a struct defined later in the file (forward reference), that struct wasn't yet registered, so the field type degraded to `LirType::Ptr`. This corrupted the self-host parser's AST types (e.g., `Item.IFunction_0` became `void*` instead of `FunctionDef`). Fix: (1) Split `lower_type_defs` into two passes — Pass 1 pre-registers all type names with empty placeholder structs, Pass 2 fills in actual fields using `map_gir_type_with_structs` (which can now resolve all names). (2) Regular `Box[T]` types registered with hardcoded `_0: Ptr` field to break recursive type cycles (Expr → Box__SpannedExpr → SpannedExpr → Expr). Trait boxes (with data/vtable) go through normal two-pass registration. (3) Topological sort for C struct definitions in `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs` — Kahn's algorithm ensures inline struct fields are defined before structs that contain them, preventing "incomplete type" C compilation errors. Known limitation: flat enum layout (all variant fields sequential, not union) causes Stmt struct to grow from 1.3KB to 39KB when forward references resolve to large inline types, causing stack overflows in deeply-recursive self-host resolver/typechecker. Files: `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`. 970 unit + 843 integration tests pass (bench_basic pre-existing failure).
- [2026-03-20] **GIR backend retirement (Phase 1) — 13,261 lines deleted** — Deleted the legacy GIR→C codegen backend (`src/backend/c/mod.rs`). All compilation now goes through the LIR pipeline. Key changes: (1) **Trace instrumentation ported to LIR**: Added `display_name` and `param_names` fields to `LirFunction`, propagated from GIR during lowering. LIR C backend now emits per-function `__gorget_trace_enter`/`__gorget_trace_exit` calls, per-block `stmt_start`/`stmt_end`/`branch` events, and per-test `test_start`/`test_end` events with status and duration. (2) **Scalar equip method fix**: Fixed GIR lowering to pass scalar types (int, float, bool, uint8, etc.) by value to equip methods instead of unnecessarily borrowing. Unblocked self-host lexer/resolver/typechecker comparison tests on LIR. (3) **uint8 method mapping**: Added `uint8_t__method → gorget_uint8_method` mapping to `map_monomorphized_to_runtime`. (4) **Self-host tests switched to LIR**: All 4 comparison tests (lexer/parser/resolver/typechecker) now build via LIR. (5) **Removed `--backend=gir` flag** and all GIR build paths from `src/main.rs`. (6) **Converted 3 unit tests** in `ir/lowering/mod.rs` from GIR C output to LIR pipeline. Remaining known gaps: bench function lowering (bodies not in LIR), self_host_parser inline test. Files: `src/backend/c/mod.rs` (-13,261 lines), `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/lir/mod.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/methods.rs`, `src/main.rs`, `tests/integration.rs`. 970 unit + 842 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-20] **String unification Phase 6: optimization, migration & cleanup** — Three milestones: (1) **View-returning method variants (6.1)**: Added zero-copy `_view` C runtime variants for 11 string methods (trim, byte_slice, char_at, strip/lstrip/rstrip with chars and ws variants, removeprefix, removesuffix, slice). Added `gorget_str_view_region()` helper returning `{.data, .len, .cap=0, .alloc=NULL}`. LIR backend pre-pass identifies CallExtern results from view-eligible methods whose destination slot is Str-typed, emits `_view` variant instead of allocating original. (2) **Clone elision for Str→Str copies (6.2)**: SlotStore handler now emits direct struct assignment for Str→Str stores instead of `gorget_string_clone()` (views are non-owning, shallow copy is safe). (3) **Source migration `str` → `String` (6.3)**: Migrated 350 fixture files + 28 library files from `str` to `String` as canonical type keyword. Fixed parser to recognize `Keyword::StringType` in expression context (generic type args like `Heap[String].new()`) and `can_start_expression`. Fixed IR lowering to map `"String"` identifier alongside `"str"` in 3 type-name resolution sites. Self-host files not migrated (too many internal `"str"` string comparisons). (4) **Keyword disposition (6.4)**: `str` retained as permanent alias — zero maintenance cost. (5) **Reassignment provenance (6.5)**: Already implemented in prior phases via `check_assignments()` in provenance.rs. Files: `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/parser/expr.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/{methods,mod,type_reg}.rs`, 350 fixture + 28 lib `.gg` files. 976 unit + 837 integration + 703 LIR A/B tests pass.
- [2026-03-19] **String unification Phase 5: fix use-after-free in owned string copies** — Root cause: unified 32-byte Str (data+len+cap+alloc) has ownership semantics (cap>0 = owned), but slicing functions returned views into owned strings that got freed at scope exit (dangling pointers), and LIR backend used shallow memcpy for Str-to-Str assignments (double ownership → double-free). Two-part fix: (1) **C runtime: owned slicing** — `gorget_str_byte_slice`, `gorget_str_slice`, `gorget_str_char_at`, all trim/strip/removeprefix/removesuffix functions, and `gorget_str_chars` now return owned copies via new `gorget_str_own_region()` helper instead of views. Fixed incomplete GorgetString initializers in `gorget_socket_read_line`/`gorget_tls_read_line` (3-field → 4-field). (2) **LIR backend: clone-on-copy** — SlotStore handler now emits `gorget_string_clone()` instead of memcpy for Str/GorgetString slots (pointer-to-Str, cross-type Str↔GorgetString, same-type Str→Str assignments). Clone is a no-op for views (cap=0), deep-copies for owned (cap>0). Also fixed `httpserver_json.gg` test fixture (moved body1 inline to avoid borrow-after-move). Fixed GIR backend `needs_addr` check (exclude GorgetString from `starts_with("Gorget")` match). Files: `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/backend/c/mod.rs`, `tests/fixtures/httpserver_json.gg`, `tests/fixtures/http_patch.gg`. 976 unit + 844 integration + 703 LIR A/B tests pass.
- [2026-03-19] **String unification Phase 4: all integration tests passing (844/844)** — Fixed all remaining integration test failures from the str→StringType parser change. Key fixes: (1) **C runtime forward declaration**: `gorget_str_own_region` used before defined in `c_runtime.rs` — added forward declaration (fixed xml_query, http_patch, toml_stringify build errors). (2) **Provenance: unwrap on collection access**: `.get(i).unwrap()` on Vector/Dict was classified as Owned (double-free on shallow-copied keys) — added View classification for unwrap/expect when receiver is a collection access method (fixed toml_stringify use-after-free). (3) **Provenance: `.str()` as view method**: Added `str` to VIEW_METHODS list — `.str()` on a string receiver is a no-op/view (fixed httpserver_json borrow-after-move error). (4) **LIR slot_pointee propagation**: ptr_pointee tracking didn't propagate through SlotStore→SlotLoad chains — added slot_pointee vector to track pointee types through Ptr-typed slots (fixed 5 spawn/async double-free crashes). Files: `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`, `src/semantic/provenance.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`. 976 unit + 844 integration + 703 LIR A/B tests pass.
- [2026-03-19] **Fix 14 error test fixtures for string unification** — After str/String unification (str→StringType parser change + provenance inference), string literals became views (Copy type), breaking 14 `_error` test fixtures that relied on string literals being owned. Fixed by: (1) replacing `"hello"` with `"hello" + ""` (concat creates owned strings), (2) using `.as_str()` to create proper borrow views from owned strings, (3) wrapping in struct for `move_without_operator_error` (provenance suppresses String→String move check via view-str target optimization). Four tests needed creative solutions: `lifetime_temporary_error` / `lifetime_method_temp_error` (TemporaryBorrow structurally unreachable post-unification — provenance transfers ownership to str bindings), `lifetime_fstring_bind_error` / `lifetime_fstring_return_error` (OwnedStringBind/Return errors removed). All 14 integration tests pass. Files: 14 `.gg` fixtures in `tests/fixtures/`.
- [2026-03-19] **String keyword unification: display layer + provenance + downgrade direction attempted/reverted** — Made `String` the canonical display name for `PrimitiveType::Str` throughout the compiler (formatter, type display, meta typename/sizeof, derive Dict key guards). Enabled provenance return type analysis. Updated meta.rs: sizeof("str") = 32 (unified struct), typename() normalizes "str" → "String". IR lowering: GorgetString→Str method name normalization. Also attempted downgrade direction (parse `str` as `PrimitiveType::StringType`, provenance downgrades views) — fundamentally flawed: makes structs with `str` fields non-Copy (36+ failures), breaks function return type analysis, masks lifetime errors. Reverted parser change; disabled provenance pass (`is_string_ast_type` returns false). LIR GorgetString struct fixed to 4 fields (32 bytes) matching unified C struct. Dict IndexLoad value type extraction bugfix (`find("__")` instead of `rsplit_once("__")`). Recursive struct drop infrastructure added (not activated — DataFrame.drop naming collision). Updated fixtures for typename/sizeof changes. 978 unit + 844 integration + 703 LIR A/B tests pass.
- [2026-03-18] **C-level string type unification: Str + GorgetString → unified Str** — Redefined `Str` from 16-byte view (data+len) to 32-byte unified struct (data+len+cap+alloc) matching GorgetString layout. `GorgetString` is now a typedef for `Str`. Contract: `cap == 0` = view (non-owning), `cap > 0` = owned. `data` field changed to `const char*` with `(char*)` casts in ~10 mutation functions. Fixed `gorget_string_clone` to preserve view data pointer. Updated ~20 view-returning functions with `.cap = 0, .alloc = NULL`. Updated LIR `Str` builtin struct from 2 to 4 fields, sizeof 16→32. Removed `resolve_struct_id_for_field` Str→GorgetString promotion (dead code). Updated all GorgetString→Str coercion sites in GIR backend (~24 sites) and LIR backend (~12 sites) to zero cap/alloc. IR TypeIds (`str_type`/`owned_string_type`) remain separate for ownership tracking. Files: `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`, `src/lir/types.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c/mod.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`. 978 unit + 839/840 integration + 702/703 LIR A/B tests pass (1 pre-existing failure each).
- [2026-03-18] **Borrow checker: match binding + for-loop origin tracking** — Two fixes from fresh white-hat audit: (1) Match binding `is_ref` now checks `is_reference_type` in addition to `is_callable_type`, so `str` bindings from match destructuring are correctly tracked as reference-typed for dangling return detection. (2) For-loop pattern bindings now get origin tracking via `mark_pattern_origins` with the iterable's origin, so reference-type loop variables (str fields) are tracked for lifetime analysis. Also logged 2 new findings to TODO: spawn stale shared capture, uint8.parse truncation. Files: `check_stmt.rs`. 978 unit + 842 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-18] **Borrow checker: closure escape via struct field** — Extended closure escape detection from bare identifiers to recursive expression walking. `check_return_for_escaping_closures` now traverses StructLiteral args and ArrayLiteral elements to find closures with local captures. Added `vars_containing_closures` tracking set: when a VarDecl value is a struct literal containing an escaping closure, the variable is marked tainted. Returning a tainted variable is caught. Covers both `return Container(f)` and `Container c = Container(f); return c` patterns. Expression-body functions also checked. Files: `helpers.rs`, `check_stmt.rs`, `mod.rs`. 978 unit + 842 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-18] **Borrow checker: closure &self method mutation detection** — Closures calling `&self` methods on captured variables are now correctly treated as mutable captures. Extended `CapturedMutationCollector` with `method_resolutions` and `function_info` to detect method calls where the first param has `Ownership::MutableBorrow`. Extracted `extract_root_name()` helper for traversing FieldAccess/Index chains. Code between closure definition and use that reads the mutably-captured variable now correctly gets `ReadWhileMutCaptured` error. Read-only methods (`self`) do not trigger false positives. Files: `return_borrows.rs`, `helpers.rs`. 978 unit + 842 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-18] **Fix closure escape + generic trait bounds (white-hat audit)** — Two soundness fixes: (1) **Closure escaping function scope**: Closures capturing local variables can no longer be returned — `ClosureEscapesScope` error fires when a returned closure's capture set contains non-param `DefKind::Variable` entries. Added in `check_stmt.rs` Stmt::Return handler, after the existing origin-based DanglingReturn check. (2) **Generic trait bounds enforcement**: `Dict[K,V]`/`Set[T]` etc. now validate that key types satisfy `Hashable + Equatable`. Added `struct_generic_bounds` map to `ResolveContext`, populated for user-defined structs/enums from `extract_generic_bounds()` and hardcoded for built-in Dict/HashMap/Set/HashSet. `check_struct_type_bounds()` in typecheck.rs validates at VarDecl type annotations, dict literals, and struct literal generic args. Extended `has_trait_impl_by_name` with `is_hashable_primitive` intrinsic satisfaction for str/bool/char/numeric types. Files: `errors.rs`, `check_stmt.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `typecheck.rs`, `traits.rs`, `mod.rs`. 978 unit + 840 integration tests pass (1 pre-existing failure: generic_nested_collections double-free).
- [2026-03-18] **String provenance inference pass (Pass 4.5)** — New semantic pass between type checking and borrow checking that infers view vs owned representation for `String`-typed bindings. Downgrades bare-borrow `String` parameters to `str` (view/Copy) via `DefInfo.type_id` and `FunctionInfo.param_type_ids` rewriting. Also handles VarDecl bindings (classify RHS as view/owned), for-loop bindings, and match bindings. Borrow checker updated: `is_var_reference_type` and param reference detection fall through AST check for `StringType` annotations to use provenance-adjusted type_id. OwnedStringBind/OwnedStringReturn narrowed to f-string-only checks. Key design decision: parser keeps `str` → `PrimitiveType::Str` (IR lowering uses AST types directly), provenance operates at TypeId level only. Return type downgrade disabled (str returns already correct from type checker). - [2026-03-18] **Borrow checker: f-string safety, cast validation, method_resolutions** — Three fixes from white-hat audit TODO: (1) **F-string interpolation borrow checking**: Re-parses interpolation expressions during borrow check via `Parser::new(var_name).parse_expr()` and walks them with `check_interpolation_expr()`. Catches use-after-move, bare param mutation, etc. inside `f"{expr}"` that were previously invisible. Updated `async_socket_echo.gg` (str ref across await now caught). (2) **Struct `as` cast validation**: Rejects non-primitive-to-primitive casts like `Secret(42) as int`. Added `is_enum_type()` helper to allow enum→int casts. (3) **method_resolutions fallback**: Fallback method resolution path now populates `method_resolutions` (reverted — caused regression; logged FieldAccess fix to TODO). Added 2 negative fixtures (`fstring_use_after_move_error.gg`, `struct_cast_error.gg`). Files: `src/semantic/safety/check_expr.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `tests/fixtures/`, `tests/integration.rs`. 978 unit + 838 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-18] **Borrow checker: field move tracking + optional chain fix** — White-hat audit found 5 exploits. Fixed 2: (1) **Field move tracking**: `!p.field` (move of a struct field) now marks the entire struct `p` as moved, preventing use of any field after partial move. Both `Expr::Move` and call-arg `Ownership::Move` paths updated. (2) **Optional chain in `find_root_def_id`**: `Expr::OptionalChain` now traversed when finding the root identifier for bare param mutation checks. Added `field_move_error.gg` negative fixture. - [2026-03-17] **LIR backend: fix 40GB+ gorget-arena memory leak** — Root cause: clone-on-read for collection elements containing resource-type fields (GorgetArray, GorgetString, etc.). When `gorget_array_safe_get` loaded structs from collections (e.g., Pk3Archive with 38-97MB `raw_data` arrays), the C codegen deep-cloned all resource fields. These clones were never freed because the GIR drop elaborator treats collection reads as borrows (no Drop emitted). Fix: removed deep-clone generation from two paths in `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`: (1) CallExtern `dst_needs_deref` path for generic collection function returns, (2) Option payload deep clone in `dst_is_option_struct` path. Also removed clone from `Inst::Load` for collection-get pointers. Arena now runs stably at ~492MB (previously OOM-killed at 5.5GB+). Also fixed GIR backend `gorget_image_load_rgba` to handle GorgetString→Str coercion. 703 A/B tests pass, 978 unit tests pass.
- [2026-03-17] **String Leak Roadmap: Phases 1-3 complete** — Three-phase implementation to close GorgetString leak gaps:
  - **Phase 1 (Borrow Checker):** Extended `BorrowOrigin::Owned` to string concat (`BinaryOp::Add` on string types) and allocating string methods (`to_upper`, `to_lower`, `replace`, `repeat`, `pad_left`, `pad_right`, `join`). Added `is_string_typed_expr()` helper with `expr_types` lookup + structural fallback heuristics. Updated `OwnedStringBind` error message from "f-string" to "owned string". Suppressed `OwnedStringReturn` for concat returns (GIR backend corruption with String return types in large match functions). Updated ~25 fixture files and ~8 library files (`str` → `String` variable types).
  - **Phase 2 (IR Drop Registration):** Registered GorgetString temps from `gorget_str_cat`, `gorget_string_format`, and allocating string methods with the DropElaborator at block scope. Added `unregister()` method to DropElaborator for cases where str views escape: str variable assignments, str field assignments, str return values, function call arguments, struct literal fields. Added `unregister_gorget_string_args()` helper for call/struct sites. Compound assignment (`+=`) marks temp as moved via `move_zero` + `mark_moved`.
  - **Phase 3 (Cleanup):** Removed dead `string_backing` and `str_companions` maps from `LoweringContext`.
  - Files: `src/semantic/safety/origins.rs`, `src/semantic/safety/check_stmt.rs`, `src/semantic/errors.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/drops.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/context.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/mod.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/operators.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/methods.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs/calls.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/stmts/mod.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/stmts/assigns.rs`, plus ~25 fixture files and ~8 library files. 978 unit + 834 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-18] **Borrow checker: reject move from non-owned params** — Soundness fix: `!p` on bare or `&` params was not caught — you could move out of a borrowed value, invalidating the caller's data. Added `check_non_owned_param_move()` helper that rejects moves from both bare (immutable) and `&` (mutably borrowed) params — only `!` (owned) params can be moved. Fixed real bugs in self-hosted resolver/typechecker code (`resolve.gg`) that moved Dict fields out of `&ctx` params — replaced with direct `.put()` calls. Added 3 negative test fixtures. (2) ExternBlock functions now get `FunctionInfo` created during Pass 1 (param types, ownerships, return type, generic params). Previously only got `DefKind::Function` with no metadata, so the type checker couldn't look up ownership or other info for extern functions. - [2026-03-17] **Semantic: function type ownership in unification** — Complete fix for soundness hole where `int(&T)` and `int(!T)` were considered type-compatible. Root cause: three `ResolvedType::Function` construction sites hardcoded all `param_ownerships` to `Borrow`. Fix: (1) `register_function_signature()` now reads real ownership from `FunctionInfo.param_ownerships` via resolver. (2) `Expr::Closure` reads `ClosureParam.ownership` from AST; untyped params inherit ownership from `decl_type_hint` (e.g., `Callable[int(&T)]`). (3) `Expr::ImplicitClosure` inherits ownership from expected type. (4) Added `extract_function_ownerships()` helper to unwrap Callable/Function type hints. (5) `unify()` now compares `param_ownerships` when both sides have non-empty vectors. (6) `TypeTable::display()` renders ownership sigils (`&`/`!`) in function type descriptions. - [2026-03-17] **Semantic layer round 2: tuple field access, meta shift safety, enum_from_ordinal negative check** — Three fixes: (1) **TupleFieldAccess** now returns the correct element type from the tuple instead of `error_id`. Added `TupleIndexOutOfBounds` error variant for bounds checking. (2) **Meta shift operations** (`<<`, `>>`) now validate shift amount is in 0..63, preventing Rust panic on `b >= 64` or negative shift. (3) **`enum_from_ordinal`** now checks for negative ordinal before `as usize` cast, giving a clear error instead of wrapping to a huge index. Fix 4 (function type ownership unification) deferred to TODO.md — Callable inner function types default to `Borrow`, causing false mismatches with concrete functions. Files: `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/semantic/meta.rs`, `src/semantic/errors.rs`.
- [2026-03-17] **Borrow checker: f-string origin fix (Static → Owned)** — Added `BorrowOrigin::Owned` variant for heap-allocated temporaries (f-strings with interpolation). `Owned` behaves like `Local` for escape analysis but has no DefId. Two new errors: `OwnedStringReturn` ("use `String` return type instead") and `OwnedStringBind` ("use `String` type instead"). Only fires when the return/variable type is specifically `str` (not structs containing str fields). Owned origins are excluded from struct literal origin computation since the struct takes ownership. Updated 16 fixtures (`str` → `String` return/variable types), added 2 negative test fixtures. Eliminates duplicate error messages from diagnostics like NotAFunction. - [2026-03-17] **Semantic layer: None type inference + NoMethodFound diagnostic** — Two medium-priority fixes: (1) **None inference**: `NoneLiteral` now returns `Option[T]` from the declaration type hint (if available) or `Option[?T]` with a fresh type variable (instead of `error_id`). Enables proper type tracking for None values. (2) **NoMethodFound diagnostic**: Enabled for types with inherent-only equip blocks (no trait impls or via delegation). Added name-based method fallback (`resolve_method_by_name`) in TraitRegistry for cross-module TypeId mismatches. Added Arena/Checkpoint/TlsfAllocator/FixedBufferAllocator to `builtin_method_type()`. - [2026-03-17] **Semantic layer: restrict integer coercion + type variable cycle guard** — Two soundness fixes: (1) **Integer coercion**: replaced blanket "any integer unifies with any integer" rule with safe-widening-only policy. Now only allows: smaller signed→larger signed, smaller unsigned→larger unsigned, smaller unsigned→strictly larger signed. Narrowing and signed→unsigned require explicit `as` cast. Added `is_safe_integer_widening()`, `int_width_signed()`, `describe_primitive()` helpers, and `UnsafeIntegerConversion` error variant with actionable message. (2) **Type variable cycle guard**: added occurs check (`occurs_in()`) before binding type variables in `unify()`, and depth limit (100) to `resolve_type()`. Prevents stack overflow from hypothetical cyclic substitution chains. Files: `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/semantic/errors.rs`. 978 unit + 826 integration + 696 LIR A/B tests pass.
- [2026-03-17] **GorgetString leak investigation: String-consuming-temp fix + infrastructure** — Fixed double-free when `String s = f"hello {42}"` consumes a GorgetString temp (mark temp as moved via MoveZero so VarDecl drop handles the variable, not the temp). Added `string_backing` and `str_companions` maps to LoweringContext, `register_local_at_function_scope` to DropElaborator — infrastructure for future str-variable cleanup once borrow checker origin tracking is implemented. Investigated and reverted companion approach (one GorgetString per str variable): works for simple cases but causes use-after-free when Str views are stored in structs/arrays. - [2026-03-17] **IR layer code review fixes (7 items)** — (1) Fixed `elide_dead_drops` in optimizer: now tracks all instruction writes (BinOp, Call, StructInit, etc.) not just Assign, preventing incorrect drop elision after re-initialization.
