- [2026-03-16] **Refactor: split monolithic `borrow.rs` (9,845 lines) into `safety/` module** — Renamed `src/semantic/borrow.rs` → `src/semantic/safety/` with 9 focused submodules: `mod.rs` (632 lines, core types + struct + entry point), `type_utils.rs` (181, copy/ref-type utilities), `origins.rs` (710, borrow origin tracking + branch state), `helpers.rs` (831, concurrency + call/lookup helpers), `check_expr.rs` (777, expression walker), `check_stmt.rs` (1,394, statement/block/function walker), `return_borrows.rs` (751, Pass 5a + closure visitors + alias map), `validation.rs` (563, private-in-public + unused imports + purity inference), `tests.rs` (4,106, all unit tests). Pure mechanical refactoring — no behavior changes. 978 unit + 825 integration + 596 LIR A/B tests pass.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: default backend switch + 6 bug fixes** — LIR is now the default backend. Fixed 6 bugs blocking the switch: (1) Printf format parser: `%.*s` and `%lld` mishandled — added proper skip for flags/width/precision (including `*` consuming extra args) and length modifiers. (2) File.create/File.open: rewrite `gorget_file_create(path)`→`gorget_file_open(cstr, "w")`, single-arg `gorget_file_open`→`gorget_file_open(cstr, "r")`, `gorget_file_read_all` Result wrapping. (3) Set iteration order: ordered sets (`Set__`) now iterate via `order[]` array (insertion order) with tombstone checks; `HashSet__` retains bucket order. (4) Double `&` on Ptr-typed closure args: inline higher-order methods (filter/map/fold/etc.) now check if closure arg is already a Ptr before adding `&`. (5) Fold accumulator type: uses destination type instead of element type, with Str/GorgetString mismatch detection. Static helper fold changed to `__typeof__` macro. (6) Dict/array value load clone: `collection_get_vals` bitmap tracks pointers from `gorget_map_get`/`gorget_array_get`; `Inst::Load` clones resource types from these pointers. Enrolled 7 new fixtures in lir_ab.rs (694 total). Flipped default: `--backend=gir` opts into legacy. Integration tests updated: self-host/comparison/trace/cli_args use `--backend=gir`. All tests pass: 694 A/B, 825 integration, 978 unit.
- [2026-03-16] **Remove `raw` keyword — type-directed Result capture**: Removed `raw` error handling keyword entirely. Replaced with type-directed Result capture: `Result[int, str] r = throwing_call()` suppresses auto-unwrap when destination type is `Result[T, E]`. Added `is_result_capture_compatible()` to typecheck.rs. Removed `RawCapture` from AST, parser, visitor, formatter, all 5 semantic passes, IR lowering (50 lines of zero-default logic), and loader. Removed from all 8 self-host .gg files (lexer, parser, resolver, typechecker). Updated all documentation (language-reference, language-design, book chapters 9/10, appendix). Fixed formatter to emit `extern` keyword for standalone extern functions (was dropped, causing idempotency failure with new `: expr` syntax). 978 unit + 824 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: hot-reload + `--shared` support** — Made hot-reload and `--shared` builds work through the LIR backend. Changes: (1) Extracted `HotReloadOpts` and `generate_hot_reload_split()` from GIR-local `c/mod.rs` to shared `backend/mod.rs`, with prefix-matching `int main(` pattern (handles LIR's `int main(void)` and GIR's `int main(int argc, char** argv)`). GIR delegates to shared fn. (2) Added 4 hot-reload metadata fields to `LirModule`, copied from GIR during lowering. (3) c_lir backend: emits `HOT_RELOAD_RUNTIME` when hot-reload enabled, emits `typedef __lir_sN State` for the state type, and adds `// ── Function Definitions ──` marker for host/guest code splitting. (4) `find_live_functions` optimizer: added `init`/`tick`/`reload` as DCE roots when `hot_reload` is true (otherwise optimizer removes all functions since there's no `main`). (5) main.rs: wired `--shared` and hot-reload two-phase build for LIR backend (recompile command includes `--backend=lir`). (6) Integration test `hot_reload_basic_lir`. Files: `src/backend/mod.rs`, `src/backend/c/mod.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/lir/mod.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/lir/optimize.rs`, `src/main.rs`, `tests/integration.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: Phase 4 — cross-module type resolution + stdlib name mapping** — Two fixes: (1) **Shared `map_stdlib_name`**: Extracted the stdlib name mapping table (~320 entries: `read_file_bytes`→`gorget_read_file_bytes`, `mem_live`→`gorget_mem_live`, etc.) from `src/backend/c/mod.rs` into a shared `pub fn map_stdlib_name()` in `src/backend/mod.rs`. Both the GIR C backend and LIR lowerer now delegate to this single canonical table, eliminating ~290 lines of duplicated mapping from `map_monomorphized_to_runtime()` in `lower.rs`. Fixes linker errors for `read_file_bytes`, `mem_live`, `mem_allocated`, `mem_freed` in gorget-arena LIR builds. (2) **Cross-module type registration**: Added `register_extern_types()` to `LoweringContext`, called after `lower_type_defs()`. Scans all extern declarations, function signatures, locals, and globals for `GirType::Named` references not yet in `struct_reg`. For types with a `TypeDef` in the registry (cross-module imports), lowers them using the same Struct/Enum logic. For types without a `TypeDef` (e.g., `Result__double__Str` from imported `parse_float`), synthesizes struct fields from the monomorphized name pattern (`Result__X__Y` → `{ tag: I32, Ok_0: map(X), Error_0: map(Y) }`). This prevents `map_gir_type_with_structs` from falling back to `LirType::Ptr` for cross-module Result/Option types. A/B tests: 596/596 (100%). Unit tests: 978 passed. Gorget-arena LIR: 0 errors, 0 linker errors, 4 C warnings. Files: `src/backend/mod.rs`, `src/backend/c/mod.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: multi-file Phase 3 — gorget-arena 6 errors → 0, ~136 warnings → 4** — Five fixes: (1) **Implicit Result::Ok / Option::Some wrapping**: SlotStore of primitive scalars (I64/F64/Bool) into Result/Option slots now emits `memset + tag=0 + field=val` instead of direct assignment (mirrors GIR C backend pattern at mod.rs:5883). (2) **GL_GLEXT_PROTOTYPES**: Added `#define GL_GLEXT_PROTOTYPES` before `#include <GL/gl.h>` on Linux, fixing 115 implicit declaration warnings + 3 int-conversion warnings (shared with GIR). (3) **const-discard warnings**: cstr_val values (from `gorget_int_to_str` etc.) now declared as `const char*` instead of `void*`. (4) **Cast-to-Str with Ptr source**: When casting a pointer to Str, uses `gorget_str_from_cstr()` instead of falling through to `gorget_int_to_str()`. (5) **_DEFAULT_SOURCE guard**: Wrapped with `#ifndef` to prevent redefinition warning. Remaining 4 C warnings: 2 `gorget_int_to_str` Ptr→int (LIR slot type inference issue), 2 memset overflow (struct size mismatch). Files: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: multi-file Phase 2 — gorget-arena C errors reduced from 61 to 6** — Six fixes: (1) **Cast-to-Str lowering**: `Instruction::Cast` with target type `Str` was blindly lowered to `Inst::IntCast { to: Str }`, generating invalid `(Str)(value)` in C. Now emits `CallExtern` to `gorget_int_to_str`/`gorget_float_to_str`/`gorget_bool_to_str` based on source type. Also handles Cast-to-Void (nop). (2) **Out-param call handling**: Added `try_emit_outparam_call_lir()` for `gorget_image_load_rgba*`, `gorget_audio_load_wav*`, `gorget_image_flip_vertically`, `gorget_deflate_decompress` — rewrites single-return calls to out-param form with LIR struct field names (`Ok_0`/`Error_0` instead of GIR's `data.Ok._0`). (3) **GL/Metal string literal wrapping**: Added `runtime_fn_str_param()` to detect GL/Metal runtime functions that take `Str` by value, wrapping string literal args with `gorget_str_from_literal()` and dereferencing Ptr args as `*(Str*)`. (4) **`gorget_sdl_get_error` cstr return**: Added to `is_cstr_returning_fn()`. (5) **`gorget_str_cat("", val)` coercion**: Detects `str()` coercion pattern (empty string + non-Str arg) and rewrites to `gorget_string_new(gorget_int_to_str(val))`. (6) **Runtime Ptr→Str deref**: Extended Ptr→Str coercion to runtime functions that take Str (not just trait box methods). Remaining 6 errors: cross-file `Result[T, str]` return types resolved as scalar by `map_gir_type_with_structs`. Files: `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: multi-file Phase 1 — gorget-arena C errors reduced from ~300+ to ~61** — Six fixes: (1) Dead global elimination index remapping — `eliminate_dead_globals` removed globals from vec without remapping `GlobalAddr` indices in functions, causing all subsequent global references to be off; added old→new index remapping table. (2) SDL/GL/Image/Audio/Compression/Metal/SQLite runtime detection — mirrored GIR backend's conditional `#include`/`#define` blocks for all optional runtimes. (3) SDL function name remapping — added `sdl_*`→`gorget_sdl_*` catch-all in `map_monomorphized_to_runtime()`. (4) RuntimeCall struct name rewriting — compound literals in global init expressions used original struct names (e.g., `Vec3`) but LIR renames to `__lir_sN`; built reverse map for rewriting. (5) Single-field newtype scalar↔struct coercion in SlotStore. (6) Float global struct field emission — `emit_global_init_value` recursed into struct fields with `LirType::I64`, causing float fields to emit as integer bit patterns (subnormals); now passes actual field types from StructDef. Also added SDL/GL/Audio opaque handle type mappings (SDLWindow, SDLRenderer, SDLTexture, SDLFont, SDLEvent, AudioChunk, AudioMusic, GLContext) to `lir_to_runtime_name()`. Files: `src/lir/optimize.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: 596/596 A/B parity (100%)** — Fixed last 2 failures (async_select, stress_channel_select) caused by select loop deadlock. Root cause: `__gorget_select_yield()` called `__gorget_try_run_one()` which could steal a blocking spawn task from the executor queue and run it inline on the main thread. When the stolen producer task blocked on `gorget_channel_send` (channel full), the main thread could never return to `poll_recv` to drain the channel → deadlock. Fix: added `__gorget_try_run_one_nonblocking()` that only steals poll-based (coroutine) tasks, skipping run-based (blocking) tasks. Select yield now uses this variant. Files: `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **Fix `gorget_array_remove_opt` buffer truncation (586→594/596)** — The runtime's `gorget_array_remove_opt` used a 256-byte static thread-local buffer to hold the removed element. `ReliableStream` (`__lir_s30`) is 664 bytes with `cwnd` at exactly offset 256 — the first field NOT copied. This caused cwnd=0 in all p2p reliable stream tests (flush_send never sent data because can_send=cwnd-flight_size=0). Fix: enlarged static buffer to 4096 bytes with heap fallback for elements exceeding that. Fixed p2p_reliable_basic, p2p_reliable_bidir, p2p_reliable_large, p2p_stream_robust. File: `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: fix TaskGroup spawn segfault (580→586/596)** — When a spawn result was stored into a `void*` destination (the `dst_is_spawn_ptr` path), the codegen extracted only `.__task` from the `Task__void` struct, discarding the `.__drop` function pointer. Later, `gorget_task_group_submit` cast the raw `void*` value as `*(Task__void*)ptr`, reading garbage memory for `__drop` (segfault). Fix: added `spawn_source_fn` tracking vector in the pre-pass to record which spawn function produced each `void*` value, then in `task_group_submit` emit `gorget_task_group_submit_raw(group, task_ptr, __spawn_drop_fn)` directly, bypassing the macro and providing the correct drop function. Fixed stress_shared_comprehensive + 5 other tests. File: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: fix Str→GorgetString coercion in SlotStore/Store** — When a function returning `Str` (16 bytes) had its result stored into a `GorgetString` slot (32 bytes), the backend emitted `memcpy(&slot, &val, sizeof(GorgetString))` — reading 32 bytes from 16-byte Str data (stack-buffer-overflow). Root cause: the SlotStore handler had coercion for GorgetString→Str (downcast) but not the reverse Str→GorgetString (upcast). Added `gorget_string_from_str()` coercion for both pointer and by-value cases in SlotStore, plus Str*→GorgetString* coercion in Inst::Store. Fixed httpserver_json segfault (exit 139). File: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **LIR backend: fix p2p_discovery (580→581/596)** — Added `gorget_udp_join_multicast` and `gorget_udp_leave_multicast` to `takes_cstr_for_str_param()` (param_idx == 1) and `needs_null_terminated_cstr()` in the LIR C backend. These UDP multicast functions take `const char*` group addresses but the LIR backend was passing raw `Str*` pointers, causing silent multicast join failure (nodes never discovered each other). File: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.
- [2026-03-16] **Fix UncheckedUnwrap false positives for `is_error()` guard**: Borrow checker's fallible-unwrap lint recognized `is_err` (Rust-style) but not `is_error()` (Gorget's actual Result method). Added `"is_error"` to the guard recognition match in `src/semantic/borrow.rs:1926`. Eliminates all 11 false-positive warnings in gorget-arena. Also resolved the "wrong span in imported files" TODO — those were the same root cause (unrecognized guard → spurious warnings).
- [2026-03-15] **LIR backend: 570→580/596 A/B tests passing (97.3%)** — Five fixes: (1) **Task struct sizeof**: Task__T types have 0 fields in LIR (GIR TypeDef has empty StructDef), causing `gorget_array_new(0)` for Vector[Task[T]] and `memset(ptr, 0, 0)` for MoveZero. Added Task__ case to `c_sizeof_with_structs` and `c_sizeof_lir_type` returning 16 (2 pointers: __task + __drop). (2) **Spawn returns Task__T**: `__gorget_spawn_fn()` was returning `void*` (raw SpawnCtx pointer). Changed to return `Task__T{.__task = __ctx, .__drop = __spawn_drop_fn}` matching GIR behavior. (3) **Await takes Task__T by value**: `__gorget_await_fn()` was taking `void*`, causing wrong extraction for both vector and non-vector cases. Changed to take `Task__T task` by value, access `task.__task` directly. (4) **Shared param cloning in spawn**: Spawn helpers now clone `Shared__T` params (refcounted) to prevent dangling pointers. (5) **IndexLoad array-slot zeroing**: When moving resource-type elements out of collections via IndexLoad, the array/dict slot is now zeroed to prevent double-free when the collection is dropped. Also fixed spawn call site handling: Task struct destinations use direct assignment; void* destinations extract `.__task`; await call sites construct Task struct from void* args or dereference Task struct pointers as needed. Files: `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.
- [2026-03-15] **LIR backend: drop_struct_collection_fields fix (recipe-based compound element drops)** — Added `ElemDropAction` enum and `elem_drop_recipes` to LirModule for compound collection element drops. When collection elements need more than a single function call to drop (Custom drop + field drops, recursive field drops, nested collection element drops), the lowerer computes a recipe tree and the c_lir backend expands it to C code. Also added `infer_drop_strategy()` name-based fallback for collection types without TypeDefs in the GIR type registry.
- [2026-03-15] **Fix false-positive/noisy compiler warnings**: (1) CouldBeConst made opt-in via `--warn-const` flag — all 121 warnings verified legitimate but too noisy for default output. (2) Fix false positive UnusedVariable for f-string complex expressions (`f"{obj.method()}"`) by extracting root variable name. (3) Fix false positive NeedlessMutableBorrow for Move-type `&` params by checking `is_copy_type()`. (4) Fix UncheckedUnwrap wrong span by using `method.span` instead of `expr.span`. (5) Fix WrongFieldCount false positive for opaque structs (AtomicInt etc.). Arena: 153→29 warnings.
- [2026-03-15] **Add 4 missing compiler diagnostics**: ContinueOutsideLoop (bug fix — was emitting "break" message for `continue`), ReturnOutsideFunction (defensive check in Stmt::Return), DuplicateStructField (detected in rewrite pass before name stripping), WrongFieldCount (struct literal arg count vs field count). 3 new test fixtures + integration tests.
- [2026-03-15] **LIR backend: 569→570/596 A/B tests passing (95.6%)** — Three fixes: (1) **FuncAddr closure wrapping**: `Inst::FuncAddr` now emits a static 2-element closure array `{__adapt_fn, NULL}` instead of raw `(void*)&fn`, fixing SIGSEGV when bare function pointers are dispatched through the closure protocol (`((void**)cv)[0]`/`[1]`). Fixed httpserver_parse_request segfault (now hangs on networking instead). (2) **Collection element drops**: Added element-level drop loops before `gorget_array_free`/`gorget_map_free` for collections whose elements have Custom or Trivial drop strategies. LIR lowerer emits synthetic `__gorget_array_drop_elems__ElemDropFn(addr)` CallExtern instructions; c_lir backend expands them to C for-loops that iterate elements and call the destructor. (3) **Box inner type drop**: `Box[T]` drop now calls `T__drop(box_ptr)` before `free(box_ptr)` when T has a Custom drop. Fixed `drop_collections` (now prints "drop boxed", "drop elem-a", "drop elem-b"). Files: `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.

- [2026-03-15] **LIR backend: 565→569/596 A/B tests passing (95.5%)** — Fixed `gorget_try_parse_int`/`gorget_try_parse_float`/`bool__parse` handlers to properly coerce Str/Ptr-to-Str arguments to `const char*` via `gorget_str_to_cstr()` instead of raw `(const char*)` cast on Str struct pointers. Added `coerce_arg_to_cstr` helper that checks `val_types`/`ptr_pointee` to detect Str structs and Ptr-to-Str pointers. Also excluded `gorget_bytes_from_str`/`gorget_bytes_from_hex` from `collection_self_by_ptr()` (they take `const char*` not `GorgetArray*`). Fixed httpserver_static_enhanced (range requests), plus 3 other tests using int.parse/float.parse on non-literal strings. Files: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.

- [2026-03-15] **LIR backend: 529→565/596 A/B tests passing (94.8%)** — Three fixes: (1) **Post-push zeroing scope narrowed**: `gorget_map_put`/`gorget_array_push` post-push zeroing was zeroing user structs that transitively contain resources (e.g., Column containing TypedColumn containing GorgetArray), which corrupted subsequent reads of the value. Changed to only zero direct resource types (GorgetArray, GorgetMap, GorgetSet, GorgetString, GorgetClosure). Added `is_direct_resource_type()` helper. Fixed dataframe_csv, dataframe_extra, dataframe_ops, dataframe_tier2_groupby and many others. (2) **C string hex escape ambiguity**: `escape_c_string()` emitted `\xc3\xa9b` which C parses as `\xc3` + `\xa9b` (consuming 'b' as hex digit). Fixed by inserting `" "` string concatenation break after `\x` escapes when next char is a hex digit. Fixed str_codepoint_index. (3) **31 new fixtures enrolled** from main (closure_iife, control_*, recursion_*, enum_*, etc.) — all pass. Remaining 31 failures: ~22 environmental (p2p, httpserver networking, stress/concurrency), ~9 real bugs (2 drop, 1-2 test-cleanup, 1 chunked body, 1 pthread, 1 Dict header, 1 async_channel). Rebased onto main. Files: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `tests/lir_ab.rs`.
- [2026-03-15] **Gorget Arena: Q3 Feature Parity Phases 1-12 (Complete)** — Implemented all 12 phases from the Q3 rendering roadmap:
  - **Phase 1: Particle Textures** — Procedural 32x32 circle texture with radial alpha falloff, `gpu_draw_billboard_fx` with alpha/additive blend modes (Metal: separate pipeline objects; GL: dynamic `glBlendFunc`), `blend_mode` field on Particle, all 9 spawners updated.
  - **Phase 2: Time-Based Shader Effects** — `eval_wave()` (sin/triangle/sawtooth/square/inversesawtooth), `eval_tcmods()` (scroll/rotate/scale/stretch/turb/transform as combined 2x2 matrix + offset), `eval_rgb_gen()`/`eval_alpha_gen()`. Fixed wave parsing bug (args index 4→5 for freq). Expanded FragU struct from 4→192 bytes. Fragment shader applies UV transform, color mod multiply.
  - **Phase 3: Alpha Test** — `alphaFunc` parsing (GT0/LT128/GE128), fragment shader discard, alpha-tested faces classified as opaque (depth write ON).
  - **Phase 4: Multi-Stage Shader Rendering** — Refactored `render_world_culled` to draw once per shader stage with per-stage texture/blend/uniform. Stage texture cache (`stage_tex_names`/`stage_tex_handles` on BspGpuData). `load_stage_textures()` pre-loads all textures referenced by shader stages.
  - **Phase 5: Animated Textures** — `animMap` parsing (freq + all frame names), `resolve_stage_texture()` selects frame based on `game_time * freq`.
  - **Phase 6: Frustum Culling** — `FrustumPlane` struct, `extract_frustum_planes()` (Gribb/Hartmann from MVP), `frustum_cull_aabb()` (P-vertex test), integrated into `collect_visible_faces_frustum()`.
  - **Phase 7: Weapon Viewmodel** — `viewmodel.gg` with ViewmodelState, bob/sway from player velocity, narrow 65° FOV MVP. Loads weapon MD3 models per weapon index, clears depth before drawing, renders after particles.
  - **Phase 8: Fog** — `fogparms` parsing with color/density extraction, fog uniforms in both Metal/GL shaders, exponential fog mix in fragment shader, auto-detected from first fog shader in cache.
  - **Phase 9: Dynamic Lighting** — `DLight` struct + `DLightState` manager (max 4 lights, lifetime decay), world position varying in vertex shader, fragment-based point light accumulation (distance attenuation), wired to weapon fire (muzzle flash) and explosion events. Metal: lights baked into FragU uniform buffer. GL: separate uniform arrays.
  - **Phase 10: Player Character Models** — `player_model.gg` with AnimationCfg parsing (animation.cfg), skin file parsing (.skin format), tag-based multi-part MD3 assembly (lower→tag_torso→upper→tag_head→head). Loads sarge model with per-surface skin textures. Bots rendered as full 3-part character models with idle/stand animations, facing player via yaw. Billboard fallback when models unavailable.
  - **Phase 11: Vertex Deformation** — `DeformType` enum (DeformWave/Bulge/Move/AutoSprite/AutoSprite2), `deformVertexes` directive parsing in shader.gg, `eval_deform_wave()`/`eval_deform_move()` evaluation functions. CPU-side vertex buffer modification deferred (requires dynamic vertex buffer architecture).
  - **Phase 12: Decals/Marks** — `decals.gg` with DecalPool (64 capacity), per-impact decal creation (bullet holes, scorch marks, plasma/railgun marks), tangent computation from surface normal, lifetime decay with 25% fade-out. Rendered with polygon offset and alpha blending.

- [2026-03-15] **4 new compiler diagnostics: NoFieldFound, NotAFunction, BreakOutsideLoop, UnknownDirective** — Added missing semantic checks: (1) `NoFieldFound` for field access on Defined structs with known fields, (2) `NotAFunction` when calling Variable/Const/Static identifiers, (3) `BreakOutsideLoop` / continue outside loop with `loop_depth` tracking, (4) item-level `@attribute` validation (known: derive, tag, should_panic, skip, timeout). Added `struct_fields` and `loop_depth` to TypeChecker struct, `has_any_impl_by_name` to TraitRegistry. `NoMethodFound` and `underivable_trait` deferred (high false positive rate / pass ordering). 4 new test fixtures + integration tests. Files: `typecheck.rs`, `mod.rs`, `traits.rs`.

- [2026-03-15] **Negative test fixtures: 29 new `*_error.gg` fixtures** — Added comprehensive negative test coverage across 6 categories: Type errors (7: type_mismatch_assign/return/arg, wrong_arg_count, non_exhaustive_match, value_out_of_range, non_printable_interpolation), Scope errors (5: undefined_name, duplicate_definition, throw_in_non_throwing, required_after_default, assignment_to_const_nested), Trait errors (5: missing_trait_method, method_signature_mismatch, duplicate_impl, unsatisfied_trait_bound, derive_from_multi_field), Ownership errors (8: use_after_move, move_without_operator, double_move, move_in_loop, borrow_conflict, read_while_mut_captured, write_while_mut_captured, ownership_mismatch), Async errors (3: await_outside_async, spawn_non_future, borrow_across_await), Misc (2: main_throws_non_int, positional_after_named). Total error fixtures: 25→54. Discovered 10 missing compiler diagnostics logged to TODO.md.

- [2026-03-15] **Fix: IIFE (immediately-invoked closure) codegen** — `((int x): x * x)(5)` now works correctly. The `lower_call` function in `calls.rs` fell through to `Operand::Constant(Constant::Unit)` for non-identifier callees. Added `Expr::Closure` handling: lower the closure inline, look up its `__Closure_N__call` function, emit the call with pointer-to-closure-struct + args, return the result. Test fixture: `closure_iife.gg`.

- [2026-03-15] **Fix: User-defined trait with builtin trait name causes "duplicate definition"** — Defining a trait named `Measurable` (or any builtin trait name) caused a spurious "duplicate definition" error. Root cause: `define_with_mutability` in `scope.rs` had `kind != DefKind::Trait` in the builtin-shadowing condition, preventing user traits from overriding builtin trait registrations (which use `Span::dummy()`). Removed that guard. Test fixture: `two_traits_basic.gg`.
- [2026-03-13] **LIR backend: 524→529 A/B tests passing (93.6%)** — Two fixes: (1) **Closure array element read corruption**: `gorget_array_get` returns `void*` to a 16-byte `GorgetClosure`, but `Load { ty: I64 }` read only 8 bytes (fn_ptr), causing SIGBUS when fn_ptr was used as a data pointer in subsequent memcpy. Fixed in `lower.rs`: detect "Callable"/"FnPtr" in `base_type_name` and use `LirType::Struct(GorgetClosure)` for the Load instruction. Fixed httpserver_router, httpserver_before, httpserver_middleware, httpserver_routing, httpserver_router_extended. (2) **Missing cstr coercion for FS functions**: `gorget_file_exists`, `gorget_is_dir`, `gorget_delete_file`, `gorget_mkdir`, `gorget_rmdir`, `gorget_readdir`, `gorget_file_size`, `gorget_path_parent`, `gorget_path_stem`, `gorget_path_absolute`, `gorget_append_file`, `gorget_rename`, `gorget_copy_file` were missing from `takes_cstr_for_str_param`/`needs_null_terminated_cstr` — Str values passed as raw bytes instead of `const char*`. Fixed httpserver_static. Files: `src/lir/lower.rs`, `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.

- [2026-03-13] **LIR backend: 520→524 A/B tests passing** — MoveZero projected places fix: when zeroing a struct field after move, follow GIR type projections to find leaf type's size instead of using parent struct size. Prevents buffer overflow from oversized memset. Files: `src/lir/lower.rs`.

- [2026-03-13] **LIR backend: 517→519 A/B tests passing (91.9%)** — Three fixes: (1) **Post-push zeroing in c_lir**: After `gorget_array_push`/`gorget_map_put`/`gorget_set_add` with collection-typed element args (GorgetArray, GorgetMap, GorgetSet, GorgetString, GorgetClosure), emit `memset(src, 0, sizeof(T))` to prevent double-free from shallow-copy aliasing. Uses `ptr_pointee` tracking to detect collection-typed args. (2) **Post-EnumInit zeroing in LIR lowering**: After `EnumInit` (e.g. `Some(vec)`), zero source locals whose slot type is a resource struct. The old GIR→C backend had this logic in its EnumInit handler but GIR doesn't emit MoveZero for it. (3) **MoveZero struct size**: Fixed to use `c_sizeof_lir_type` for struct types instead of `scalar_size` fallback of 8 bytes (GorgetArray=40, GorgetMap=104). Files: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`, `src/lir/lower.rs`.

- [2026-03-13] **LIR backend: 503→515 A/B tests passing (91.2%)** — Five key fixes: (1) **Callable struct-by-value dispatch**: `__callable_N` closure dispatch passed struct args (Entity, Health) as `void*`, truncating 16-byte structs to 8-byte pointers. Fixed by checking `ptr_pointee` in callable dispatch to use actual struct types and dereference pointers. (2) **gorget_read_file/write_file cstr coercion**: File I/O functions missing from `takes_cstr_for_str_param`; Str was passed as raw bytes instead of `const char*`. (3) **Result unwrap payload alignment**: All Result unwrap code used hardcoded `(char*)ptr + 8` byte offset for payload extraction, which was wrong for Result types where Ok payload is smaller than 8 bytes (e.g., `Result[GorgetSocket, Str]` has `Ok_0` at offset 4, not 8). Fixed by using named struct field access (`->Ok_0`) via `FieldPtr`-style resolution instead of byte offsets. This fixed httpserver, p2p, async socket, and UDP tests. (4) **CallPtr ABI**: Added proper type casting for `Inst::CallPtr` args (ptr-to-aggregate → dereference for by-value passing). (5) Same callable fix for `Inst::CallPtr` path. Files: `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`.

- [2026-03-15] **Phase A: Field move-zeroing for resource-type fields** — When a resource-type field is extracted from a struct via assignment (`Vector[int] v = h.data`), the source field is now zeroed with `MoveZero` to prevent double-free at scope-end drop. Added `field_load_origins: FxHashMap<LocalId, (Place, TypeId)>` to `LoweringContext`, populated in `lower_field_access` for owned and mutable-borrow locals (skips const Ptr). Consumed in `lower_var_decl`, `lower_assign`, method call move-zero sites, and function call move-zero sites. Extended C backend `MoveZero` handler to resolve `Projection::Field(idx)` types from struct TypeDef. Test fixture: `drop_field_move_zero.gg` (4 scenarios). Files: `context.rs`, `exprs/mod.rs`, `stmts/mod.rs`, `stmts/assigns.rs`, `exprs/methods.rs`, `exprs/calls.rs`, `backend/c/mod.rs`.

- [2026-03-15] **Phase B: ParamABI fallback removal** — Removed heuristic fallback in `format_args_with_coercion` (c/mod.rs). ParamABI is now the single source of truth for pointer coercion. Only `gorget_sqlite_*` extern functions were using the fallback path; these now get explicit ABI entries. File: `backend/c/mod.rs`.

- [2026-03-15] **Phase C: OAT const correctness** — Replaced 13 `__typeof__` sites in Option/Result macros with explicit inner types via `resolve_oat_inner_type` helper. Fixed 3 `__typeof__({self_str})` sites in Option combinators (OptionAndThen, OptionFilter, OptionFlatten) using `self_effective_type` helper. Removed Option/Result exemption from `const_borrow_locals` — all borrow locals now get `const` uniformly. File: `backend/c/mod.rs`.

- [2026-03-15] **Fix module-level struct constant initialization in C backend** — Module-level variables of struct types (e.g., `Vec3 PLAYER_MINS = Vec3(-15.0, -24.0, -15.0)`) were declared as `static Vec3 = {0}` and never initialized. Root cause: `eval_static_init()` in `src/ir/lowering/mod.rs` had a hardcoded dispatch table for known types (AtomicInt, Barrier, Dict, Vector, etc.) but fell through to `GlobalInit::Zeroed` for arbitrary struct constructors. Fix: (1) Added generic struct constructor handler in the default match arm — if callee matches type name and all args are compile-time literals, emit C compound literal `(TypeName){arg1, arg2, ...}` as RuntimeCall. (2) Fixed `eval_literal_arg` to handle `UnaryOp::Neg` for negative int/float literals (e.g., `-15.0`). Added `is_literal_arg()` helper. Impact: arena's `PLAYER_MINS`/`PLAYER_MAXS`/`PLAYER_CROUCH_*` now initialized correctly — player no longer falls through the ground due to zero-extents point trace.

- [2026-03-15] **Gorget Arena: restore 263 borrow checker fixes + text V-flip for Metal** — Restored backup arena code had 263 borrow checker errors (backup predated previous ownership fixes). Re-applied all fixes: `GpuContext &ctx`, `GameSounds &gs`, `ParseCursor &cur`, `MenuState &menu` parameter changes; `&` at ~200+ call sites; Vec3 field extraction to float locals; VFS method shadowing workaround; entity_parser `pairs` loop restructure. Also applied Metal text V-flip in `draw_text_atlas` (hud.gg) — Metal textures have top-left V origin vs GL's bottom-left, so font atlas UVs need `1.0 - v` remapping on macOS. Inlined Vec3 values in `trace_player` as workaround for the struct constant init bug (fixed separately in compiler).

- [2026-03-14] **Static analysis: fallible unwrap, const promotion, needless mut borrow, private-in-public** — Four new analyses pushing Gorget past Rust/Swift/Kotlin in static safety. Phase 1: Fallible Unwrap Tracking — warns when `.unwrap()`/`.expect()` is called on `Option[T]`/`Result[T, E]` without prior guard (`is_some()`/`is_ok()` or `match` destructuring). `FallibleState` enum (Unchecked/Checked) tracked per DefId, integrated with branch state save/restore/merge (conservative: unchecked in any branch = unchecked after merge). Phase 2: Immutability Inference — warns when a Copy-type variable is never reassigned (`CouldBeConst`). Tracks `var_reassigned: FxHashSet<DefId>` across Assign/CompoundAssign; emits at function exit for non-`_`-prefixed, non-param, Copy-type variables. Phase 3: Needless Mutable Borrow — warns when `&` param is never mutated (`NeedlessMutableBorrow`). Tracks `mut_param_mutated` via `mark_mut_param_if_applicable()` reusing `find_root_def_id()` pattern. Phase 4: Private-in-Public — errors when `public` function exposes a `private` type in signature (`PrivateTypeInPublicSignature`). AST-level visibility collection, recursive generic type walking (catches `Vector[PrivateType]`), skips builtins. 23 new unit tests (8+6+5+4), 972 unit + 712 integration tests pass.
- [2026-03-15] **LIR backend: 486→492 A/B tests passing** — Fixed: (1) Option[uint8_t] payload offset — `payload_align` in c_lir was 8 for U8/I8/U16/I16 types but actual struct offset is 4 (after int32_t tag); fixed at both unwrap and expect code paths. Fixes: bytes_ops, encoding_basic, encoding_edge, uuid_basic, uuid_props. (2) Enum payload implicit extraction — GIR assigns `_21 = copy _23` from Option/Result-typed source to scalar dest (implicit unwrap); LIR was copying the whole struct instead of extracting field 1 (Some_0/Ok_0). Added `try_enum_payload_extract()` in lower.rs to detect this pattern and emit FieldPtr+Load. Fixes: string_coerce_args.

- [2026-03-14] **Static analysis: fallible unwrap, const promotion, needless mut borrow, private-in-public** — Four new analyses pushing Gorget past Rust/Swift/Kotlin in static safety.

- [2026-03-14] **Static analysis: purity integration + dead code detection** — Four-phase analysis upgrade.

- [2026-03-14] **Purity inference (novel feature)** — Automatic function purity tracking with zero annotations.

- [2026-03-14] **ParamABI extended to all function types** — Phase 1 of ABI cleanup.

- [2026-03-14] **Sentinel-to-Option metadata flag** — Replaced fragile `is_collection_or_string` string matching with `sentinel_to_option_methods`.

- [2026-03-13] **LIR backend: 478→482 A/B tests passing** — Fixed: map_err Str type mismatch, Shared__Vector__double__at return type, regex missing args, nullable cstr/ptr→Option wrapping, task group submit dereference, channel recv timeout Option wrapping.

- [2026-03-14] **Fix const qualifier on borrow locals + remove suppression flags** — Split `Instruction::Borrow | BorrowMut` match in `compute_type_overrides` (C backend) to track immutable borrows separately in `const_borrow_locals` set. Apply `const` qualifier only at declaration emit (not in `type_overrides`) to avoid breaking `__typeof__` in OAT macros. Skip Option/Result types since `__typeof__(*const_ptr)` inherits const and breaks temporary assignments. Removed `-Wno-discarded-qualifiers` (GCC) and `-Wno-ignored-qualifiers` (clang) suppression flags from `main.rs`. Fixed `MoveZero` C backend handler to walk `Deref` projections for correct `sizeof` computation.

- [2026-03-14] **ParamABI threaded to C backend** — Added `fn_param_abis: FxHashMap<String, Vec<ParamABI>>` to `Module` struct, populated from `LoweringContext` at end of `lower_module`. `format_args_with_coercion` now uses `fn_param_abis` as single source of truth for pointer coercion decisions, with heuristic fallback for extern functions.

- [2026-03-14] **Fix generic identity double-free** — `T identity[T](T x) = x` with `T = Vector[int]` caused double-free: callee made shallow copy from `const T*` without zeroing source. Fix: pre-scan generic function bodies for bare Move-type params that are directly returned; upgrade those params from `Borrow → Move` (const ptr → mut ptr). Caller emits `BorrowMut` + `mark_moved` for the arg. Callee emits `MoveZero(*param)` after copying return value. Updated `register_fn_sigs` to register `fn_param_ownerships` for generic functions so callers use correct ABI. Added `move_override_params` to `LoweringContext` for return-statement lowering access.

- [2026-03-14] **Embed stb_image.h for JPEG/PNG/TGA/BMP support in `image_load_rgba_from_memory`** — The `gg.image` runtime previously used `__has_include("stb_image.h")` to detect system stb_image, falling back to a minimal TGA-only loader when not found. This caused JPEG decoding to fail with "unsupported format (no stb_image)" on systems without stb_image.h installed (e.g., the arena project had 24/44 textures missing — all JPEGs). Fix: downloaded stb_image.h v2.30 (public domain, 7988 lines) and bundled it at `src/backend/c/stb_image.h` via `include_str!`, following the same pattern as `sqlite3/sqlite3.c`. The header is emitted directly into the generated C source with STB_IMAGE_IMPLEMENTATION + format-specific defines (STBI_ONLY_PNG/JPEG/TGA/BMP). Removed the `__has_include` fallback and TGA-only loader. All platforms now get full image format support unconditionally. 929 unit tests pass.

- [2026-03-14] **Borrow checker: Phase 1 shared atomicity completions + Phase 2 generic container origin + Phase 3 ParamABI infrastructure** — Three-phase borrow checker improvement. Phase 1: (1a) Compound yield race detection — `expr_contains_yield_point()` recursively checks expression trees for await/blocking calls; `Stmt::Assign` handler detects `x = x + blocking_call()` patterns on with-tracked vars. (1b) Multi-variable invariant messages — `find_with_tracked_in_condition` returns `Vec<String>` instead of `Option<String>`; `WithCheckThenAct` holds `shared_names: Vec<String>` naming all affected shared vars. (1c) Closure capture of with binding — detects closures inside `with` blocks that capture with-tracked bindings (stale-after-yield risk). Phase 2: `compute_ref_type_structs` extended to visit `Item::Enum` alongside `Item::Struct`, so `Result`/`Option` with ref-type variant fields are tracked as reference-containing types. Phase 3: `ParamABI` enum (`ByValue`/`ByPtr`/`ByMutPtr`) defined in `context.rs`; `fn_param_abis` map populated during function signature registration; `lower_call_arg` uses ABI lookup with fallback to old derivation. 7 new unit tests, 936 unit + 714 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-14] **Fix 4 borrow checker false positives in imported modules** — Arena `gg build` reported 4 false "borrows from local variable" errors in pk3.gg, bsp.gg, and collision.gg. Root cause: built-in methods (unwrap, get) aren't in `method_resolutions`, so `compute_method_call_origin` falls through to conservative receiver-origin propagation. For `Result[str, str] r = bytes_to_str(...)`, Result is not in `ref_type_structs` (only structs, not enums), so `r`'s origin isn't tracked in `var_origins`, and `compute_expr_origin(r)` returns `Local(r_def_id)` instead of `Static`. Fix: added `imported_module_depth` counter to `BorrowChecker` — DanglingReturn/UnresolvedBorrowOrigin checks are skipped when inside `Item::Module` wrappers (imported code is validated by its own project's checker). Deeper origin tracking fix deferred to TODO. 929 unit + 714 integration tests pass, arena builds with 0 errors.

- [2026-03-14] **Gorget Arena: fix 83 ownership/copy/VFS errors** — Reduced `gg check` errors on `target/gorget-arena/src/main.gg` from 87 to 4. Fixes: (1) shader.gg — added `&` at 25+ call sites for `ParseCursor &cur` params (`cursor_next`, `cursor_rest_of_line`, `cursor_skip_line`, `parse_stage`, `parse_shader_body`). (2) main.gg — removed `file_exists`/`read_file` from `std.fs` import (shadowed by VFS methods), used `vfs.read_file_str()` for shader loading, extracted `bot_pos` Vec3 to float locals. (3) bot.gg — extracted all Vec3 values used at multiple sites to `float` locals (`bp_x/y/z`, `ip_x/y/z`, `rt_x/y/z`, `ep_x/y/z`, `nt_x/y/z`) and reconstructed fresh Vec3 at each use. (4) entity_parser.gg — restructured inner loop with `entity_done` boolean flag to move `pairs` push outside the loop. (5) collision.gg — replaced `Vec3 clip_normal` with `clip_nx/ny/nz` floats. (6) physics.gg — extracted `position`, `step_up`, `step_end` Vec3 fields to float locals. (7) weapon.gg — extracted `new_pos` to `np_x/y/z` floats. (8) game_state.gg — extracted `fire_pos`, `dest`/`vel`, `bot_pos` to float locals. (9) console.gg — used `!trimmed` move sigil. Remaining 4 errors are borrow checker false positives in pk3.gg/bsp.gg/collision.gg.

- [2026-03-14] **LIR backend: fix GorgetArray sizeof (341→442 A/B tests)** — `c_sizeof_lir_type` for `Struct(sid)` was computing size from LIR field list, but `GorgetArray` in LIR omits the `alloc` pointer field (4 fields = 32 bytes) while the C runtime struct has 5 fields = 40 bytes. This caused dict constructors for `Dict[str, TomlValue]` and similar types containing `GorgetArray` fields to use val_size=200 instead of the correct 208, corrupting stored values. Fix: in `c_sizeof_lir_type`, check the struct name against a runtime size table (GorgetArray→40, GorgetMap/GorgetSet→104, GorgetString→32, Str→16) before falling back to field-count computation. 442/565 A/B tests now pass (78.2%, up from 341/60.4%).

- [2026-03-14] **LIR backend: Phase 2 progress (422→430)** — Fixed SizeOf for struct types (use c_sizeof_lir_type instead of scalar_size), gorget_str_to_cstr for parse functions (json_parse, json_pretty, json_edge_cases), gorget_regex_find missing start_offset arg injection, InlineC dst value type inference from block parameters, ordered set constructor (Set[T] now uses gorget_ordered_set_new instead of gorget_set_new), __gorget_map_grow runtime bug (unconditionally allocated order array for unordered HashMaps, causing missing elements after grow). 430/565 A/B tests now pass (76.1%).

- [2026-03-14] **Borrow checker: enable error reporting in imported modules** — Removed `imported_module_depth` suppression: borrow checker now reports errors in imported library code. Three root causes fixed: (RC1) Type checker now recurses into `Item::Module` via `register_signatures_recursive` + `check_items_recursive_tc`, populating `expr_types`/`method_resolutions` for imported code (errors discarded, type info kept). (RC2) Added `MatchBinding` variant to `BorrowOrigin` with per-binding `binding_def_id` (for independent move tracking) and `is_ref` flag (only callable bindings propagate scrutinee lifetime — Gorget match destructuring always moves, so str/struct bindings own their data). (RC3) `check_spawn_args` now skips callable-type identifiers and value-type identifiers via combined type check; field access args without type info skipped to avoid false positives. Library fixes: math3d.gg `Plane.from_points` (use-after-move of `n`), jsonpath.gg + query.gg (`current = !next` move annotation), self-host type checker (6 `!ptypes` moves, `!new_params` reorder, `wexpr` copy elimination). 929 unit + 714 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-14] **LIR backend: Phase 2 progress (397→422)** — Fixed enum variant field offset for Ptr-wrapped types, inlined Vector/Dict/Set higher-order methods (including `count`), added guard slot type overrides, float/str sort comparators, collection void param indices in Option-wrapping path, val_c_type_override for runtime struct returns, c_sizeof_with_structs for collections, `unwrap_error` rename compat, `gorget_array_zip` inline, Printf Str decomposition via `%.*s`, `find()` Option field offset fix (use struct field name instead of `sizeof(int32_t)` to respect alignment padding), Mutex/RWLock self-by-value (not by-ptr) for pointer-typed concurrency objects. 422/565 A/B tests now pass (74.7%).

- [2026-03-14] **Borrow checker: site 4 recursion into imported modules** — All 4 borrow-check iteration sites now recurse into `Item::Module` wrappers. Site 4 (main `check_module` loop) uses `check_items_recursive` which tracks `imported_module_depth` — errors are suppressed for library code (analyzed for metadata but not reported) to avoid false positives from (1) `SpawnWithBorrowedRef` on callable handler params in httpserver spawn calls, (2) `DanglingReturn` on match bindings with missing scrutinee origin. Also: `compute_call_origin` returns `Static` for analyzed functions with bodies where no borrows were found (`has_body` guard prevents bodyless function misclassification). 929 unit + 714 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-14] **Arena Metal crash fix: find()/index_of() Option wrapping restricted to collections** — Root cause: GIR lowering unconditionally wrapped ALL `.find()` method returns (except Regex) in `Option[int]`, including user-defined methods like `TextureManager::find()`. When user `find()` returned -1 (not found), the Option wrapping checked `>= 0` → true (for the raw -1), then read `.data.Some._0` which was uninitialized stack garbage. This caused all 44 BSP texture lookups to appear "cached" with the same stale pointer, crashing Metal on `setFragmentTexture:`. Fix: added `is_collection_or_string` guard at 3 sites in `src/ir/lowering/exprs/methods.rs` — Option wrapping now only applies to Vector/Dict/Set/Str/Bytes types. Also added Metal runtime guard for invalid texture handles (`< 0x10000`). 929 unit + 714 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-14] **Borrow checker: partial module recursion + 3 root-cause fixes** — (1) `compute_all_return_borrows` (site 3) now recurses into `Item::Module` wrappers via `all_spanned_items`, so imported functions get `return_borrows_from` metadata for cross-module origin tracking. (2) `compute_call_origin` returns `Static` for `DefKind::Struct` constructors — generic collection constructors (Dict, Vector, Set) no longer produce `Unknown` origin. (3) `check_spawn_args` skips value-type non-identifier args (int, float, bool) via `expr_types` lookup — field accesses like `self.timeout_ms` no longer trigger `SpawnWithBorrowedRef`. (4) httpserver.gg: 5 functions changed `Socket conn`/`TlsSocket conn` → `!conn` (move semantics); 3 internal call sites + 6 fixture call sites updated to `!client`. Site 4 (full check) NOT enabled due to span collisions (no file ID in Span type). 929 unit + 714 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-13] **Borrow checker: method-call mutation on bare params + struct metadata module recursion** — Added detection of `&self` method calls on bare (immutable) struct parameters via `method_resolutions` + `function_info` lookup in the `Expr::MethodCall` handler. Added `all_spanned_items` helper that recursively descends into `Item::Module` wrappers; applied to struct metadata passes (compute_ref_type_structs, compute_struct_field_ref_flags) so imported module structs are analyzed. 4 new unit tests (method-call rejected/allowed, closure capture, match arm). New integration test fixture `bare_param_method_error.gg`. 929 unit + 712 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-13] **Borrow checker: reject mutation of bare struct params** — Added `param_ownership` field to `DefInfo`, populated during resolver pass. Borrow checker now rejects: (1) field/index assignment through bare non-Copy params, (2) compound assignment through bare params, (3) creating `&` (mutable borrow) from bare params, (4) passing bare params with `&` at call sites. New error: `MutationOfBareParam`. Added 7 unit tests and 2 integration tests (`bare_param_immutable`, `bare_param_mutation_error`). Fixed `httpserver_concurrent.gg` to use `!conn` (move). 925 unit + 711 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-13] **String interning at lex time (Phase 1: Token layer)** — Added `src/intern.rs` with `Symbol` type (u32 handle), thread-local `Interner`, and ergonomic API (`intern()`, `Symbol::as_str()`, `Display`, `PartialEq<str>`). Changed `Token::Identifier(String)` → `Token::Identifier(Symbol)` — all identifiers are now interned at lex time, deduplicating allocations. Parser bridge resolves Symbol→String for AST (AST migration deferred to Phase 2). Removed unused `src/semantic/intern.rs`. 918 unit + 709 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-13] **Parser error recovery synchronization** — Added recovery loops inside struct field, enum variant, trait method, equip method, and match arm parsing so the parser reports multiple errors instead of bailing at the first one. Added `synchronize_to_top_level()` for indentation-aware recovery, `at_error_limit()` (MAX_ERRORS=10) to prevent cascading, and 4 new recovery tests. 917 unit + 709 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-13] **SoA token storage in parser** — Split `Parser`'s `Vec<Spanned<Token>>` into parallel `Vec<Token>` + `Vec<Span>` for better cache locality on the hot path (peek/peek_ahead only touch token kinds). 7 access sites updated.

- [2026-03-13] **Parser fuzzing harness** — Added `fuzz/` with cargo-fuzz targets for lexer, parser, and formatter roundtrip. Seeded from 663 test fixtures.

- [2026-03-13] **Generic type deduplication in TypeTable** — Added `intern_generic()` with `generic_cache: FxHashMap<(DefId, Vec<TypeId>), TypeId>` to deduplicate identical generic instantiations (e.g., every `Vector[int]` now shares a single TypeId). Replaced `find_generic()` (dead code) and ~38 `insert(ResolvedType::Generic(...))` call sites across typecheck.rs, resolve.rs, and types.rs.

- [2026-03-13] **"Did you mean?" suggestions for undefined names** — Added Levenshtein edit-distance-based name suggestions to all 3 `UndefinedName` error emission sites in resolve.rs. New helpers: `edit_distance()`, `visible_names()`, `suggest_name()` in scope.rs. Extended `UndefinedName` variant with `suggestion: Option<String>`. Threshold: edit distance ≤ max(2, len×2/5), skipping single-char names.

- [2026-03-13] **Gorget Arena: GL backend compiles (0 errors)** — Fixed 20 GL/Linux C compilation errors: GL string functions (gl_get_string, gl_get_shader_info_log, gl_get_program_info_log) return `str` not `String`; added gl_buffer_data/gl_tex_image_2d/gl_uniform_matrix4fv/gl_load_matrix to `takes_array_ptr_args`; made GorgetGLContext an int64_t opaque handle matching Metal pattern.

- [2026-03-13] **Borrow pass-through for standalone & cross-module functions** — Registered standalone function signatures in `fn_sigs` (was missing, only equip methods and closures). Added `fn_param_ownerships` check in `lower_call_arg` to detect explicit `&` params, enabling pointer forwarding even when `is_resource_type` returns false (cross-module structs with Vector fields). Fixed infinite loop in shader parser where `cursor_next(cur)` copied `ParseCursor` via deref instead of forwarding the pointer.

- [2026-03-13] **LIR Phase 2 progress: 341/565 A/B tests passing (60.4%), 35 build failures, 189 output mismatches** — Seven more fixes: (1) **Drop elaboration** — `Instruction::Drop`/`DropIfAlive` now lowers to actual drop calls instead of Nop. `Trivial(fn_name)` drops emit `CallExtern/Call` to the drop function with `SlotAddr` (or `SlotLoad` for `free()`). Box trait objects use `free(val.data)` via `FieldPtr`+`Load`. `Custom` drops also wired. `Recursive` still Nop. (2) **Guard/ReadGuard/WriteGuard wrapper support** — `parse_guard_method`, `emit_guard_wrapper`, `emit_wrapper_typedef` for Guard types. Guard__T__drop→gorget_guard_release, get/set/get_ptr. Added to runtime detection, extern skip, unmonomorphized wrapper check. (3) **Box trait vtable dispatch parameters** — `emit_box_wrapper` and VTable struct field declarations now look up extern declarations to get full parameter lists. Fixes `Box__Serializer__field(self, name, idx)` etc. (4) **Early Box__* typedefs** — Box types referenced in struct fields now get `typedef void*` before struct definitions, preventing "unknown type name 'Box__Expr'" errors. (5) **Collection helper element type resolution** — `elem_type_to_c_with_sn` resolves struct element types (like `Option__int64_t`) to their C names (`__lir_s11`) via orig_to_c map. (6) **Unmonomorphized wrapper typedef skip** — `emit_monomorphized_typedefs` now skips `Guard__T`, `ReadGuard__T`, `WriteGuard__T` etc. (7) **SYNC_RUNTIME detection** — ReadGuard__/WriteGuard__ prefixes trigger sync runtime inclusion.

- [2026-03-13] **LIR Phase 2 progress: 334/565 A/B tests passing (59.1%), 49 build failures, 182 output mismatches** — Six more fixes: (1) **Shared__Vector at/set_at/slen** — `emit_shared_wrapper` now uses `gorget_shared_array_get/set/len` for Shared[Vector[T]] methods instead of `gorget_shared_get_ptr` with array indexing. Fixed 8 tensor + 1 shared_vector_elem fixtures. (2) **FnRef global init** — `lower_global_init` now looks up FuncId from func_index for `ir::GlobalInit::FnRef`, instead of zeroing (preserves vtable function references for DCE). (3) **VTable global preservation** — globals with FuncAddr references kept alive by dead global elimination (vtable constants referenced indirectly through pointer casts). Global init value emission refactored to avoid `= ` prefix in struct field positions. (4) **Trait vtable dispatch return types** — `find_trait_method_return_type` now checks extern declarations first (not just function bodies), since trait impl functions may be present as externs when impl bodies were DCE'd. Fixed print_trait_object, measurable_trait build errors. (5) **Vector constructor calls** — `Vector__int64_t(cap)` mapped to `gorget_array_with_capacity(sizeof(int64_t), cap)` via `emit_collection_constructor`. Also handles `Dict__K__V` and `Set__T` constructors. Fixed vector_capacity. (6) **Unmonomorphized wrapper skip** — `is_unmonomorphized_wrapper` prevents emission of `Shared__Vector__T` etc. typedefs/wrappers with bare type variable `T`.

- [2026-03-13] **LIR Phase 2 progress: 332/565 A/B tests passing (58.8%), 44 CC errors remaining** — Seven categories of fixes: (1) **unwrap_or reverse type fixup** — when dst is concrete struct (Str) but default is Ptr, cast the default via `*Type*&val`. Fixed httpserver_body_parsers, httpserver_query_string. (2) **Signal runtime detection** — `gorget_signal_*` and `gorget_getpid` prefixes trigger PROCESS_SPAWN_RUNTIME inclusion. Fixed 8 httpserver + signal_basic builds. (3) **Thread spawn helpers** — added `ThreadSpawnedFn` to LirModule, lowering from GIR `thread_spawned_fns`, and `emit_thread_helpers()` in c_lir generating Thread__T typedefs + `__gorget_thread_spawn_*` + join methods. Skip thread-generated names from extern/function forward declarations. Fixed thread_basic, thread_atomic, thread_mutex, sync_condvar. (4) **Dead function elimination roots** — thread-spawned function names added as roots in `find_live_functions()` to prevent DCE from removing functions only called from generated thread entry helpers. (5) **GorgetClosure from void*** — when SlotStore target is GorgetClosure but value is non-struct (Ptr/Void/I64), emit memcpy instead of direct assignment. Fixed 7 httpserver fixtures. (6) **Test harness timeouts** — added 30s per-fixture timeout in lir_ab.rs to prevent hangs from blocking tests. (7) **THREAD_RUNTIME detection** — detect `gorget_current_thread_id` and `__gorget_thread_spawn_*` prefixes + non-empty `thread_spawned_fns`.

- [2026-03-13] **Static str literal initializer fix** — `static str x = "hello"` now generates `gorget_str_from_literal(...)` in `__gorget_init_globals()` instead of `{0}`. Fixed Metal shader sources being empty at runtime.

- [2026-03-13] **Frontend code review round 3: code smells, workarounds, unnecessary special cases** — (1) `Token::Error(String)` → unit variant `Token::Error` — removed 7 ad-hoc string constructions; error details already live in `Lexer::errors`. Removed unused `name` parameter from `parse_int_literal`. (2) Extracted `parse_body_or_expr()` helper for rethrow/catch block-or-expr pattern, and `parse_arm_body()` for match arm block-or-expr pattern (4 sites deduplicated). (3) Added `Blocking` keyword — `spawn blocking` now uses `match_keyword(Keyword::Blocking)` instead of string comparison. `On` kept as contextual identifier (used in stdlib `dataframe.gg`). (4) Extracted `string_prefix_kind()` + `is_string_prefix()` helpers — centralizes r/b/c/f prefix detection, replacing duplicated guard conditions in tokenizer dispatch and scanner segmentation loop. (5) Extracted `parse_one_quoted_char()` — shared escape/literal char parsing used by both `scan_char_literal` and `scan_byte_literal`. (6) Extracted `parse_simple_stmt()` for `continue`/`pass` keyword-only statements.

- [2026-03-12] **Frontend code review round 2** — (1) Removed dead `!matches!(Token::EqEq)` condition in `parse_call_arg`. (2) Escape span consistency: use `backslash_pos` in catch-all branch. (3) Removed redundant `i = pos + 1` in `scan_char_literal`. (4) Improved error message: "expected string" → "expected quote after string prefix". (5) Added `debug_assert!(start <= end)` to `Span::new`. (6) Descriptive `unreachable!()` messages in `parse_postfix` and `parse_const_item`. (7) Documented `try_parse` error retention design.
- [2026-03-12] **LIR Phase 2 progress: 512→546 build pass (84 CC errors, down from 118)** — Seven categories of fixes: (1) **runtime_arg_by_ptr()** — generalized struct-by-pointer arg handling. `gorget_crypto_*` functions, `gorget_bytes_concat`, `gorget_udp_sendto` now correctly pass GorgetArray/struct args by pointer. Applied in both normal call and last_error result-wrapping paths. (2) **takes_cstr_for_str_param()** — expanded Str→`const char*` coercion to cover `gorget_udp_bind`, `gorget_socket_connect`, `gorget_tls_connect`, `gorget_server_socket_bind`, `gorget_path_*`, `gorget_file_open`, `gorget_regex_compile`, `gorget_exec*`. Fixed parse_int/parse_float arg index (was 1, should be 0). (3) **unwrap_or type mismatch** — void*/double ternary: when dst is Ptr but default arg is concrete (e.g. double), emit temp variable with correct type + memcpy. (4) **gorget_str_join arg 2** — `str_fn_non_str_arg()` prevents Str wrapping for non-Str args of `gorget_str_*` functions. (5) **Newtype constructors** — detect extern name matching struct name, emit compound literal `(StructType){ ._0 = arg }` instead of function call. Also fix LIR lowering to set struct return type for newtype constructors. (6) **last_error path arg handling** — unified with `takes_cstr_for_str_param` and `runtime_arg_by_ptr` instead of ad-hoc checks. All 13 p2p fixtures now pass.


- [2026-03-12] **LIR Phase 2 progress: 198→241 A/B tests passing (47.9%)** — Six categories of fixes: (1) **Polymorphic extern fix** — `is_polymorphic_extern()` returns None from `infer_inst_type()` for option/result unwrap, expect, and combinator externs that are called with different return types. Prevents type poisoning from merged extern declarations. (2) **Unwrap/expect payload alignment** — default alignment changed from 4 to 8 (correct for int64_t, Ptr, Struct payloads). (3) **Inline combinator expansion** — Option/Result map/filter/and_then/or_else now inlined at each call site using GCC statement expressions instead of static helpers. Fixes cross-type map (`Option[int].map(→str)` producing `Option[str]`). Slot declarations moved after type fix-up passes. (4) **Channel/Shared/Weak/Atomic monomorphized wrappers** — `emit_monomorphized_typedefs()` scans structs, struct_names, spawned_fns, and CallExtern instructions. Emits typedefs (`typedef GorgetChannel* Channel__int64_t;` etc.) and inline wrapper functions that adapt runtime calling conventions (void* ↔ typed values). Undid lowering's `map_monomorphized_to_runtime()` for Channel/Shared/Weak methods. (5) **Vector/Dict/Set/HashMap/HashSet monomorphized typedefs** — `typedef GorgetArray Vector__int64_t;` etc. for element types used in Shared wrappers. (6) **Runtime detection** — sync/async/channel runtime modules now also trigger on monomorphized names (Channel__, Shared__, Weak__, AtomicInt/AtomicBool structs).

- [2026-03-12] **Fix: channel double-indirection bugs in C backend** — Channel types are typedef'd to `GorgetChannel*` (already a pointer), so frame-stored `Channel__T*` locals are double-pointers. Fixed 4 issues: (1) poll-path ChannelSend/ChannelRecv emit now dereferences channel arg with `(*...)`. (2) `format_args_with_coercion` auto-deref limited to Channel-type arg 0 only (was breaking Guard/Shared methods). (3) Channel drop wrapper changed to take `self*` (matching `emit_drop_code`'s `&place_str` pattern). (4) Poll-context generic Call handler now applies Channel-specific auto-deref for arg 0. Also added `__gorget_select_yield()` to all 4 scheduler backends and select loop header to prevent spin-loop starvation. Result: 709/709 integration tests pass (was 708/709 with `async_channel_poll` failing).

- [2026-03-12] **Fix: deduplicate GIR globals from multi-file merges** — Module-level variables with the same name from different source files (e.g., GPU_BLEND_ZERO defined in gpu_backend.gg, shader.gg, backend.gg) caused GIR validation failure. Fixed by skipping duplicates during static declaration lowering. gorget-arena now builds end-to-end (C generation succeeds).

- [2026-03-12] **Fix: flatten_meta_ifs skips Item::Module wrappers** — `flatten_meta_ifs()` in `meta.rs` only processed top-level items, not items inside `Item::Module` wrappers from `merge_modules()`. Functions defined inside `meta if` blocks in imported modules were never flattened, so their `FunctionInfo` was never registered — the borrow checker silently skipped ownership checks for calls to those functions. Fixed by recursing into `Item::Module` nodes during meta-if flattening.

- [2026-03-12] **LIR Phase 2 progress: 166→198 A/B tests passing (35%)** — Six categories of fixes: (1) Printf format fix — `fix_printf_format()` helper replaces `%lld` with `%f` for F32/F64 args in printf/fprintf calls. (2) GorgetRange struct — removed from RUNTIME_STRUCTS (not defined in runtime), now generated by LIR like other user structs (7 tests). (3) Conditional runtime modules — scan CallExtern inside function bodies for runtime module needs; added BYTES, REGEX, CRYPTO, SOCKET, SERVER_SOCKET, UDP_SOCKET, TLS, PROCESS, PROCESS_SPAWN, THREAD, TRACE, REACTOR, BLOCKING_POOL, TASK_GROUP, TASK_COMMON, SCHEDULER runtimes. (4) Void value declarations — void-typed and None-typed values/slots now declared as `void*` instead of commented out, fixing 17 `__v0 undeclared` errors. (5) `__gorget_box_alloc_*` generation — scan externs for box alloc functions, generate `static inline void* fname(Type val) { malloc+store+return }`. (6) SlotStore Ptr→Str/GorgetString wrapping — introduced `cstr_vals` bitmap tracking values from `is_cstr_returning_fn()` (gorget_int_to_str etc.); ONLY these get wrapped with gorget_str_from_literal/gorget_string_new. Other Ptr values (SlotAddr, FieldPtr) fall through to aggregate memcpy handler, fixing ~30 wrong-output tests where slot addresses were incorrectly cast to `const char*`.

- [2026-03-11] **Fix: compound index assign (`obj[i] += val`)** — `lower_compound_assign()` in `src/ir/lowering/stmts/assigns.rs` had no handler for `Expr::Index` targets, silently producing no code. Added full handler: reads current value via `index_load` (built-in collections) or getter method (custom types), computes the binary op (including string concat and operator overloads), writes back via `Vector__T__set` / `Dict__K__V__put` / custom setter. Handles nested field access (`self.vec[i] += val`) by copying the field to a temp local for the read (so the C backend can determine collection type) while using the original field Place for the write. Supports all compound ops: `+=`, `-=`, `*=`, `/=`, `%=`, bitwise, wrapping.

- [2026-03-11] **Fix: augmented assignment on struct fields (`self.field += 1`)** — `lower_compound_assign()` in `src/ir/lowering/stmts/assigns.rs` only handled `Expr::Identifier` targets, silently producing no code for `Expr::FieldAccess`. Added FieldAccess handler that resolves the field to a Place via `try_resolve_field_place`, reads the current value, computes the binary op (including string concat and operator overloads), and writes back. Supports `+=`, `-=`, `*=`, `/=`, `%=`, `<<=`, `>>=`, bitwise ops on struct fields through `&self` and on local struct copies.

- [2026-03-11] **Fix: Dict/Vector index assign on struct fields (`self.dict[key] = val`)** — `lower_index_assign()` in `assigns.rs` called `lower_expr()` on the object, which for `FieldAccess` targets copied the collection struct to a temporary. After `gorget_map_put`, the copy's updated metadata (count, order_len) and reallocated pointers (after hash table resize) were discarded. Fixed by resolving FieldAccess objects via `try_resolve_field_place` to get a Place pointing to the original field, and using the resolved field type for dispatch (since `infer_operand_type_full` doesn't walk projections).

- [2026-03-11] **LIR backend: concurrency type mappings (Mutex/Guard/Shared/Channel/RWLock)** — Added monomorphized function mappings for all concurrency types to `map_monomorphized_to_runtime()` and `runtime_extern_sig()` in `src/lir/lower.rs`. Opaque pointer types (Mutex, Shared, Weak, Channel, RWLock) skip LIR struct creation so they lower to `Ptr`. Guard types (Guard, ReadGuard, WriteGuard) get fixed `{owner: Ptr, ptr: Ptr}` layout. Added sizeof synthesis for constructors (`gorget_mutex_new`, `gorget_shared_new`, `gorget_rwlock_new`, `gorget_channel_new`) and guard_set. Struct-return functions (`gorget_mutex_lock`, `gorget_rwlock_read/write`) rewritten to `_to` output-pointer variants. Guard get/get_ptr inlined as FieldPtr+Load to preserve concrete inner type. Added C runtime stubs: `gorget_guard_get/set/get_ptr`, `gorget_mutex_lock_to`, `gorget_rwlock_read_to/write_to`, `gorget_read_guard_get/get_ptr`, `gorget_write_guard_get/set/get_ptr`, `__gorget_push/pop_allocator`. c_lir backend conditionally includes SYNC/ASYNC/CHANNEL/SHARED/MUTEX runtime blocks. Refactored duplicate Call/CallExtern sizeof synthesis into shared `emit_extern_call()` method. Verified: mutex_basic produces correct output through LIR; all 913 unit tests and 76 concurrency integration tests pass.

Defined `Backend` trait in `src/backend/mod.rs` with `CodegenOutput` struct. Implemented `CLirBackend` in `src/backend/c_lir/mod.rs`. LIR build path uses the trait. Also: applied `map_monomorphized_to_runtime` to `CallExtern` targets (was only on `Call` path), added collection sizeof argument synthesis for constructors (`gorget_array_new(sizeof(T))`), `gorget_array_contains` elem_size arg, `collection_self_by_ptr()` for runtime functions taking self via pointer, mapped `GorgetArray__`/`GorgetMap__`/`GorgetSet__` prefixes. - [2026-03-11] **LIR Phase 9: 136→139 A/B tests passing** — three fixes to LIR lowering and C backend: (1) Enum null variant tag — `Constant::Null` assigned to enum-typed locals now emits proper tag ordinal (e.g. None=tag 1) via FieldPtr+Store instead of NullPtr→memset(0) which set tag=0 (Some). Fixes match_option_result, trait_default_meta. (2) `resolve_struct_id` Ptr unwrapping — recursively unwrap `GirType::Ptr`/`MutPtr` to find inner `GirType::Named` for struct ID resolution. Previously fell back to StructId(0)=Str for pointer-typed method params, causing wrong field access casts and void-parameter errors in ~40 fixtures. (3) EnumFieldLoad pointer dereference — when the base local's effective type is a pointer (e.g. `self` in equip methods), load the pointer value before emitting FieldPtr. Fixes derive display showing Blue(0) instead of Blue(42).

- [2026-03-11] **LIR Phase 8: 117→136 A/B tests passing** — seven fixes to the LIR C backend and lowering: (1) Collection void* return dereference — `gorget_array_get`/`gorget_map_get` return `void*`; now emit `*(Type*)gorget_array_get(...)` to dereference to concrete element type. (2) Collection void* param wrapping — `gorget_array_push`/`set`/`insert`/`map_put` take `void*` element pointers; now emit `&(Type){value}` compound literals for concrete args. (3) Printf-like functions — `gorget_string_format`, `snprintf`, `sprintf` now decompose Str args into `(int)len,(char*)data` pairs and wrap bool args as `val ? "true" : "false"`. (4) Sleep rewrite — `sleep(x)` → `gorget_sleep_ms((int64_t)(x * 1000))`, skip extern decl for `sleep`/`mkdir`/`rename`. (5) Void struct fields — `LirType::Void` fields in structs emit `uint8_t` placeholder instead of invalid `void`. (6) String literal → GorgetString slot — `gorget_string_new(lit)` instead of memcpy-from-short-literal buffer overflow. (7) NullPtr → aggregate slot — `memset(&slot, 0, sizeof(type))` instead of memcpy-from-NULL segfault (fixes None/Option initialization). New fixtures: assert_return_msg, char_methods2, field_access, generic_method_chain, generic_struct_methods, is_bindings, lifetime_groups, lifetime_reassign, main_throws, on_error_rethrow, ownership_calls, ownership_keywords, rethrow_bare, rethrow_basic, str_byte_slice, str_codepoint_len, string_format, string_methods3, string_stdlib.

- [2026-03-11] **LIR Phase 7: 105→117 A/B tests passing** — five systemic fixes: (1) Runtime extern signature table — `gorget_str_*` and `gorget_string_*` functions now have canonical parameter types (Str vs GorgetString), preventing call-site inference from corrupting extern declarations; (2) GorgetString→Str coercion in CallExtern — when a `gorget_str_*` function receives a GorgetString-typed arg, emit `(Str){.data=v.data, .len=v.len}` inline coercion; (3) `fprintf_stderr` → `fprintf(stderr, ...)` rewrite — was emitting `fprintf_stderr()` which is undefined in C; (4) `time()` → `time(NULL)` — C requires NULL argument; (5) Void-returning CallExtern — skip dst assignment and store for functions like `gorget_panic` that return void. Also: global variable lowering (GlobalRef→GlobalAddr+Load, GlobalRefPtr→GlobalAddr, GlobalAssign→GlobalAddr+Store). New fixtures: assert_fails, drop_move_zero, lifetime_method/struct, math_constants/trig, on_error_basic/inline, ownership_showcase, print_builtin, static_mutation, struct_string_coerce.

- [2026-03-11] **LIR Phase 6: 74→105 A/B tests passing** — three systemic fixes: (1) `Inst::Store` memcpy sizeof — track pointee types for Ptr values via `SlotAddr`/`FieldPtr`, use correct `sizeof(struct_type)` instead of `sizeof(*(void*))` = 1 byte; (2) `Inst::Cmp` string comparison — detect Str-typed operands and emit `gorget_str_eq`/`gorget_str_cmp` instead of raw pointer comparison; (3) SSA construction `patch_terminators` — fix HashMap non-deterministic iteration causing block param args in wrong order, plus recursive reaching definition lookup for slots not accessed in back-edge blocks. Added `-lm` to test harness. New fixtures: tuples, chars, pattern_destructure, string_concat, string_methods, named_args, loops_advanced, enum_nullary_bare, trait_defaults, trait_inheritance, via_delegation, try_from_trait, option_assign, result_propagation, result_str_concat, assert_* (8), catch_basic, async_* (7).

H2: added 2 missing Option methods (or, filter). H3: added 3 missing Result methods (unwrap_err, map_err, or). H4: documented `meta match` (§19.27) — compile-time pattern matching with examples. H5: documented `meta while` (§19.28) — compile-time conditional guard with examples. M16: noted associated types are parsed but not validated. M17: documented LIFO drop ordering in Drop trait section. M18: added "not yet fully implemented" status note for fixed-size arrays/slices.
