Updated ~50 access sites across `src/ir/lowering/mod.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/generics.rs`, `src/backend/c/mod.rs`, `src/sim/mod.rs`, `src/sim/dispatch.rs` to use `module.runtime.field`. 730 unit + 557 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-07] **GIR optimization passes — constant folding, branch folding, drop elision**: Added three new GIR optimization passes to `src/ir/transforms/optimize.rs`: (1) Constant folding — evaluates BinOp/UnOp/Cmp instructions with constant operands at compile time (i64, f64, bool arithmetic and comparisons), replacing them with Assign of the result. Handles overflow safely (checked ops bail out, wrapping ops fold). (2) Constant branch folding — replaces Branch terminators with constant Bool conditions by Jump, and Switch terminators with constant I64 values by Jump to the matching case. Combined with dead block elimination, this removes unreachable code. (3) Drop elision — removes DropIfAlive instructions for locals that were MoveZero'd earlier in the SAME basic block (no cross-block elision to avoid unsound removal on conditional move paths). Reassignment between MoveZero and DropIfAlive re-validates the local. Copy propagation attempted but reverted — requires SSA form for soundness (pointer aliasing through BorrowMut makes non-SSA analysis unsound). 13 new unit tests. 730 unit + 557 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **GIR optimization passes — dead block & unused local elimination**: Added `src/ir/transforms/optimize.rs` with two backend-agnostic GIR optimization passes: (1) Dead block elimination — BFS from entry block, removes unreachable BBs, remaps all terminator block IDs. (2) Unused local elimination — scans all instructions/terminators for referenced locals, removes unreferenced ones (skipping _0 and params), remaps all local IDs. InlineC functions conservatively keep all locals. `optimize_module()` runs both passes on all functions after lowering, before backend emission. 717 unit + 557 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`std.alloc` arena checkpoint/restore**: Added `ArenaCheckpoint` value type and `Arena.checkpoint()`/`Arena.restore(cp)` methods. `checkpoint()` captures the current block pointer + used offset; `restore(cp)` frees any overflow blocks allocated after the checkpoint and resets the position. C runtime: `GorgetArenaCheckpoint` struct with `gorget_arena_checkpoint()`/`gorget_arena_restore()`. Simulator dispatch added. Type mapping in codegen (`GorgetArenaCheckpoint`), method name mapping (`Arena__checkpoint`/`Arena__restore`). 1 new fixture: `arena_checkpoint.gg`. 717 unit + 557 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Coroutine codegen fixes (3 of 4 limitations resolved)**: (1) `Constant::Str` args in coroutine `Call` instructions now wrapped in `gorget_str_from_literal()` via `fmt_args_poll_gorget()` — coroutines can now pass string literals to Gorget functions expecting `Str`. (2) `IndexLoad` in poll context now uses type-aware dispatch: `GorgetArray`/`Vector__*` → `gorget_array_get` cast, `Str` → `gorget_str_index`/`gorget_str_slice`, `Dict`/`HashMap` → `gorget_map_get`. (3) `CallExtern` for printf in poll context now expands `Str` args to `(int)len, data` via `fmt_printf_args_poll()`. (4) Multiple awaits per basic block now supported — `coroutine_state_ids()` assigns 1+N states per BB with N awaits, `emit_coroutine()` generates proper state machine with per-await yield/resume pairs. Extracted `emit_await_result_call()` helper. 2 new fixtures: `spawn_multi_await.gg`, `spawn_coroutine_str_args.gg`. 717 unit + 556 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Multiple scheduler backends**: Added compile-time selectable scheduler backends via `directive scheduler=pool|thread|inline|single` and `--scheduler=X` CLI flag. `SchedulerMode` enum in IR module, parsed from directives and CLI flags with CLI override. Refactored `EXECUTOR_RUNTIME` into `TASK_COMMON` (shared GorgetTask struct) + 4 backend runtimes: `SCHEDULER_POOL_RUNTIME` (M:N work-stealing, default), `SCHEDULER_THREAD_RUNTIME` (1:1 OS thread per spawn), `SCHEDULER_INLINE_RUNTIME` (synchronous on caller), `SCHEDULER_SINGLE_RUNTIME` (N:1 cooperative event loop). Unified abstraction macros `GORGET_SCHEDULER_SUBMIT`/`GORGET_SCHEDULER_WAIT` replace 6 hardcoded call sites in codegen. TaskGroup.join now uses `GORGET_SCHEDULER_WAIT` for cross-backend compatibility. Semantic analysis validates directive values. 3 new fixtures: `scheduler_thread.gg`, `scheduler_inline.gg`, `scheduler_single.gg`. 717 unit + 553 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Work-stealing executor + concurrency stress tests**: Fixed thread-pool starvation deadlock in the M:N executor. When tasks blocked waiting for child tasks (TaskGroup.join, Task.await, Task drop), they consumed worker threads, preventing queued child tasks from executing — causing deadlocks with nested spawn patterns. Fix: added `__gorget_run_task_inline()` and `__gorget_try_run_one()` work-stealing helpers to the C runtime. All blocking-wait sites (TaskGroup.join, spawn_drop, gorget_await) now work-steal from the executor queue instead of sleeping on condvar. Refactored `__gorget_worker` to use the shared inline-execution helper. Added 11 stress test fixtures: `stress_spawn_fan_out` (200 parallel tasks), `stress_mutex_hammer` (8x1000 mutex increments), `stress_atomic_hammer` (8x1000 atomic increments), `stress_channel_mpsc` (4 producers x 500 values), `stress_shared_multi_token` (3 tokens, 6 tasks, deadlock freedom), `stress_taskgroup_fan` (100 TaskGroup tasks), `stress_channel_select` (4-channel select drain), `stress_rwlock_writers` (4 RWLock writers), `stress_nested_spawn` (nested TaskGroup spawn), `stress_nested_return` (nested spawn with return values), `stress_pipeline` (3-stage channel pipeline). 717 unit + 550 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Auto-borrow for equip method non-self params + remove redundant tensor deep-copies**: Equip methods (plain, trait, generic, default trait) now auto-borrow Move-type non-self params to MutPtr on both callee and caller sides, matching the existing behavior for free functions. Caller-side uses `lower_call_arg` (was `lower_expr`) with `gir_equip_methods` tracking set to distinguish GIR-lowered methods (which auto-borrow) from C runtime methods (which take values by value). MoveZero post-call logic added for Move-ownership method args. Also removed 6 redundant `vec_int_copy()` wrapper calls in `lib/gg/tensor.gg` where `tensor_zeros[T]()` already copies internally. New fixture: `method_mut_borrow_arg.gg`. 717 unit + 540 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Guard[T] auto-deref for struct fields**: `guard.field_name` now transparently accesses inner struct fields through `Guard[T]`, `ReadGuard[T]`, and `WriteGuard[T]`. Reads and writes work for Guard and WriteGuard; ReadGuard is read-only. Implementation: added `guard_inner_suffix()` and `emit_guard_get_ptr()` helpers in IR lowering, inserted Guard detection in `lower_field_access` (reads) and `lower_field_assign` (writes), added `ReadGuard__T__get_ptr` to C backend. New fixtures: `guard_struct_field.gg` (Mutex[Point] field read/write), `guard_rwlock_field.gg` (RWLock[Config] with ReadGuard/WriteGuard field access). 717 unit + 539 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Auto-borrow + MoveZero for Move-type function arguments**: Eliminates double-free/use-after-free when passing Move-type values (Vector, Dict, String, Set, Box, user Move structs) to functions. (1) Borrow-ownership params of Move types are auto-borrowed to MutPtr, reusing the existing `mut_capture_locals` auto-deref infrastructure — no value copy, no aliasing. (2) Move-ownership (`!`) params emit MoveZero after the call to transfer ownership. Added `TypeRegistry::is_move_type()` method, simplified `is_move_type_local`. Updated all 8 param-lowering sites in `lower_function`/`lower_generic_function`. Fixed `lower_call_arg` to check callee's param type (not caller's local type) for auto-borrow decisions. Fixed Box vtable dispatch to handle pointer receivers (no double-indirection). New fixtures: `move_fn_arg_last_use.gg` (Move ownership transfer), `move_fn_arg_not_last_use.gg` (Borrow preserves caller access). 717 unit + 521 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`shared` keyword Phase 9 — Tests + Polish**: Added 7 new integration tests: `shared_float` (float type across spawn), `shared_string` (str with ARC-only read), `shared_atomic_error` (compile-time rejection of `shared(atomic) float`), `shared_stale_while`/`shared_stale_match` (stale-condition warnings for while/match), `shared_multi_spawn` (3 shared tokens with 3 tasks), `shared_early_return` (guard release on early return). **Fixed critical bug**: `type_name_for_id()` only checked named types, causing all primitive-typed shared variables (float, bool, etc.) to fall back to `int64_t` for Mutex/Guard/Shared mangling — `shared float x` would generate `Guard__int64_t` instead of `Guard__double`, producing wrong values at runtime. Added `c_type_name_for_id()` helper that resolves primitives and falls back to named types. Fixed 15+ call sites across `stmts.rs`, `exprs.rs` (shared helpers, spawn wrappers, lock/get/set functions). Updated outdated "stubbed" comment on Mutex runtime to reflect actual waker support. 717 unit + 528 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Fix coroutine Drop leak + CallIndirect codegen**: Coroutine poll functions now properly emit Drop/DropIfAlive instructions for Move-type locals (Task, Vector, String, etc.) stored in the coroutine frame. Previously skipped with a "frame owns memory" comment, causing heap leaks for any coroutine with non-trivial locals. Extracted `emit_drop_code`/`emit_drop_if_alive_code` helper functions shared by both normal and poll codegen paths. Fixed `CallIndirect` in poll context (was generating broken cast with argument values in type position). Moved `Task__T__drop` emission before coroutine helpers to fix forward-reference C errors. New fixtures: `spawn_coroutine_drops.gg`, `spawn_coroutine_string.gg`. 717 unit + 522 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **M:N Scheduler Phases 4+5: Stackless coroutine state machines + cooperative yield**: Spawned functions that internally await other tasks are now transformed into stackless coroutines instead of blocking worker threads. Added `poll` + `scheduled` fields to `GorgetTask`. `__gorget_worker` calls `task->poll()` for coroutines, handling `GORGET_POLL_READY` (signal done=1) vs `GORGET_POLL_PENDING` (wait for waker). Race-free `scheduled` protocol prevents double-submission: 0=being-polled, 1=queued, -1=waiting-for-waker. `__gorget_fiber_waker_wake` re-submits parent coroutine when child completes. Codegen: `fn_is_coroutine_candidate` detects spawned functions with internal awaits; `emit_coroutine` generates frame structs (`GorgetTask base + __state + all locals`) + poll state machines (switch-case per basic block, pre/post await states with waker-check blocks) + spawn/await/drop helpers. Non-coroutine helpers emitted first to avoid forward-reference issues. Full CFG supported (loops, branches via state ID mapping). New `spawn_nested_await.gg` + `spawn_many.gg` fixtures. 717 unit + 519 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **M:N Scheduler Phases 1-3: Executor pool replaces pthread-per-spawn**: `spawn` no longer creates one OS thread per task. Each spawned task is now submitted to the bounded executor thread pool (N = CPU cores) via `__gorget_executor_submit`. `__SpawnCtx_fn` now embeds `GorgetTask base` (replacing `pthread_t thread`) as its first field; a per-fn `__spawn_run_fn(GorgetTask*)` is set as `base.run`. `__gorget_worker` now signals task completion (sets `done=1`, broadcasts condvar, fires parent_waker) after `task->run(task)` returns. `await` waits via condvar on `base.done` instead of `pthread_join`. `TaskGroup` updated from `pthread_t[]` to `GorgetTask*[]` with condvar-based join. `__gorget_executor_resubmit` added for future Phase 4 waker use. New `tests/fixtures/spawn_many.gg` fixture proves 10,000 spawns with bounded threads. 717 unit + 517 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Fix use-after-free for Move-type values in struct-init and unwrap**: Added `is_move_type_local` helper that checks `CopySemantics::Move` on any type (not just GorgetArray). Two ownership-transfer fixes: (1) MoveZero after Option/Result unwrap — extracting a Move-type inner value zeros the Option/Result to prevent its drop from freeing the shared buffer. (2) Generalized struct-init MoveZero from GorgetArray-only to all Move types (Dict, String, Set, Box, user Move structs). Updated all existing `is_gorget_array_local` call sites (Shared constructor, generic Shared constructor) to use `is_move_type_local`. New fixtures: `move_type_fn_arg.gg` (struct-init with Move fields), `move_type_unwrap.gg` (unwrap of Move-type from Option). Function-call argument MoveZero deferred — blanket approach caused 40+ test regressions; needs last-use/liveness analysis to apply selectively.

- [2026-03-06] **GIR-to-GIR transform replaces `build_shared_async_variant`**: The fragile "re-lower the AST" approach for shared-async function variants has been replaced with a proper IR transform pass. New `src/ir/transforms/shared_async.rs` module with `inject_shared_token_management()` — clones the already-lowered GIR function and surgically modifies it: remaps param types, inserts lock preamble, splices release/reacquire around `__gorget_await_*` calls, inserts guard cleanup before returns. Exhaustive `LocalId` remapping walker covers all `Instruction`/`Terminator` variants. Spawn sites now record `PendingSharedVariant` requests processed after all functions are lowered. Removed: `build_shared_async_variant` (~180 lines), `emit_shared_param_reacquire` (~50 lines), `active_shared_params`/`SharedParamInfo` fields, await-handler token management code. 9 new transform unit tests. 717 unit + 517 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Prefix `await expr` syntax with double-await detection**: Both `await expr` and `expr.await()` are now supported as equivalent suspension points. `await expr.await()` is rejected with a `DoubleAwait` compile error. 708 unit + 517 integration tests.

- [2026-03-06] **Stale-condition warning (§3.4)**: When a local variable is derived from a shared binding and an await point occurs between the read and a branch condition using that local, the compiler emits a warning: `` `val` was read from shared `x` before an await — value may be stale ``. Tracks `shared_derived` (locals assigned from shared vars) and `stale_shared_derived` (marked at await points when tokens are released). Checks If/While/Match conditions for stale-derived locals. Reassignment clears stale status (refreshed value). Branch state save/restore/merge extended for both maps. New `SemanticWarningKind::StaleSharedCondition`. 2 new unit tests (`stale_shared_condition_warns`, `stale_shared_condition_refreshed_no_warn`). 2 new integration fixtures (`shared_stale_warning.gg`, `shared_stale_refreshed.gg`). 707 unit + 516 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Token release/reacquire at await points (§3.1/§3.2) + multi-token ordering (§3.3) + missing shared suggestion (§7.2)**: Three design plan items implemented. (1) Async token semantics: when an async function with shared mutable params reaches `.await()`, tokens are released before suspension and reacquired after resume. `build_shared_async_variant` re-lowers the function body with `Shared[Mutex[T]]` params, facade locals, and guard pointers. `emit_shared_param_reacquire` re-locks and re-derives pointers. Spawn site detects async+await callees via `expr_has_await`/`stmt_has_await` AST scanners and routes to the async variant. `fn_ast_bodies` + `active_shared_params` on `LoweringContext`. (2) Multi-token deadlock prevention: tokens acquired in ascending `decl_order`, released in reverse. `SharedSpawnArg.decl_order` field added. (3) `SpawnWithBorrowedRef` error now includes variable name and suggests `shared`. New tests: `shared_await_release.gg`, `shared_multi_token.gg`. 705 unit + 514 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Phase 0: Async Mutex — waker-based non-blocking lock**: `GorgetMutex` now has a waiter queue (`GorgetWaker` array + protecting `pthread_mutex_t`). New `gorget_mutex_poll_lock(m, out, waker)`: uses `pthread_mutex_trylock` — if succeeds, fills guard and returns `GORGET_POLL_READY`; if contended, registers waker in FIFO queue and returns `GORGET_POLL_PENDING`. `gorget_guard_release` updated: after `pthread_mutex_unlock`, wakes one waiter from the queue (FIFO order). New `gorget_mutex_free` properly destroys lock, wait_mtx, waiters array, and data. `gorget_mutex_new` initializes the waiter queue fields. Blocking `gorget_mutex_lock` retained for synchronous contexts. Both paths are compatible — a sync thread releasing a lock wakes async waiters. New integration test `mutex_async_contention.gg` (4 tasks × 100 increments = 400). 705 unit + 512 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Shared[Mutex[T]] — ARC-wrap sync primitives for lifetime safety**: Mutex sync primitives now wrapped in Shared (ARC) per design doc line 36 ("Always ARC-wrapped") and line 43 (`Shared[Mutex[T]]`). Bare Mutex pointers risked dangling across threads; Shared handles refcounting/RAII/drop, Mutex handles synchronization. Two-step access: `Shared.get()` → Mutex, then `lock → get/set → release`. C emission reordered (mutex/rwlock before shared defs). `Guard__T__get_ptr` / `WriteGuard__T__get_ptr` methods added for token wrapper mutable access. `emit_shared_mutex_lock_get` / `emit_shared_mutex_lock_set` helpers. Token wrapper unwraps Shared before locking. 705 unit + 511 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Token semantics for spawn — callee-transparent shared access**: Auto-shared (`shared int x`) variables now use token semantics at spawn sites. The compiler generates `__shared_token_<fn>` wrapper functions that: (1) acquire the token (lock/read/write as appropriate), (2) call the original function with plain types, (3) release the token. The lock is held for the entire function call — the synchronous execution region. Mutable borrow params (`int &counter`) get a pointer via `Guard__T__get_ptr` / `WriteGuard__T__get_ptr` (new runtime methods). Immutable params get a value copy under the lock. User-overridden shared vars (`shared(atomic)`, `shared(rwlock)`) bypass token wrappers and pass the raw sync primitive to callees that use explicit wrapper types. Added `fn_param_ownerships` to `LoweringContext` (populated during pre-scan). `SharedKind` from AST stored in `shared_locals` tuple to distinguish auto vs override. CFA uses `param_ownerships` from `FunctionInfo` to determine lock type per arg. Updated fixtures: `shared_spawn_mutex.gg`, `shared_spawn_readonly.gg`, `shared_arc_only.gg`, `shared_transparent.gg` — callees now use plain types. 705 unit + 511 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **Unnecessary `shared` warning**: Variables declared `shared` that never cross a concurrency boundary (i.e., never passed to `spawn`) now emit a warning: `variable 'x' is declared 'shared' but never crosses a concurrency boundary`. Added `SemanticWarning`/`SemanticWarningKind` types to `errors.rs`, `warnings: Vec<SemanticWarning>` field on `AnalysisResult`, `shared_spawned: FxHashSet<DefId>` tracking in borrow checker (populated in `cfa_at_spawn`), warning emission in final CFA pass, `report_semantic_warning` in `ErrorReporter` (uses `Diagnostic::warning()` for yellow output), and display in `build`/`check`/`run` command paths. Non-fatal — compilation proceeds. 705 unit + 511 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`shared(rwlock)` codegen — reader-writer lock for shared bindings**: `shared(rwlock) int x = 10` now produces working RWLock-based code with concurrent reads and exclusive writes. Transparent access: reads → `RWLock__T__read` + `ReadGuard__T__get` + `ReadGuard__T__drop`, writes → `RWLock__T__write` + `WriteGuard__T__set` + `WriteGuard__T__drop`, compound-assign → write-lock + get + compute + set + release. IR lowering: `emit_rwlock_read_get`, `emit_rwlock_write_set`, `emit_rwlock_write_get`, `emit_rwlock_write_finish` helpers. `ensure_rwlock_type_def` registers RWLock as Copy pointer type. `SharedLocalKind::RwLock` variant added. CFA: `cfa_at_spawn` recognizes `RWLock` callee param types to force `ArcRwLock` strategy. Method dispatch: `RWLock[T].read()` → `ReadGuard[T]`, `RWLock[T].write()` → `WriteGuard[T]`, `ReadGuard[T].get()` / `WriteGuard[T].get()` / `WriteGuard[T].set()` all wired in IR lowering. Key fix: broadened `lower_var_decl` UNIT_TYPE fallback to use `map_ast_type_mut` for ALL unregistered generic Named types (not just Callable), so `ReadGuard[int]`/`WriteGuard[int]` in function-body VarDecls auto-register. New fixture: `shared_rwlock.gg` (transparent + explicit reader/writer spawn). 705 unit + 511 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`shared(atomic)` codegen — lock-free atomic operations for shared scalars**: `shared(atomic) int x = 0` and `shared(atomic) bool done = false` now produce working lock-free code using AtomicInt/AtomicBool runtime primitives. Transparent access: reads → `atomic_load`, writes → `atomic_store`, `+=` → `atomic_add`, `-=` → `atomic_sub`. Other compound ops fall back to load+compute+store. Type validation rejects non-int/bool types at type-check time. CFA recognizes AtomicInt/AtomicBool callee params to force ArcAtomic strategy. `SharedLocalKind` enum replaces `bool` flag in `shared_locals` for clean 3-way dispatch (Mutex/SharedArc/Atomic). Runtime: `gorget_atomic_int_free`/`gorget_atomic_bool_free` added for RAII drop. C backend: `AtomicInt__new`/`AtomicBool__new`/`AtomicInt__free`/`AtomicBool__free` mappings added. New fixtures: `shared_atomic.gg` (int: read/write/add/sub/spawn), `shared_atomic_bool.gg` (bool: read/write/spawn). 705 unit + 510 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`shared` keyword Phase 6 — CFA ArcOnly optimization**: Read-only shared bindings now use `Shared[T]` (ARC-only, no locking) instead of `Mutex[T]`. CFA write tracking added to `Stmt::Assign` and `Stmt::CompoundAssign` handlers in borrow checker. Final CFA decision pass: shared vars never written → ArcOnly; written or spawned with Mutex param → ArcMutex; locally written + spawned as ArcOnly → upgraded to ArcMutex. Param-type-aware CFA: when callee param type is `Mutex[T]`, force ArcMutex (interior mutability). IR lowering branches on strategy: ArcOnly uses `Shared__T__new`/`Shared__T__get` (no Guard, no lock); ArcMutex uses existing Mutex+Guard path. `emit_shared_get` helper for lock-free reads. `shared_locals` tuple extended to 4-element `(hidden_local, inner_type, wrapper_type, is_arc_only)`. Shared[T] ↔ T coercion added to type checker `unify()`. Resolution map now includes shared VarDecl pattern spans for DefId lookup during IR lowering. Updated `shared_spawn_readonly.gg` to use `Shared[int]` param. New fixture: `shared_arc_only.gg`. 705 unit + 508 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`shared` keyword Phase 5 — transparent access**: `shared int x = 0` now allows plain read (`print(x)`), assignment (`x = 42`), and compound assignment (`x += 1`) — the compiler auto-emits lock+get/set+release behind the scenes. No explicit `.lock()` needed for local usage. Implementation: (1) Type checker reverted to keep shared vars at inner type T (not Mutex[T]). (2) IR lowering creates hidden mutex local + facade local (inner type); `shared_locals` map tracks the mapping. (3) `emit_shared_lock_get` / `emit_shared_lock_set` emit lock+borrow guard+get/set+release sequences. (4) `shared_pass_raw` flag ensures spawn passes the raw Mutex pointer. (5) Mutex[T] ↔ T coercion in type checker `unify()` allows spawned functions to take explicit Mutex params. New fixture: `shared_transparent.gg` (read+assign+compound-assign+spawn+readback). 705 unit + 507 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`shared` keyword Phases 2-4 MVP — CFA + IR Lowering + Codegen**: `shared int x = 0` now produces working code: the compiler automatically wraps the init value in a `Mutex[T]` constructor. CFA infrastructure added to borrow checker (`shared_var_defs`, `shared_out`, `cfa_at_spawn` tracking shared bindings across spawn boundaries). Borrow checker relaxes `SpawnWithBorrowedRef` rejection for shared bindings (origin marked Static). Type checker assigns `Mutex[T]` type to shared variables (lookup "Mutex" DefId, wrap inner type in `ResolvedType::Generic`). IR lowering: new `lower_shared_var_decl` creates temp for inner value, calls `Mutex__T__new(temp)`, registers wrapper type. `SharedStrategy` enum + `shared_bindings: FxHashMap<DefId, SharedStrategy>` threaded through `AnalysisResult`. `DefInfo.shared` field stores `SharedKind` from AST. MVP always uses Mutex (transparent access / ArcOnly deferred). 3 new integration fixtures: `shared_spawn_mutex.gg` (2 threads increment → 2), `shared_spawn_readonly.gg` (spawn read → 42), `shared_keyword_local.gg` (local lock → 10). 705 unit + 506 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-06] **`shared` keyword Phase 1 — Parser + AST**: Added `SharedKind` enum (`None`, `Auto`, `RwLock`, `Atomic`) to AST. New `shared` field on `Stmt::VarDecl` and `FieldDef`. Lexer: `Shared` keyword added. Parser: `parse_shared_var_decl()` handles `shared`, `shared(rwlock)`, `shared(atomic)` syntax before type in VarDecl position; struct field parser handles same syntax. Formatter: emits `shared`/`shared(rwlock)`/`shared(atomic)` prefix for both VarDecls and struct fields. All construction sites updated (5 VarDecl + 13 FieldDef). 3 new parser unit tests. Design doc at `docs/shared-keyword-design.md`. Renamed `shared` → `dh_secret` in `lib/gg/p2p.gg` to avoid keyword conflict. Language reference updated with `shared` syntax for VarDecl and struct fields. 705 unit tests + 503 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-05] **`spawn` on method calls — thread dispatch instead of blocking call**: `spawn receiver.method(args)` now generates real pthreads, matching the behavior of `spawn fn_name(args)`. Approach: thin wrapper function `__spawn_method_wrap_<Type__method>` that takes the receiver by value, borrows it locally to get the self pointer, and calls the mangled C method. The wrapper is treated as a regular spawned function — the C backend's `emit_spawn_helpers` generates all thread infrastructure automatically (zero backend changes). Changes: (1) Borrow checker extended with Case 4 in `Expr::Spawn` handler to accept `MethodCall` — validates receiver is owned/static (not borrowed), checks args. Two existing rejection tests flipped to pass. (2) IR lowering: `Expr::Spawn` handler extended with Case D for `MethodCall`; new `lower_method_spawn` resolves the method name via `fn_sigs`, builds/reuses the wrapper, registers Task type, and emits spawn call. New `build_method_spawn_wrapper` creates the thin wrapper function. Receiver zeroed after spawn to prevent double-free for Move types. (3) Removed `run_server_n` workaround wrapper from `httpserver_lifecycle.gg` — now uses direct `spawn s.accept_n(handle, n)`. Removed workaround comment from `httpserver_e2e.gg`. Two new integration fixtures: `spawn_method_basic.gg`, `spawn_method_void.gg`. Result: 702 unit tests + 502 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-05] **`variant_payloads(T)` + `meta for` inside match arm bodies**: New meta feature enabling dispatch wrappers to be collapsed from 4 explicit arms to a single `meta for` loop. Full pipeline implementation: (1) **AST** — `MatchItem` enum wrapping either `Arm(MatchArm)` or `MetaFor { vars, range, arm_template, span }`; `Stmt::Match.arms` changed to `Vec<MatchItem>`; all ~20 consumer sites updated with `filter_map(|i| i.arm())`. (2) **Parser** — `parse_meta_for_match_item` detects `meta` keyword inside match arm list; wraps result in `MatchItem::MetaFor`. (3) **Semantics** — `substitute_pattern` function for pattern meta-var substitution; `substitute_match_arm` (pub); `meta_str_to_type` (pub) for string→AST Type conversion; `expand_match_meta_for` expands MetaFor items at monomorphization time with type_env built from meta strings; `variant_payloads(T)` builtin returning `[[vname, inner_type], ...]` pairs via GIR type_registry with `__`-separator splitting + C-name reverse-mapping; `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` for Stmt::Match now calls `expand_match_meta_for` first. (4) **Generic scanner** — `non_generic_enum_defs` map added to `GenericCollector`; `scan_meta_for_match_arm` performs AST-level `variant_payloads` expansion during generic collection so `col_slice_inner[T]` calls are monomorphized; `extract_variant_payloads_enum_name` helper. (5) **IR lowering** — `lower_function` (non-generic functions) now calls `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` so MetaFor match items are expanded before lowering. (6) **Formatter** — `Stmt::Match` arm iteration updated to format MetaFor items as `meta for ... in ...: case ...`. (7) **Exhaustiveness checker** — skips check when any `MetaFor` items present. (8) **`lib/gg/dataframe.gg`** — 4 equip Column methods (`col_len`, `is_null_at`, `dtype_name`, `null_count`) + 4 free functions (`col_slice`, `col_filter`, `col_gather`, `col_concat`) collapsed from 4 explicit arms each to single `meta for vname, T in variant_payloads(Column)` loops. (9) **Test** — `tests/fixtures/meta_variant_payloads.gg` integration test. Result: 702 unit tests + 501 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-05] **Soundness fixes for closure spawn codegen + MutCallable aliasing**: Six issues found in review, all fixed. Feature A: (1) Borrow checker now rejects `spawn` of closures with mutable captures (`SpawnClosureCaptureMutable` error) — mutable captures store pointers to parent stack, unsafe across threads. (2) Spawn captures now track original struct field indices (`Vec<(String, TypeId, u32)>`) to prevent field index mismatch when mixed ByValue/ByMutRef captures exist. Feature B: (3) `BranchState` now includes `mut_captured_vars` and `mut_capture_owners` for correct save/restore/merge in if/else branches. (4) `mut_captured_vars` changed from `HashMap<DefId, (S,D,Span)>` to `HashMap<DefId, Vec<(S,D,Span)>>` — supports multiple closures capturing the same variable. (5) Closure reassignment (`c = new_closure`) releases old closure's mut capture locks and registers new ones; lock release happens before RHS evaluation to avoid false positives. (6) Two closures both mutably capturing same variable is now correctly rejected (body walk of second closure detects write conflict). 5 new unit tests: `spawn_closure_with_mutable_capture_rejected`, `spawn_closure_with_readonly_capture_ok`, `two_mut_closures_same_var_rejected`, `sequential_mut_closures_in_scopes_ok`, `closure_reassignment_releases_old_locks`. Result: 702 unit tests + 500 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-05] **Closure spawn codegen + MutCallable aliasing enforcement**: Two deferred items completed. (A) **Closure spawn codegen**: `spawn c()` and `spawn ((): body)()` now generate real pthreads. Approach: thin wrapper function `__spawn_wrap___Closure_N` that accepts flat capture+arg params, reconstructs the closure struct, and calls `__Closure_N__call(&struct, args...)`. The wrapper is treated as a regular spawned function by the C backend — `emit_spawn_helpers` generates context struct, thread function, and spawn/await helpers automatically. Changes: `closure_info` map extended to carry by-value captures; `spawn_wrapper_fns` accumulator added to `LoweringContext`; `build_spawn_wrapper` and `lower_closure_spawn` helpers in `exprs.rs`; `Expr::Spawn` handler extended with Case A (closure variable) and Case B (inline closure literal); wrappers emitted into module after closure call functions in `mod.rs`; `spawn_closure_copy.gg` updated to actually spawn a closure; two new integration fixtures (`spawn_closure_inline.gg`, `spawn_closure_void.gg`). (B) **MutCallable aliasing enforcement**: If a `MutCallable` closure captures variable `x` mutably, reading or writing `x` directly while the closure is live is now a compile error. Two new error variants: `ReadWhileMutCaptured` and `WriteWhileMutCaptured`. New `mut_captured_vars` and `mut_capture_owners` maps track the constraint. Populated in `VarDecl` from the pending capture set. Checked in `Expr::Identifier` (reads) and `Stmt::Assign`/`CompoundAssign` (writes). Cleared in `check_move` (closure moved via `!c`) and `check_block` (closure goes out of scope at block exit). 5 new unit tests. Result: 697 unit tests + 500 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-05] **`meta op` compile-time operator parameters + `int_to_float` to `std.conv`**: Two paired improvements to `lib/gg/tensor.gg` and `lib/gg/dataframe.gg`. (1) `int_to_float` moved from duplicate local definitions in both libraries to `std.conv` — added C impl (`gorget_int_to_float`), backend mapping, stdlib declaration, updated imports. (2) New `meta op` language feature: compile-time operator token parameters. New syntax: `meta op_name` in function param lists (no runtime value), `a meta[op] b` for infix usage in template bodies, `meta +` / `meta <` etc. at call sites. Implemented across the full pipeline: AST (`Param.is_meta_op`, `Expr::MetaOpInfix`, `Expr::MetaOpToken`), parser (`parse_param`, `infix_bp`, `parse_infix`, `parse_prefix`), all semantic passes (resolver, typecheck, borrow, meta substitution, rewrite, loader, formatter, visitor), GIR lowering (`GenericCollector.register_instance_with_ops` with op-suffix mangling, `lower_generic_function` filters meta op params + pre-populates meta env with `MetaValue::Op`, `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` initial env sweep for upfront substitution). Collapsed 14 tensor functions into 3 templates: `tensor_elemwise[T]`, `tensor_scalar_op[T]`, `tensor_cmpwise[T]` — with 14 thin expression-body wrappers preserving the public API. Result: 692 unit tests + 498 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-05] **Closure capture set tracking in borrow checker**: The borrow checker now computes full capture sets for closures — tracking which variables are captured, whether by Read or Mutable access, and whether each has a borrowed origin. Two new AST visitors: `CapturedMutationCollector` (detects assignments inside closure bodies) and `CaptureSetCollector` (collects all free variables with their capture mode and origin status). `compute_capture_set()` method orchestrates both phases. Capture sets are stored per closure-typed variable via `closure_capture_sets: FxHashMap<DefId, CaptureSet>`. Spawn enforcement extended from direct-function-call-only to three cases: (1) direct function calls (unchanged), (2) closure variable calls (`spawn c()` — checks capture set), (3) inline closure calls (`spawn ((): body)()` — computes capture set on the fly). New error variant `SpawnClosureCaptureBorrowed` reports each borrowed capture by name. 8 new unit tests, 2 integration fixtures (`spawn_closure_copy.gg`, `spawn_closure_shared.gg`). Note: IR/backend codegen for closure spawn not yet implemented (falls through to direct call); tracked in TODO.md. Result: 692 unit tests + 498 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`gg.db` + `gg.sqlite` + `gg.influx` — database connection modules**: Three new stdlib modules: (1) `lib/gg/db.gg` — shared foundation with `Row`, `Value`, `Param` (enum with Int/Float/Str/Bool/Null variants), `FromRow` trait, `Queryable` trait (query_raw/exec/exec_simple), `DbConnection` extends `Queryable`. (2) `lib/gg/sqlite.gg` — zero-external-dependency SQLite client via embedded SQLite3 amalgamation (260K lines, public domain). SQLite amalgamation embedded inline via `include_str!` in `c_runtime.rs`; emitted conditionally when SQLite functions are detected. Gorget C wrapper functions in `src/backend/c/sqlite3/gorget_sqlite.c`. `SqliteConn` struct with `int _handle` (stores `sqlite3*` as int64); implemented `Queryable` + `DbConnection` via `equip with`. String values from SQLite are deep-copied using `GORGET_ALLOC` to prevent use-after-free when `sqlite3_step()` invalidates column text buffers. (3) `lib/gg/influx.gg` — pure-Gorget InfluxDB 2.x client using `gg.http` for HTTP; writes via Line Protocol (`POST /api/v2/write`); queries via Flux (`POST /api/v2/query`, annotated CSV response). (4) `@derive(FromRow)` in `src/semantic/derive.rs` — auto-generates `equip T with FromRow:` using `Row.get()`, `get_int()`, `get_float()`, `get_bool()` typed accessors for struct fields. Integration test `tests/fixtures/sqlite_basic.gg` passes: CREATE TABLE, INSERT with params (via `Vector[Param]().push()`), SELECT with row mapping and `@derive(FromRow)`, DELETE, COUNT. Result: 676 unit tests + 493 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`gg.dataframe`: TypedColumn[T] unification + ownership fix cascade**: Replaced the 4 concrete column structs (`IntColumn`, `FloatColumn`, `StrColumn`, `BoolColumn`) and their duplicate equip blocks with a single generic `TypedColumn[T]` struct and one `equip TypedColumn[T]`. The `Column` enum now carries `TypedColumn[T]` payloads directly. This required moving `TypedColumn[T]` before `Column` in source order to fix a forward-reference bug in the type registry. Collapsed `col_concat` from 4×2 nested loops to one `col_concat_inner[T]`. Simultaneously fixed a cascade of Move-type ownership bugs exposed by the newly-emitted DataFrame drops: (1) `df_from_columns` must deep-copy `names` via `vec_str_copy` to avoid aliasing the caller's local; (2) all `col_from_*` and `col_*_null` functions must deep-copy their `data` (and `mask`) args before storing in `TypedColumn` — using new `vec_int_copy`, `vec_float_copy`, `vec_bool_copy` helpers; (3) `column_names(self)` must return `vec_str_copy(self.col_names)` instead of a shallow copy; (4) `merge_sort_idxs` must return `vec_int_copy(idxs)` instead of `idxs` to avoid aliasing with the caller's `idxs` local; (5) `df_clip`'s early `return df` branches replaced with `return df_copy(df)` (new helper); (6) `df_inner_join`, `df_left_join`, `df_outer_join` must use `vec_int_copy(right_idx.get(key).unwrap())` to avoid use-after-free when two left rows share the same join key. Root cause of all bugs: Gorget does not zero the caller's local when passing a Move type as a function argument, so the callee's parameter and the caller's local alias the same heap buffer; both are freed at their respective scope exits → double-free / use-after-free. Result: all 16 dataframe tests pass, 493 integration tests pass, 670 unit tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Named scopes + relaxed borrow-across-await**: Two paired features. (A) **Named scope blocks** — mid-function drop zones: `identifier:\n    body` syntax. Variables created inside are dropped at block exit via existing RAII drop elaborator. Thread safety falls out naturally: `Task[T]`'s drop joins the thread, so tasks spawned inside a named scope are always joined before the scope exits. Implemented across all 12 compiler passes (AST, parser, visitor, name resolver, type checker, borrow checker, meta evaluator, generics scan, AST rewriter, loader, formatter, IR lowering). Two integration fixtures: `named_scope_basic.gg`, `async_param_across_await.gg`. (B) **Relaxed borrow-across-await** — `str` parameters are now allowed across `.await()` suspension points: caller is blocked at the direct-await call site, so params remain alive. Two paired borrow checker changes make this sound: (1) spawn-site enforcement rejects passing borrowed references to `spawn` (new `SpawnWithBorrowedRef` error); (2) `Await` invalidation filter now exempts `Param`-only origins (`contains_local() = false`). Updated 3 existing tests to expect NO error (param-only origins), renamed them, added 4 new tests (`param_str_across_await_ok`, `local_str_from_param_across_await_ok`, `local_str_across_await_still_rejected`, `spawn_with_borrowed_str_rejected`, `spawn_with_static_str_ok`). Documentation updated in §6.16 (new named scope section) and §7 async suspension-point safety. Result: 675 unit + 495 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Fix `field_access` and `meta_enum_ordinal` integration tests**: Two fixtures written before/during the f-string opt-in change used plain `"..."` strings for interpolation. After the change, only `f"..."` strings interpolate. Updated `meta_enum_ordinal.gg` (`"{vname}={idx}"` → `f"{vname}={idx}"`, `"{vname}"` → `f"{vname}"`) and `field_access.gg` (`"{fname}={v}"` → `f"{fname}={v}"`). The meta substitution system already correctly handles f-strings: compile-time meta variables are inlined as `Literal` segments; runtime variables remain as `Interpolation` segments for runtime evaluation. Result: 670 unit + 493 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Remove `char` type — Python-model strings + `byte` type**: Completed 7-part plan to remove `char` from Gorget and establish Python-model string semantics. (1) `byte` type added as `uint8` alias; `b'A'` byte literals supported (lexed as `IntLiteral(65)`, unify with `byte` type); `byte_at(i)` → `byte` O(1); `byte_len(s)` → `int` O(1). (2) `str` methods added: `is_alpha()`, `is_digit()`, `is_alnum()`, `is_whitespace()`, `is_upper()`, `is_lower()`, `is_hex_digit()`, `is_ascii()`, `to_upper()`, `to_lower()` (all-codepoints semantics). (3) `ord(s: str) → int` takes str (not char); `chr(n: int) → str` already returned str. (4) Removed `char` from type system: `Token::CharLiteral`, `PrimitiveType::Char`, `Expr::CharLiteral`, `Constant::Char`, `CHAR_TYPE` all removed; single-quoted `'a'` now emits `Token::StringLiteral` (1-char str). (5) stdlib migrated. (6) Fixtures migrated. Result: 662 unit + 488 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **f-strings + single-quoted strings + char→str coercion**: Adopted Python/C#-style opt-in interpolation — only `f"..."` / `f'...'` strings interpolate; plain `"..."` treats `{` as a literal character. Result: 662 unit + 486 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Duplicate equip detection + trait inheritance cycle detection**: (1) Duplicate inherent equip blocks (`equip Foo:` twice) now emit `DuplicateImpl { trait_: "(inherent)", type_ }` with message "duplicate inherent impl block for type `Foo`". Previously they were silently merged. Duplicate trait impls were already caught; inherent was the missing case. (2) Trait inheritance cycles (`trait A extends B` + `trait B extends A`, or `trait A extends A`) previously caused a fatal stack overflow in `collect_all_required_methods`. Fixed via: new `TraitCycle { trait_, cycle }` error variant; `validate_trait_cycles()` DFS pass runs before `validate_trait_impls`; `collect_all_required_methods` refactored to use a visited `FxHashSet` as a hard cycle guard. Cycle error message includes the full path e.g. "A → B → A". Five new unit tests. Result: 668 unit tests + 491 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Method signature validation in equip blocks**: `validate_trait_impls()` in `src/semantic/traits.rs` now compares each implemented method's `FunctionSig` (return type, param count, per-param types, has_self, self_ownership) against the corresponding trait definition. `error_id` in a trait sig is treated as a wildcard (for `Self`/generic-dependent positions in builtin traits). `self_ownership: None` in a trait sig is a wildcard (don't enforce). Fixed two bugs in builtin trait registrations: `Iterator.next` and `Iterable.iter` were registered with `Borrow` for `&self` but should be `MutableBorrow` (since `&self` parses as `MutableBorrow` in Gorget). Fixed `test_cleanup.gg` and `test_with_clause.gg` which used `void drop(self)` instead of the correct `void drop(!self)`. `TypeTable` threaded into `validate_trait_impls` for `display()` in error messages. Three new unit tests: `method_return_type_mismatch`, `method_param_count_mismatch`, `method_signature_correct_no_error`. Result: 663 unit tests + 491 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Generic traits with default meta implementations (`make_variant`, `Self is Enum`, blank equip)**: Closed four gaps so default trait method bodies can use meta expressions that refer to `Self`. (1) `make_variant(T, "Variant")` builtin rewrites to `Expr::Path ["T", "Variant"]` in `substitute_expr` (meta.rs) and `rewrite_expr` (rewrite.rs); name resolver special-cases it. (2) `Enum`/`Struct` categories added to `eval_type_is_check` using the GIR TypeRegistry. (3) `parse_equip_block` makes the colon+body optional so `equip Color with FromStr` (no body) compiles. (4) All three equip lowering paths (`lower_equip_method`, `lower_generic_equip_methods_with_defaults`, `lower_static_trait_method`) evaluate `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` with `Self = equipped_type`. Root-cause fixes: (A) `substitute_type` in generics.rs was missing the `Type::SelfType` arm — `Option[Self]` mangled as `Option__unknown` because `Self` is a keyword type, not `Type::Named { name: "Self" }`. (B) Newly-created `Option__Color` types were not in `ctx.enum_variants` (the scan ran before the types were created by `map_ast_type_mut`); added a re-scan of monomorphized enum type_defs after `register_unregistered_trait_equip_sigs`. Fixture: `tests/fixtures/trait_default_meta.gg` — `FromStr` trait with `Option[Self] from_str(str s)` default body equipped on `Color` and `Direction` enums. Result: 660 unit tests + 491 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`meta log(...)` — compile-time debug output**: Soft diagnostic (vs. `meta assert` hard failure) that prints to stderr during compilation as `[meta] message`. Implemented as a new AST node: `Item::MetaLog(MetaLog)` for file scope, `Stmt::MetaLog { args, span }` for function bodies. `log` is NOT a new keyword — recognized as `Token::Identifier("log")` after the `meta` keyword in the parser. File-scope `meta log` is evaluated in `process_meta_item` (Phase 0). Function-body `meta log` is evaluated in `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` (GIR lowering for generics) and `eval_meta_fn_body_stmt` (compile-time function evaluation). All other passes (resolver, type-checker, borrow checker, rewriter, GIR stmts, generics scan/substitute, loader qualifier) treat `Stmt::MetaLog` and `Item::MetaLog` as transparent no-ops or skip. Formatter fully implemented with newline for both item and statement variants. Fixture `tests/fixtures/meta_log.gg` tests file-scope and generic-body meta log with `typename(T)`. Documented in `docs/language-reference.md` §19.3. Result: 660 unit tests + 490 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`embed_file(path)` — compile-time file embedding**: Phase 0 meta builtin that reads a file at compile time and inlines its contents as a `str` constant. Path is resolved relative to the source file's directory. Implemented by adding `source_dir: Option<PathBuf>` to `MetaContext`, threading it through `evaluate_meta_consts_with_source_dir` (new) and `analyze_with_source_dir` (new, wraps existing `analyze`). The 3 main entry points in `main.rs` now pass `source_dir`. Fixture `tests/fixtures/embed_file.gg` embeds two sibling `.txt` files. Documented in `docs/language-reference.md` §19.7. Result: 660 unit tests + 489 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`field_value(val, fname)` — compile-time field access builtin**: Source-to-source rewrite that transforms `field_value(val, "x")` → `val.x` (FieldAccess). Two rewrite sites: (1) `substitute_expr` in `meta.rs` — fires during `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` (GIR lowering) after `fname` is substituted to a string literal; (2) `rewrite_expr` in `rewrite.rs` — fires for direct literal usage at Phase 2.5. Name resolver special-cased so `field_value(val, fname)` inside `meta for` bodies does not produce "undefined name" errors for either `field_value` or the meta-loop variable. `eval_delayed_expr` in `meta.rs` emits a targeted error if `field_value` reaches compile-time evaluation. Fixture `tests/fixtures/field_access.gg` tests direct literal access, generic serializer with `to_debug[T]`, and generic int-field sum with `sum_int_fields[T]`. Documented in `docs/language-reference.md` §19.15. Result: 660 unit tests + 488 integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`enum_ordinal(T, name)` + `enum_from_ordinal(T, n)` builtins**: Two new compile-time builtins for enum reflection. `enum_ordinal(T, name)` returns the zero-based ordinal of a named variant (`MetaValue::Int`). `enum_from_ordinal(T, n)` returns the variant name at ordinal `n` (`MetaValue::Str`). Both work in delayed meta contexts (inside generic function bodies): as `meta if` conditions, as `meta for` range bounds (`0..enum_ordinal(T, "Blue")`), and as string comparison targets. Fixture demonstrates nested double-loop ordinal printing (outer integer range + inner variant_names), enum_from_ordinal round-trip verification, and enum_ordinal as a range bound. Documented in `docs/language-reference.md` §19.15. Result: 660 unit tests + 19 meta integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`fields(T)` + multi-var `meta for` destructuring**: New `fields(T)` builtin returns `(name, type)` pairs for all struct fields, iterable via the new `meta for fname, ftype in fields(T):` two-variable destructuring form. Implementation: (1) AST: `Stmt::MetaFor.var_name: Spanned<String>` → `vars: Vec<Spanned<String>>`; (2) Parser: comma-separated identifier list before `in`; (3) Formatter updated; (4) Evaluator: integer-range branch guards against multi-var, list-iteration branch positionally binds `MetaValue::List` items to each variable; (5) `fields(T)` builtin emits `MetaValue::List(vec![List([Str(name), Str(type)]), ...])` with GIR→Gorget name normalization (`"Str"` → `"str"`); (6) `substitute_block` called per-iteration so loop variable values are inlined into string interpolations and other expressions before inner meta constructs are evaluated; (7) `meta_expr_to_type_name` extended to handle `Expr::StringLiteral` so `meta if ftype is numeric:` continues to work after variable substitution. Documented in §19.14. Result: 660 unit tests + 18 meta integration tests all pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Meta `T is Category` — type predicate syntax**: `T is X` in delayed meta conditions evaluates to `bool` at monomorphization time. Categories: `int`/`integer` (all integer types), `float` (any FP), `signed`, `unsigned`, `numeric`. Single-member categories: `bool`, `str`, `char`, `void`. Everything else is an exact canonical-name match (`float32`, `int8`, `MyStruct`, etc.). Negation (`T is not X`) and composability (`T is signed or T is float`) work automatically. Implementation: (1) Extended `src/parser/pattern.rs` to handle type-name keywords (`int`, `float`, `bool`, etc.) as `Pattern::Binding` so they parse in `is` RHS position. (2) Added `pattern_to_name`, `eval_type_is_check`, and `Expr::Is` arm in `eval_delayed_expr` in `src/semantic/meta.rs`. (3) Added `tests/fixtures/meta_type_is.gg` fixture + integration test. (4) Documented in `docs/language-reference.md` §19.13. Result: 660 unit tests + new `meta_type_is` integration test pass; all 17 meta integration tests green.

- [2026-03-04] **Module-scoped namespaces — Phase 5: GIR name mangling**: All non-entry module functions get a mangled C name: `path.join("__") + "___" + func_name` (e.g., `gg.fmt.join` → `gg__fmt___join`, avoiding linker collisions when modules share function names). Implementation: (1) `module_fn_manglings` pre-computed BEFORE flat module creation by recursing into `Item::Module` nodes, keyed by `func.name.span.start`; excludes `FunctionBody::Declaration` and `FunctionBody::Extern` (C-runtime symbols must keep their C names). (2) Pre-scan registers the mangled name in `fn_sigs` and `fn_param_names`, and sets `extern_bindings[bare_name] = mangled_name` so all call sites resolve transparently. (3) `lower_function` accepts `name_override: Option<&str>` to emit the mangled C symbol. Three regressions fixed: (A) `infer_method_return_type` in the C backend incorrectly classified mangled names as method calls via `rfind("__")` — added early-return guard for names containing `___`. (B) Spawn infrastructure used the Gorget bare name to call the target function; updated `Expr::Spawn` lowering to resolve via `extern_bindings` first so spawn wrappers call the mangled C symbol. (C) Synthetic stdlib functions (`FunctionBody::Declaration`) were mangled by mistake — excluded by the `FunctionBody::Declaration` guard in `collect` fn. Result: 660 unit tests + 482 integration tests pass (same baseline as before Phase 5).

- [2026-03-04] **Module-scoped namespaces — compiler phases 1–4 (core implementation)**: File-based modules now preserve identity through the semantic analysis pipeline. (1) AST: Added `Item::Module { path, items }` variant wrapping non-entry module items. (2) Loader: `merge_modules()` wraps non-entry modules in `Item::Module` instead of flattening them, carrying the logical import path through `load_recursive`. (3) Scope: Added `ScopeKind::FileModule { path }`, `names_in_current_scope()`, and `export_non_private()` to `ScopeTable`. (4) All exhaustive `match item.node` sites updated: `resolve.rs` `collect_top_level_inner` pushes FileModule scope, defines items, exports non-private to parent, pops; `resolve_item_body` recurses into nested items so function bodies are properly resolved (critical bug fix: without this, identifiers inside module functions weren't in the resolution map, breaking the struct-literal rewrite pass and causing missing `MoveZero` GIR instructions → use-after-free in tensor tests); `rewrite.rs`, `meta.rs`, `typecheck.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `traits.rs`, `derive.rs`, `formatter/mod.rs` all handle `Item::Module`. (5) GIR lowering: `lower_module` computes a flat view of all items (flattening `Item::Module` wrappers) before the existing lowering loop, preserving all downstream behavior. Result: 660 unit tests + 482 integration tests pass (only 2 pre-existing httpserver failures remain, unrelated to this change). Phase 5 (GIR name mangling) and Phase 6 (lib prefix cleanup) remain as future work.

- [2026-03-04] **JSON body/response support for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `from gg.json import Json, json_parse, json_stringify` to httpserver.gg. Added `req.json() -> Result[Json, str]` to `equip HttpRequest` — delegates directly to `json_parse(self.body)`, content-type agnostic. Added `HttpServerResponse.of_json(Json val)` factory — calls `json_stringify(val).str()` and sets `Content-Type: application/json`. Key finding: Gorget `{}` string interpolation conflicts with JSON object literal notation `{...}`, so JSON values must be built via `Dict[str, Json]` + `json_stringify` rather than raw string literals. New fixture: `httpserver_json.gg` (valid JSON parse → correct fields; invalid JSON → Err; of_json content-type; of_json body round-trip; wire-level POST→echo→parse). 469 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 6: Server Lifecycle for `gg.httpserver`**: (6A) Per-connection max requests: added `max_requests_per_conn: int` field to `HttpServer` (0 = unlimited); added `HttpServer.with_max_requests(host, port, max_req)` factory; added `http_server_handle_conn_ka_limited` and `http_server_handle_conn_tls_ka_limited` (4-param variants with request counting and `server_wants_close` logic); updated `serve_plain` and `serve_tls` to spawn `_limited` variants passing `self.max_requests_per_conn`; kept original 3-param `http_server_handle_conn_ka`/`http_server_handle_conn_tls_ka` for backward-compatible external use. (6B) `accept_n` method: `HttpServer.accept_n(handler, n)` binds the port, accepts exactly `n` connections using `_limited` handler, waits for all to finish via `TaskGroup.join()`, then closes the server socket. New fixture: `httpserver_lifecycle.gg` (serve_n two GET requests → both 200; max_requests_per_conn=1 → server closes after 1st request on keep-alive socket). 660 unit + 468 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 5: Static File Enhancements for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `http_etag(str content) -> str` — computes SHA-1 hash of file content via `crypto_sha1(bytes_from_str(content))` and returns it as a double-quoted hex string (RFC 7232 format). Updated `http_serve_file` to: (1) compute ETag for every file read and add it to the `200 OK` response headers; (2) check `if-none-match` header — return `304 Not Modified` with ETag header when it matches; (3) parse `Range: bytes=start-end` (or `bytes=start-`) header — return `206 Partial Content` with `Content-Range` header and the requested byte slice. Added imports `bytes_from_str`, `bytes_to_hex` from `std.bytes` and `crypto_sha1` from `gg.crypto`. New fixture: `httpserver_static_enhanced.gg` (200+ETag, 304 on match, 200 on miss, range `0-3`, range `5-end`). 660 unit + 467 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 4: Body Parsers & Cookies for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `parse_form_body(str body) -> Dict[str, str]` — splits on `&`, decodes key/value pairs via `form_decode` (`+` → space, `%XX` → byte). Added `parse_cookies(str header) -> Dict[str, str]` — splits on `;`, trims, splits at first `=`, no encoding (cookie values are raw). Extended `equip HttpRequest` with `form_params() -> Dict[str, str]` (delegates to `parse_form_body(self.body)`) and `cookies() -> Dict[str, str]` (reads `self.headers["cookie"]` and delegates to `parse_cookies`, returns empty dict if header absent). New fixture: `httpserver_body_parsers.gg` (form `+` decoding, `%20` decoding, multi-cookie parsing, no-cookie empty dict, empty body empty dict). 660 unit + 466 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 3: Routing Enhancements for `gg.httpserver`**: (3A) Wildcard routes: added `wildcard_routes: Dict[str, Callable[...]]` field to `Router`; `route()` now detects `*` prefix segments and stores them there; added `http_router_match_wildcard()` module-level helper that finds the `*name` segment, verifies preceding segments match exactly, and captures all remaining path parts joined by `/` into `params["name"]`; added wildcard dispatch as step 2b in `dispatch_inner` (between param match and HEAD auto-handling); updated `allowed_methods()` to scan `wildcard_routes` in addition to `exact_routes` and `param_routes`. (3B) Prefix mounting: added `prefix_mount(&self, str prefix, Router sub)` method that copies all exact, param, and wildcard routes from `sub` into `self` with `prefix` prepended (middlewares not copied — parent controls the pipeline). New fixture: `httpserver_routing.gg` (wildcard match, wildcard non-match, prefix exact route, prefix param route, unmounted path → 404). 660 unit + 465 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 2: Pre-dispatch Middleware for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `pre_middlewares: Vector[Callable[Option[HttpServerResponse](HttpRequest)]]` field to `Router`. Added `router.before(hook)` method to register pre-dispatch hooks. Rewrote `dispatch()` to run `before` hooks first — first `Some(resp)` short-circuits (handler is skipped); post-dispatch middlewares still run on short-circuit responses for logging/CORS. Extracted `run_post_middlewares` helper to avoid code duplication. New fixture: `httpserver_before.gg`. 660 unit + 464 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 1: Protocol Correctness for `gg.httpserver`**: (1A) URL percent-decoding: `http_server_parse_request` (and TLS variant) now apply `url_decode` to path and query string after splitting; `parse_query_string` decodes each key and value individually. (1B) 405 Method Not Allowed: `dispatch_inner` now checks `allowed_methods()` before returning 404 — returns 405 with `Allow` header when the path exists under a different method. (1C) Chunked Transfer-Encoding: Added `http_server_parse_hex`, `http_server_read_body_chunked`, and TLS variant; `http_server_parse_request` now checks `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` before Content-Length. (1D) Request body size limit: Added `max_body_bytes: int = 16777216` (16MB) parameter to parse functions; added `max_body_bytes` field to `HttpServer` with `HttpServer.with_max_body()` factory. Updated `httpserver_router_extended` expected output from 404 to 405 (correct behavior). New fixtures: `httpserver_protocol.gg`, `httpserver_chunked.gg`. 660 unit + 463 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`gg.http` + `gg.httpserver` REST completion, HEAD/OPTIONS auto-handling, large body test, and documentation**: (1) Added `http_patch` to `lib/gg/http.gg` completing the REST method set (`GET`/`POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE`/`PATCH`). (2) Added `put()`, `delete()`, `patch()` convenience methods to `Router` in `lib/gg/httpserver.gg` (all delegate to `route()`). (3) Added `HEAD` auto-handling in `dispatch_inner`: when no explicit HEAD route is registered, re-dispatches as GET and strips the response body. (4) Added `OPTIONS` auto-handling: `allowed_methods()` helper scans `exact_routes` and `param_routes` for all registered methods on a path; OPTIONS returns 200 with `Allow` header or 404 if no routes match. (5) Fixed `language-reference.md`: replaced the entirely wrong `gg.http` stub (which described a `Response`/`Client` API that doesn't exist) with correct docs for `HttpResponse`, all 5 client functions, and added a complete `gg.httpserver` section covering `HttpRequest`, `HttpServerResponse`, `Router`, middleware, TLS, static files, and connection handlers. (6) Three new integration test fixtures: `httpserver_methods.gg` (PUT/DELETE/PATCH routes + HEAD/OPTIONS auto-handling), `httpserver_large_body.gg` (8192-byte POST body via real TCP verifying `read_exact` multi-read path), `http_patch.gg` (end-to-end PATCH client+server). 660 unit + 461 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Code review + exhaustive tests for `gg.http` and `gg.httpserver`**: Fixed 4 bugs and added 7 new integration test fixtures. **(1A) `redirect()` bug**: `resp.headers.put()` mutated a copy due to Dict value semantics — fixed by building the headers dict before passing it to the constructor. **(1B) `http_parse_url` redirect port loss**: relative redirects dropped the port number on reconstruction — fixed by including non-default port in the rebuilt URL. **(1C) Dead code removal**: Deleted unused `http_server_default_not_found` function. **(1D) DRY `get()`/`post()`**: Simplified to delegate to `route()`. **(Codegen fix — callable parameter → GorgetClosure assignment)**: Callable function parameters are lowered as `void*` in the C ABI (callers pass `(void*)(void*[2]){fn_ptr, env}`). When assigning such a parameter to a `GorgetClosure` local (`Callable[T] h = handler`), the C backend emitted `_5 = _4` which fails because `GorgetClosure` is a struct. Fixed `emit_instruction` `Assign` handler: when `dst_is_escaped_closure && src_c_type == "void"`, emit `*(GorgetClosure*)src` dereference. **(Borrow checker fix — `compute_method_call_origin`)**: Static factory calls like `HttpServerResponse.not_found()` produced `UnresolvedBorrowOrigin` errors because the type checker doesn't populate `method_resolutions` when the receiver is a struct type name (struct `DefInfo.type_id = None` → `infer_expr` returns `error_id` → `traits.resolve_method` fails). Fixed in `compute_method_call_origin`: when `method_resolutions` has no entry and the receiver resolves to `Struct`/`Enum`/`Newtype`, return `BorrowOrigin::Static` (static constructors always produce fresh values). Also added `BorrowOrigin::Static` return for callable variable calls (`DefKind::Variable`) and variant constructors (`DefKind::Variant`) in `compute_call_origin`. **(New fixtures)**: `httpserver_router.gg` (restored missing fixture), `httpserver_router_extended.gg`, `httpserver_response.gg`, `httpserver_query_string.gg`, `httpserver_parse_request.gg`, `http_urls_extended.gg`, `httpserver_e2e_extended.gg`. Updated `http_urls` integration test to include `tls` bool output. All 660 unit + 458 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **New meta/comptime builtins + `meta while`**: Extended the delayed meta system (evaluated at monomorphization time in generic bodies) with 11 new builtins and a new statement form. **Builtins**: `typeof(T)` (alias for typename), `bitwidth(T)` (8/16/32/64 for numeric types), `min_val(T)` / `max_val(T)` (integer range bounds as MetaValue::Int), `implements(T, "TraitName")` (checks both intrinsic numeric traits and user-defined equip blocks), `field_names(T)` / `field_count(T)` / `has_field(T,"name")` / `field_type(T,"name")` (struct reflection), `variant_names(T)` / `variant_count(T)` (enum reflection). **MetaValue::List**: New variant added to support list-returning builtins (`field_names`/`variant_names`); `meta for x in field_names(T):` now works, binding each field name as a MetaValue::Str usable inside nested meta if/match. **`meta while`**: New `Stmt::MetaWhile { condition, body }` AST node; parser (`parse_meta_while_stmt`), all 13 walker arms, and delayed evaluator with 100k iteration safety limit. Primary use: guard pattern where condition is false for instantiated types (0 iterations). **DelayedMetaContext** extended with `trait_registry` and `type_registry` references for reflection queries. **Test fixtures**: `meta_numeric_meta.gg`, `meta_implements.gg`, `meta_while.gg`, `meta_reflection.gg`. 660 unit + 458 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Codegen bug: `return` inside `if` inside match arm emitted `goto if_end` instead of function return**: Root cause in `build_if_chain_expr` (`src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`): after calling `lower_block_expr` on each branch body, it unconditionally emitted `assign(result_id, val)` + `jump(merge_bb)`, overwriting the `ret` terminator that `lower_return` had just set via `set_terminator`. Fix: guard each branch's post-body emit with `if !builder.is_terminated()`. Also fixed the related double-drop in `lower_if` (`src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`): replaced the `block_always_returns` check (conservative, AST-level) with `builder.is_terminated()` (precise, GIR-level) for all three branch types (then/elif/else), mirroring the pattern already used in `lower_match_stmt`. This prevents `pop_scope` from emitting drop instructions that `emit_early_exit_drops` already emitted inside `lower_return`. Removed the now-dead `block_always_returns` helper. Regression test: `tests/fixtures/return_in_if_in_match.gg`. 454 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`std.datetime`: `dt_days_from_epoch` pre-epoch bug**: The while loop `while dt_y < dt_year` in `lib/std/datetime.gg` never executed for years < 1970 (loop starts at `dt_y=1970`, condition `dt_y < dt_year` is false for e.g. 1969). Fix: split into two branches — for `dt_year >= 1970`, count days forward as before; for `dt_year < 1970`, count backward by subtracting each year's day count from the epoch, starting at `dt_year` and walking up to 1969 inclusive. Then add `day_of_year - 1` in both cases. Verified: `dt_days_from_epoch(1969, 12, 31) = -1`, `(1969, 1, 1) = -365`, `(1968, 12, 31) = -366`, `(1968, 1, 1) = -731`. `to_epoch()` and round-trip through `from_epoch_utc` also correct for pre-epoch dates. Added pre-epoch test cases to `tests/fixtures/datetime_extended.gg`.

- [2026-03-03] **DataFrame: `meta match` + TypedColumn[T] refactor + `df_cast`/`df_clip`**: Three-part improvement to the Gorget dataframe library. **Part 1 — `meta match` statement**: Added `meta match <expr>: case <val>: ... else: ...` as a cleaner alternative to chained `meta if` for compile-time multi-way type dispatch inside generic function bodies. Added `Stmt::MetaMatch` AST variant, parser dispatch, walker arms in all 13 relevant files, `meta_values_eq` helper, and evaluator arm in `evaluate_delayed_meta_block`. Test fixture: `meta_delayed_match.gg`. **Part 2 — TypedColumn[T] refactoring**: Introduced `struct TypedColumn[T]` and 6 generic inner helpers (`col_slice_inner`, `col_filter_inner`, `col_gather_inner`, `col_gather_nullable_inner`, `col_compare_inner`, `value_as_str_inner`) to eliminate ~400 lines of 4-way type-dispatch boilerplate in `lib/gg/dataframe.gg`. Root cause fix: `map_type_with_subs` in `src/ir/lowering/context.rs` had a condition `!self.type_name_subs.is_empty()` that blocked fragment substitution for generic params (e.g., `Vector[T]` resolved to UNIT_TYPE); widened to `|| !self.generic_fragment_subs.is_empty()`. **Part 3 — `df_cast`/`df_clip`**: Added `df_cast(DataFrame, str col_name, str target_type) -> Result[DataFrame, str]` (int↔float↔str↔bool conversions; null propagation; error on unknown column or type) and `df_clip(DataFrame, str col_name, float lo, float hi) -> DataFrame` (clamp numeric values; preserve nulls; unchanged for non-numeric columns). Used per-type cast helper pattern and `df_replace_col` to work around the existing compiler bug where `return` inside `if` inside `match` arm generates incorrect goto. Test fixtures: `dataframe_cast.gg`, `dataframe_clip.gg`. All 16 dataframe integration tests pass. 453 total integration tests.

- [2026-03-03] **Delayed meta: Zig-style comptime in generic bodies (Phase 1+2)**: Added `meta if` and `meta for` statement constructs inside generic function bodies, evaluated at monomorphization time (when type parameters are concrete). Previously, meta evaluated only at Phase 0 (before semantic analysis), making it impossible to branch on generic type params. **New syntax**: `meta if typename(T) == "int": ... elif typename(T) == "float": ... else: ...` and `meta for i in 0..N: ...` inside generic functions/methods. **Implementation**: Added `Stmt::MetaIf` and `Stmt::MetaFor` AST variants, parser dispatch for the `meta` keyword in function bodies (`parse_meta_stmt`/`parse_meta_if_stmt`/`parse_meta_for_stmt`), walker arms in visitor/loader/formatter/generics/stmts; fixed resolver/typechecker/borrow-checker/rewriter to skip regular name resolution/type-inference on meta conditions (they're evaluated at monomorphization time, not runtime); added `DelayedMetaContext` struct, `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` (in-place splice with `Vec::splice`), and `eval_delayed_expr` (recurses through BinaryOp/UnaryOp, resolves `typename(T)` and `sizeof(T)` via type_subs, delegates to Phase 0 evaluator for literals/constants) in `src/semantic/meta.rs`; hooked into `lower_generic_function` in `src/ir/lowering/functions.rs` to evaluate delayed meta on a cloned template before GIR lowering. **Reflection builtins**: `typename(T)` resolves generic params to concrete names ("int", "float", "str", etc.); `sizeof(T)` resolves generic params then queries primitive size table. **Test fixtures**: `meta_delayed_basic.gg` (typename dispatch → integer/float/string/other), `meta_delayed_for.gg` (meta for unrolling + nested meta if), `meta_delayed_nested.gg` (nested meta if + sizeof). Files modified: `src/parser/ast.rs`, `src/parser/stmt.rs`, `src/parser/visitor.rs`, `src/parser/expr.rs`, `src/loader.rs`, `src/formatter/mod.rs`, `src/semantic/meta.rs`, `src/semantic/resolve.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/semantic/rewrite.rs`, `src/semantic/borrow.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/generics.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/functions.rs`, `tests/integration.rs`. 660 unit + 450 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.dataframe` Phase 1+2: Bugs/correctness + Tier 2 features**: Fixed 10 correctness bugs and added 15+ new features to the dataframe library. **Phase 1 (bugs):** (1.1) Replaced O(n²) bubble sort with O(n log n) bottom-up merge sort using direct typed column comparison. (1.2) Fixed `sort_by` destroying column types — now uses `col_gather` to reorder by index preserving types/nulls. (1.3) Fixed `df_inner_join` destroying column types — same `col_gather` fix. (1.4) Fixed `df_concat` destroying types/nulls — replaced string round-trip with new `col_concat` function. (1.5) Fixed `apply_int/float/str` ignoring null masks — now checks `is_null` before applying, carries forward original mask. (1.6) Fixed `apply_*/fillna_*` changing column order — replaced `drop().add_column()` with new `replace_column` method. (1.7) Fixed `filter_by` string comparison issues — now dispatches on column dtype for typed comparison. (1.8) Added missing `col_bools_null` constructor. (1.9) Fixed `GroupBy.agg` coercing everything through float — now produces typed IntColumn/FloatColumn output. (1.10) Fixed `value_counts` O(n²) → O(n) using `Dict[str, int]`. **Phase 2 (features):** Added `rename_column`, `dtypes`, `isna`/`notna`, `df_unique`/`df_nunique`, `df_median`, `df_var`, `nlargest`/`nsmallest`, `sample`/`shuffle`, `sort_by_multi` (multi-column sort), `col_add`/`col_sub`/`col_mul` (column arithmetic), `df_left_join`/`df_right_join`/`df_outer_join`/`df_cross_join`, `df_group_by_multi` (multi-column groupby), `df_from_records_typed` (type-inferring records constructor). Infrastructure: `col_gather`, `col_concat`, `col_gather_nullable`, `replace_column`, `col_compare`, `merge_sort_idxs`. 4 new test fixtures (dataframe_tier2_basic/sort_arith/joins/groupby). 660 unit + 14 dataframe integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`mod` operator (true modulo) alongside `%` (remainder)**: Added `mod` keyword operator with true modulo semantics (result sign matches divisor), keeping `%` as C-style remainder (result sign matches dividend). Renamed `BinaryOp::Mod` → `BinaryOp::Rem` throughout AST/IR/codegen for naming consistency, added new `BinaryOp::Mod` for the keyword operator. Added `Mod[Out]` trait with `mod(self, Self rhs)` method alongside existing `Rem[Out]`. Extended `Numeric` composite trait to include `Mod`. Wired through all compiler stages: lexer (`mod` keyword), parser (same precedence as `%`), type checker, semantic analysis, IR lowering, constant folding (with correct modulo formula), C backend (inline sign-correction code for int/float), simulator, formatter, and self-hosting resolver fixture. Added `mod_rem.gg` integration test covering negative operand combinations for both operators. Updated language-reference.md. 660 unit + 443 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`Numeric` trait + `One` trait + Tensor library deduplication**: Implemented a 6-phase plan to add composite `Numeric` trait (`Add + Sub + Mul + Div + Rem + Neg + Comparable + Default + One`), `One` identity trait, and intrinsic numeric trait satisfaction for all numeric primitives. Wired `T.one()` through all backend layers (type checker, IR lowering, C backend, simulator). Fixed `T.method()` resolution in generic function bodies (pre-existing bug where `T.default()` / `T.parse()` failed inside monomorphized bodies). Added trait bound propagation so `[Numeric T]` functions can call other `[Numeric T]` functions with `T` transitively. Fixed generic collector to skip unresolved type parameters during discovery and added transitive discovery phase for nested generic calls. Fixed generic function parameter drop elaboration (used `register_local` instead of `register_param`, causing double-free of borrowed GorgetArray params). Migrated tensor library from 26 duplicated `_int`/`_float` function pairs (~1109 lines) to single generic `[Numeric T]` functions (~733 lines). Updated all 8 tensor test fixtures. 660 unit + 442 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Targeted integration tests for `gg.csv`, `gg.xml`, `gg.http` coverage gaps**: Added 3 new fixture files (`csv_edge.gg`, `xml_roundtrip.gg`, `http_urls.gg`) and wired them into `integration.rs` (440 total). `csv_edge` covers `has_column`/`column_index` (true/false), unterminated-quote error path, tricky roundtrip with embedded delimiter and `""` double-quote escaping, and empty-input `Ok`. `xml_roundtrip` covers entity encoding in text (`&lt;` `&amp;` `&gt;`) and attributes (`&amp;` `&quot;`) across stringify→re-parse roundtrips, mismatched-closing-tag error, processing-instruction skip (`<?foo?>`), and unterminated-comment error. `http_urls` covers `http_parse_url` (https default port 443, http explicit port, no-path default `/`, port + root path) and `http_parse_hex` (lower/upper/decimal/zero). **Also fixed two bugs**: (1) `src/main.rs` linker-flag detection used `source` (user file only) instead of `concat_source` (full transitive imports) — caused `gg.http`'s transitive `std.net.tls` dependency to be missed, so `-lssl`/`-lcrypto` were not added. (2) `gorget_try_parse_int` codegen passed non-null-terminated `Str.data` pointer to `strtoll`, causing port parse to fail on substrings like `"8080/path..."`. Added `gorget_try_parse_int_n(const char* s, size_t n)` length-bounded variant to runtime, and updated codegen to use it for `Str` operands. 658/658 unit + 440/440 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Fix `int.parse()` / `float.parse()` on non-null-terminated `Str` slices**: `gorget_try_parse_int` and `gorget_try_parse_float` used `*endptr != '\0'` as the termination check, which fails when the `Str` pointer comes from `byte_slice()` (interior pointer into a larger allocation, not null-terminated). Example: `int.parse("18086")` on the port substring of `"127.0.0.1:18086/hello"` saw the trailing "/" and returned `None`, so `http_parse_url` fell back to port 80, causing all HTTP client requests to connect to the wrong port. Fix: updated both parse functions to accept `(const char* s, size_t len)` and check `(size_t)(endptr - s) == len` instead of null-termination. Updated `bool.parse()` to use `strncmp + length check` instead of `strcmp`. Updated `extract_str_data_len` closure in the codegen to emit `_N.data, _N.len` for `Str`/`GorgetString` operands (string literals use the literal length). This was the root cause of the `httpserver_e2e` hang: the client connected to port 80 instead of 18086, causing "Connection refused" for all requests; `serve_n` then blocked in `accept()` forever waiting for connections that never arrived. Added `tests/fixtures/httpserver_e2e.gg` end-to-end test (gg.http client vs gg.httpserver over real TCP: GET/POST/404/custom headers/query strings). All 658 unit + 447 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.httpserver` V2 — Phase D (TLS HTTPS server)**: Added `GorgetTlsServerSocket` C runtime (`TLS_SERVER_RUNTIME`): struct `{int fd; SSL_CTX* ctx}`, `gorget_tls_server_bind` (SSL_CTX_new + cert/key load + bind/listen), `gorget_tls_server_accept` (accept + SSL_new + SSL_accept, returns `GorgetTlsSocket` with `ctx=NULL`), `gorget_tls_server_close`. Added `gorget_tls_set_timeout` to `TLS_SOCKET_RUNTIME`. Extended `gen_tls_socket_module()` in `stdlib.rs`: added `TlsServerSocket` opaque struct, `tls_server_bind(host, port, cert_path, key_path) → Result[TlsServerSocket, str]`, `TlsServerSocket.accept()` and `TlsServerSocket.close()`, and `TlsSocket.set_timeout(ms)`. Wired in `mod.rs`: KNOWN_MAPPINGS (`tls_server_bind`, `TlsServerSocket__accept`, `TlsServerSocket__close`, `TlsSocket__set_timeout`), `is_cstr_param_fn` (`gorget_tls_server_bind`), `runtime_type_name` + `collection_type_alias` (`TlsServerSocket → GorgetTlsServerSocket`), preamble emission (TLS_SERVER_RUNTIME after TLS_SOCKET_RUNTIME when `needs_tls_server`), `last_error_fn` (TlsServerSocket check before TlsSocket to avoid `gorget_tls_server_` prefix collision). Added `needs_tls: bool` to `GirCodegenOutput` and updated `main.rs` to add `-lssl -lcrypto` when `gir_output.needs_tls` (fixes linker errors when TLS comes from imported library modules, not user source). Added TLS connection functions to `lib/gg/httpserver.gg`: `http_server_parse_request_tls`, `http_server_write_response_tls_ka`, `http_server_handle_conn_tls_ka`. Extended `HttpServer` struct with `use_tls/cert_path/key_path`, added `HttpServer.new_tls` constructor, split `serve()` into `serve_plain()` + `serve_tls()`. Added `httpserver_tls.gg` smoke test (compile + field access, no live TLS connection). All 446 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.httpserver` V2 — Phases C4 + E (Middleware, Static files) + Vector[Callable] codegen fix**: (A) **Phase C4 (Middleware)**: Added `middlewares: Vector[Callable[HttpServerResponse(HttpRequest, HttpServerResponse)]]` field to `Router`. Added `Router.use(hook)` method and split `dispatch` into `dispatch_inner` (routing only) + `dispatch` (applies middleware pipeline after route match). Hooks receive the original request and current response, returning the (possibly modified) response.
