- [2026-02-19] std.yaml Phase 2 — Multi-line Block Scalars. Added `parse_block_scalar()` method to `equip YamlParser` supporting literal (`|`) and folded (`>`) block scalars with full chomping indicators (clip/strip `-`/keep `+`) and optional explicit indent width (`1`-`9`). Literal mode preserves newlines exactly; folded mode joins consecutive non-blank content-indent lines with spaces, preserves blank lines as paragraph breaks, and preserves more-indented lines literally. Extended `parse_plain_scalar()` for multi-line continuation — continuation lines (indented deeper than `min_indent`, not mapping keys or sequence items) are folded with spaces; multi-line plain scalars always resolve as strings (skip `yaml_resolve_scalar()`). Added `|`/`>` dispatch in both `parse_block_value()` and `parse_inline_value()`. Updated `yaml_pretty_internal()` to emit multi-line strings as literal block scalars (`|`) with proper indentation. Added `yaml_str_contains_newline()` helper. Integration test covers: literal block scalar, folded block scalar, strip/keep chomping, multi-line plain scalar, block scalar in sequence, folded with more-indented lines, and pretty-print round-trip with block scalars. Files: `lib/std/yaml.gg`, `tests/fixtures/yaml_parse.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `TODO.md`, `DONE.md`. All 479 unit + 249 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Implement `.enumerate()` for for-loops. Syntactic sugar that exposes a sequential index via tuple destructuring: `for (i, elem) in items.enumerate():`. Zero-cost universal counter approach — detects `.enumerate()` MethodCall in `gen_for_loop()`, unwraps to inner iterable, emits a `size_t` counter before the loop, and binds `int64_t idx = (int64_t)counter++` after element binding. Works for all iterable types (Vector, str, Set, Iterable trait, etc.) because the counter increments only when a valid element is reached (map/set `continue` for deleted entries happens before binding). Type checker extended to assign proper types to enumerate tuple pattern bindings (int for index, element type for value) — also fixes the broader issue that for-loop variables over Vector/Set didn't have type_id assigned, which would cause wrong printf format specifiers in string interpolation. Files: `typecheck.rs` (enumerate in `builtin_method_type()` + pattern type assignment in `Stmt::For`), `c_stmt.rs` (enumerate detection + counter in `gen_for_loop()`), `enumerate.gg` + `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 248 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] std.yaml Phase 1 — Core YAML 1.2 parser. Pure Gorget implementation (`lib/std/yaml.gg`, ~700 lines) following the same file-based module pattern as json/toml/xml. `enum Yaml: Null, Bool(bool), Int(int), Float(float), Str(str), Seq(Vector[Yaml]), Map(Dict[str, Yaml])`. Recursive-descent parser with indentation-tracked block collections (mapping/sequence), flow collections (`{}`/`[]`), double/single-quoted strings, plain scalars with auto-resolution (null/~/true/false, decimal/hex/octal/binary integers, floats including .inf/.nan), `#` comments (full-line and inline), `---`/`...` document markers. Public API: `yaml_parse(str) -> Result[Yaml, str]`, `yaml_stringify(Yaml) -> str` (compact flow-style), `yaml_pretty(Yaml) -> str` (block-style indent=2). Full accessor method suite matching `equip Json`: `get`, `at`, `has`, `has_index`, `as_str`, `as_int`, `as_float`, `as_bool`, `is_null`, `is_object`, `is_array`, `is_string`, `is_number`, `is_bool`, `len`, `keys`. All variable names use `yaml_` prefix to avoid `lookup_by_name_anywhere` cross-module collisions. Registered in `stdlib.rs` (3 insertion points: `is_stdlib_module`, `generate_stdlib_module`, `stdlib_module_source`). 4 new unit tests (is_stdlib_yaml, generate_yaml_returns_none, yaml_module_source_exists, yaml_source_parses). Integration test `yaml_parse.gg` covering block/flow collections, nested structures, quoted scalars, scalar resolution, comments, document markers, stringify, pretty print, all accessors, and error cases. Files: `lib/std/yaml.gg` (new), `src/stdlib.rs`, `tests/fixtures/yaml_parse.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 248 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Add `Option.flatten()` method. `Option[Option[T]].flatten() → Option[T]` — unwraps one nesting layer. Type checker (`builtin_method_type()`) inspects `elem_type()` for nested `ResolvedType::Generic(Option_DefId, _)` and returns the inner Option TypeId directly. Codegen (`gen_option_method()`) uses `infer_generic_type_args(receiver)` to get the inner Option mangled name and emits: if outer is Some, return inner value; if outer is None, construct inner None. Type inference (`c_expr_generic.rs`) adds `flatten` to Option-returning method list for method chain support. Note: nested generic enum constructors like `Some(Some(100))` have a pre-existing bug where inner constructors resolve using the outer `decl_type_hint` — workaround is constructing inner Options as separate variables. Files: `typecheck.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `option_result_combinators.gg`, `integration.rs`, `language-reference.md`. All 475 unit + 247 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 23: Pre-Drop Old Values on Collection `set()`/`put()` Overwrite. When `Vector.set(i, val)` or `Dict/HashMap.put(key, val)` overwrites an existing element, the old value's non-Copy fields (String, Vector, Dict, Set, structs containing them) are now properly dropped before overwriting. Added `gen_inline_drop_code()` — a string-based counterpart to `emit_drop_entry()` that generates inline C drop statements without CEmitter, handling all DropAction variants recursively with `__pdi{depth}`/`__pde{depth}` loop variables. Added `compute_predrop_code()` parallel to `compute_push_clone_code()` — dispatches by C type name: direct collection types get buffer-only frees, struct types get full StructDrop with user drop + recursive field drops via `drop_action_for_struct_name()`. Vector `set()`: extracts old element with bounds check (`(size_t)__set_idx < recv.len`) before `gorget_array_set()`. Dict/HashMap `put()`: uses existing `__get_ptr()` to locate old value slot before `__put()`. Known limitation: nested element drops for direct collection values (e.g., `Vector[Vector[String]]`) only free outer buffer, not inner elements — tracked in TODO. Files: `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `tests/fixtures/drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 247 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Option[T] standardization batch 2: `Vector.pop()`, `Vector.remove()` → `Option[T]`; `Vector.index_of()`, `str.index_of()` → `Option[int]`. Same four-layer pattern as batch 1 (.get()): semantic return types in `builtin_method_type()`, codegen with `register_generic("Option", ...)` and bounds/sentinel checks, type inference in `builtin_method_return_type()` and `infer_generic_enum_mangled_name()`, receiver type mapping in `c_expr_generic.rs`. Pop codegen saves element before decrementing len (GORGET_ARRAY_AT bounds check). Remove codegen validates index in-range before extracting element. Index_of wraps the existing `gorget_array_index_of`/`gorget_string_index_of` sentinel (-1) in Option. Updated ~20 caller sites across stdlib (json.gg, ecs.gg, http.gg) and examples (md2html.gg, ecs/entity.gg) — value-capturing calls add `.unwrap()`, sentinel checks rewritten to `Option[int]` + `.is_some()`/`.unwrap()`. Void-context pop/remove calls (agentic_loop, snake, sparse_set) unchanged. Files: `typecheck.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `language-reference.md`, 6 test fixtures, 3 stdlib .gg files, 2 example .gg files, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 246 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 22: Deep-Clone on Collection Write/Read + Full Element Drops. Removed the `safe_element_drop()` safety filter that stripped collection-field drops from struct elements stored in collections (e.g., `Vector[TomlSection]` where `TomlSection` has Dict/Vector fields). Elements now get full drops including nested collection cleanup. To prevent double-frees from shallow copies, deep-clone infrastructure ensures collection elements independently own their buffers. **Write-side (push/set/put):** `source_is_safe_for_push()` checks whether the source expression will be move-zeroed (Identifier with drops, FieldAccess with zero target) or is a fresh allocation (function return, constructor call). Unsafe sources get deep-cloned via `compute_push_clone_code()` before insertion. **Read-side (.get()):** `.get()` deep-clones non-Copy elements, returning an independently-owned copy. The `deep_cloned_expr` flag signals VarDecl to register user Drop only (not field drops) to avoid double-frees when the deep-cloned result's fields are shallow-copied elsewhere (the TOML parser's get→modify→set pattern). **Clone infrastructure:** `gorget_string_clone()`, `gorget_set_clone()` in c_runtime.rs; `gorget_array_clone()` already existed; `{mangled}__clone()` for each monomorphized Dict/HashMap type in c_item.rs. `CloneAction` enum (`ArrayClone`, `StringClone`, `MapClone(String)`, `SetClone`) drives `compute_clone_fields()` which inspects struct field types to determine per-field clone actions. Known limitations: deep-cloned `.get()` results' collection-field buffers leak if not consumed (tracked in TODO); `set()`/`put()` doesn't pre-drop old values (tracked in TODO). Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `codegen/c_item.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `tests/fixtures/drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 246 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Complete Option/Result method suite. Type checker: registered `map`, `and_then`, `or_else`, `filter`, `or`, `unwrap_or_else` for Option and `map`, `and_then`, `or_else`, `or`, `map_err`, `unwrap_or_else`, `unwrap_err` for Result in `builtin_method_type()`. Codegen: added `unwrap_or_else` (calls closure on None/Error), `filter` (turns Some to None if predicate fails), `or` (returns self if present, else alternative) for Option; `unwrap_or_else` (calls closure with error value on Error), `unwrap_err` (extracts error, panics on Ok), `map_err` (transforms error type), `or` (returns self if Ok, else alternative) for Result. Bonus fixes: (1) `infer_receiver_type` in `c_expr_generic.rs` now recognizes Option/Result combinator methods as returning the same type, enabling method chaining like `.or(x).unwrap()`. (2) Fixed `is_some`/`is_none`/`is_ok`/`is_err` operator precedence bug — non-temp comparison expressions now wrapped in parens to prevent cast misapplication in print interpolation. Deferred: `Option.flatten()` (needs nested generic type extraction), cross-type `map`/`map_err` type inference (needs closure body type in type checker). Files: `typecheck.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `option_result_combinators.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 247 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 21: Extended Field-Level Move-Zeroing (self.field + chained access). Replaced the hard-coded `Identifier`-only logic in `queue_field_move_zero()` with a recursive `resolve_field_zero_target()` helper that handles three cases: (1) `Identifier` — preserves identical behavior from Phase 20; (2) `SelfExpr` — enables `self.field` zeroing in mutable equip methods (`&self` / `!self`), generating `memset(&self->field, 0, ...)` to prevent double-free when extracting fields; (3) chained `FieldAccess` — resolves `obj.a.b` chains recursively, building the full C access path and type-checking at each level. Added `self_is_mutable: bool` field to `CodegenContext`, set from self parameter ownership in both `emit_function_def` (regular methods) and `emit_monomorphized_equip_method` (generic instantiations), with proper save/restore alongside `pointer_params`. Safety gates preserved: pointer params rejected, structs with user Drop rejected, only non-Copy fields zeroed. Also fixed pre-existing ownership violation in `LinkedList` and `SparseSet[T]` iterators — both created aliased non-Copy `Vector[int]` copies (shallow copy sharing the same buffer). Replaced with collect-into-fresh-vector pattern: `iter()` now traverses the data structure and populates a new `Vector[int]` with copied element values, giving the iterator sole ownership of its buffer. Extended `drop_field_move_zero.gg` with two new test cases: self.field extraction via `take_payload(&self)` method, and chained access `o.nested.items`. Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_item.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `tests/fixtures/drop_field_move_zero.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `tests/fixtures/linked_list.gg`, `examples/linked_list.gg`, `lib/std/ecs.gg`, `examples/ecs/sparse_set.gg`. All 475 unit + 245 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Extend `String` with polymorphic `push()` and `push_line()`, plus capacity constructor `String(int)`. `push()` dispatches at codegen time based on argument type: `str`/`String` → `gorget_string_append`, `int` → `gorget_string_push_int`, `float` → `gorget_string_push_float`, `char` → `gorget_string_push_char`, `bool` → `gorget_string_push_bool`. `push_line()` mirrors the same dispatch with `\n` appended. No type-specific method names needed — one method handles all primitive types. C runtime: `gorget_string_with_capacity`, `gorget_string_push_int`, `gorget_string_push_float`, `gorget_string_push_bool`, `gorget_string_push_line` + typed variants. Constructor overload in `c_expr_call.rs` detects integer arg for capacity. Files: `c_runtime.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs`, `string_builder.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 246 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 20: Field-Level Move-Zeroing. When a non-Copy struct field is consumed (e.g., `items.push(h.data)` or `Vector[int] v = h.data`), the source field is now zeroed via `memset(&obj.field, 0, sizeof(obj.field))`, making the copy the sole owner. Previously, both the copy and the struct's scope-exit drop would free the same buffer → double-free. Implementation reuses existing `pending_move_zeros` + `flush_move_zeros()` infrastructure — pushing `"h.data"` generates valid C `memset(&h.data, 0, ...)`. Added `queue_field_move_zero()` helper with safety gates: only fires for simple Identifier objects, non-pointer-params, structs without user Drop (partial zeroing + user Drop = UB), and non-Copy field types. Extended all 4 consuming-site queue functions in `c_expr_call.rs` with `FieldAccess` branches alongside existing `Identifier` checks. Extended `Expr::Move` handler, Return handler (direct field access returns), and VarDecl drop registration (field-access RHS now gets full drops when source field is zeroed, instead of being skipped as "aliased buffer"). New integration test `drop_field_move_zero.gg` validates: push-field-into-collection (was double-free) and assign-field-to-variable (was leak). Files: `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `codegen/c_expr.rs`, `drop_field_move_zero.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 245 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Interactive slash-command menu for TUI. Replaced cooked-mode `BufRead::read_line()` with raw-mode line editor using `crossterm` for key events. New `src/tui.rs` module with `read_line(prompt, show_menu) -> ReadLineResult` — enters raw mode with RAII `RawModeGuard` (Drop-based cleanup), handles full line editing (cursor movement, insert at cursor, backspace, delete, Home/End), and a box-drawn slash-command menu that appears above the prompt when `/` is typed at position 0. Menu filters as the user types, arrow keys navigate (with wrapping), Enter selects and executes, Tab fills without submitting, Escape dismisses. Ctrl+D = EOF on empty line, Ctrl+C = clear line. Menu rendered via full-redraw approach with `prev_render_lines` tracking and `ensure_menu_space()` for terminal scroll management. Six commands: `/run`, `/check`, `/show`, `/reset`, `/help`, `/quit`. Continuation prompt (`"... "`) uses `show_menu: false` for line editing only. Refactored `run_tui()` in main.rs to use `tui::read_line()`, removed `stdin.lock()` / `BufRead` import. Files: `Cargo.toml` (+crossterm 0.28), `src/tui.rs` (new), `src/main.rs`. All 475 unit + 244 integration tests pass.

Semantic layer: `builtin_method_type()` in typecheck.rs returns `Option[elem_type]` with `expr_types` insertion for codegen. Updated ~120 `.get()` call sites across stdlib (bytes, json, toml, xml, ssh, http, ecs), test fixtures, and examples with `.unwrap()`. Rewrote `bounds_check.gg` to test Option behavior. - [2026-02-18] Complete String→str coercion in all intercepted stdlib calls. Changed `gen_expr()` to `gen_str_arg()` for every `str`-typed parameter across all intercepted stdlib function calls in `c_expr_call.rs`. Previously only `parse_time` and `format_time` had this fix; now covers 35+ calls: filesystem ops (`read_file`, `write_file`, `append_file`, `file_exists`, `delete_file`, `mkdir`, `rmdir`, `rename`, `copy_file`, `file_size`, `is_dir`), path ops (`path_parent`, `path_basename`, `path_extension`, `path_stem`, `path_join`), OS ops (`getenv`, `setenv`, `readdir`), conversions (`parse_int`, `parse_float`), I/O (`input`, `exec`, `exec_output`), SDL string args (`sdl_create_window` title, `sdl_load_texture` path, `sdl_load_font` path, `sdl_render_text`/`sdl_draw_text` text, `sdl_text_width`/`sdl_text_height` text), crypto (`crypto_hmac` algo), networking (`socket_connect`/`tls_connect` host), and `File.open`/`File.create` path (both call sites). `gen_str_arg()` checks if the argument expression is `GorgetString` and coerces via `.data`; otherwise passes through unchanged. This prevents C compiler errors when users pass `String` values to intercepted stdlib calls. File: `c_expr_call.rs`. All 475 unit + 243 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Add math constants to `std.math`: `PI`, `E`, `TAU`, `INFINITY`, `NAN`. Constants are declared as `ConstDecl` items in the synthetic module (alongside existing function declarations) and mapped to `GORGET_*` C runtime constants via the existing `is_stdlib_const()` → `GORGET_{name}` codegen path. `PI`, `E`, `TAU` use `static const double`; `INFINITY` and `NAN` use `#define` to delegate to `<math.h>` macros. Refactored `gen_math_module()` to build items directly (mixed `ConstDecl` + `Function` items) instead of using `make_module()` which only accepts `FunctionDef`. New integration test `math_constants.gg` verifying values, arithmetic with constants, and trigonometric identity. - [2026-02-18] Convert REPL to TUI with `/run` and `/check` commands. Renamed `run_repl()` → `run_tui()`, decoupled input from execution — users now type code freely (appended to `definitions` or `statements` immediately) and explicitly compile/run with `/run` or check for errors with `/check`. Removed the marker system (counter, `__GORGET_REPL_MARKER_N__`, output filtering) and clone-then-swap pattern (`new_defs`/`new_stmts`). Extracted `generate_tui_source()` helper that both commands share. `/run` compiles and executes, printing all stdout/stderr. `/check` compiles only, printing "OK: no errors" on success. Updated welcome message, `/help` output, temp directory name (`gorget_tui`), and CLI help text. Files: `main.rs`, `TODO.md`, `DONE.md`.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 19: Move-Guard + Partial Element Drops for Structs with Collection Fields. Two focused changes: (1) Added zero-guard to user Drop calls in `emit_drop_entry()` — wraps `StructDrop` user Drop calls in `memcmp(&var, &zero, sizeof(var)) != 0` check using `__typeof__` block scope. Move-zeroed structs (via `.push()`, etc.) now correctly skip their user Drop at scope exit instead of calling it with NULL/zero fields (previously printed "drop (null)"). (2) Changed `safe_element_drop()` from full filtering to partial stripping — struct elements with collection fields now preserve user Drop + non-collection field drops, stripping only collection field drops (aliasing-unsafe). Previously the entire `StructDrop` was filtered to `None`. Types with user Drop but no non-collection fields (like `Container` with `str label` + `Vector[int] data`) now get `StructDrop { user_drop: true, field_drops: [] }`. Types without user Drop and only collection fields (like `TomlSection`) still filter to `None` — TOML aliasing chain remains safe. New integration test `drop_struct_collection_fields.gg` validates: Vector[Container] element drops fire user Drop ("drop first", "drop second") while move-zeroed local variables skip user Drop (no "drop (null)" output). Files: `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 243 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 18: Collection Drops for Function/Method-Returned Collections. Expanded the drop registration gate beyond constructor-initialized collections to also cover collections returned by function and method calls. Added `DropAction::buffer_only()` method that strips element/key/value drops while preserving buffer-level cleanup — safe because function/method-returned collection elements may be shallow-copied from the source (e.g., `.keys()` copies key structs sharing internal pointers). Added `is_fresh_allocation_source()` predicate: accepts all `Expr::Call` (user function calls return freshly-constructed collections) and a whitelist of `Expr::MethodCall` methods known to allocate fresh buffers (`keys`, `values`, `items`, `filter`, `map`, `sorted`, `slice`, `split`, `union`, `intersection`, `difference`). Methods like `.get()` and `.unwrap()` are excluded — they return shallow copies that share the source's buffer. Updated VarDecl drop registration from two-tier to three-tier logic: (1) non-collection → full drops always, (2) constructor-initialized → full drops with element iteration, (3) fresh allocation source → buffer-only drops, (4) otherwise (variable copy/field access) → no drop. The whitelist approach was necessary because accepting all method calls caused double-frees in `nested_generics.gg` where `.get()` on `Vector[Vector[int]]` returns an aliased inner vector. Known limitation: function-returned collections of non-Copy elements leak element destructors (buffer freed but elements not iterated). Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `drop_fn_return_collection.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 241 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Fix memory leaks in 12 stdlib functions returning malloc'd strings as `str`. Changed return types from `ty_str()` (non-owning `const char*`) to `ty_string()` (owning `GorgetString`) for: `read_file`, `path_join`, `path_parent`, `path_basename`, `path_extension`, `path_stem`, `getcwd`, `readline`, `input`, `format_time`, and extern methods `File.read_all`, `Socket.read_line`, `TlsSocket.read_line`. Free functions wrapped in `gorget_string_adopt()` in codegen; extern methods changed to return `GorgetString` directly from C. Fixed UB in path functions where `gorget_string_adopt()` was called on string literals (`"."`, `"/"`, `""`) — `free()` on read-only memory. Changed all literal returns to `strdup()`. Also fixed pre-existing memory leaks in `gorget_path_extension` and `gorget_path_stem` where `gorget_path_basename`'s malloc'd buffer wasn't freed on early returns. Added `gen_str_arg()` helper for coercing `String` arguments to `str` in intercepted stdlib calls (bypasses `param_type_ids` coercion). Files: `c_runtime.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `stdlib.rs`, `time_format.gg`. All 475 unit + 241 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Add date/time formatting to `std.time`. Added `format_time(int, str) -> str` (strftime wrapper via `gorget_format_time`) and `parse_time(str, str) -> int` (strptime wrapper via `gorget_parse_time`). `format_time` returns a human-readable string from epoch seconds using the given format pattern; `parse_time` returns epoch seconds from a date string or -1 on failure. Also fixed `escape_string` bug: split into `escape_string` (for regular string literals) and `escape_printf_string` (with `%` → `%%` for printf/sprintf contexts). Previously, all string literals had `%` doubled, breaking strftime format strings like `"%Y-%m-%d"` which became `"%%Y-%%m-%%d"`. Updated `c_expr_print.rs` to use `escape_printf_string` for printf/fprintf/gorget_format contexts. New integration test `time_format.gg`. Files: `c_runtime.rs`, `stdlib.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs`, `time_format.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 241 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 17: Element-Level Collection Drops. When a collection is destroyed, it now iterates non-Copy elements and runs each element's drop action before freeing the buffer. Extended `DropAction::ArrayFree`, `MapFree`, `SetFree` with element/key/value drop info: `ArrayFree { element_drop, element_c_type }`, `MapFree { mangled_name, key_drop, value_drop }`, `SetFree { element_drop, element_c_type }`. `drop_action_for_type()` recursively computes element drops for collections — `Vector[String]` gets `ArrayFree { element_drop: Some(StringFree), element_c_type: Some("GorgetString") }`, `Dict[str, String]` gets `MapFree { value_drop: Some(StringFree) }`, etc. `emit_drop_entry()` gains `depth: usize` parameter for unique loop variable names (`__di0`/`__de0`, `__di1`/`__de1`, ...) enabling nested collections like `Vector[Vector[String]]`. Element iteration: ArrayFree loops `0..len` casting `void*` elements to typed pointers; MapFree loops `0..cap` checking `states[i] == 1` for live entries; SetFree loops `0..cap` with state check and typed key cast. Safety gate: `safe_element_drop()` filters out `StructDrop` elements that `involves_collection()` — struct elements with collection fields are skipped because field-level move-zeroing isn't implemented yet, and shallow copies (e.g., passing Vector by C value) can alias inner collection buffers causing use-after-free (TOML module canonical example). Test-body cleanup (`emit_test_cleanup_for_action`) updated to match new struct variants but stays buffer-only (no element iteration in panic path). Extended `drop_collections.gg` with `Vector[Tracked]` (proving element destructors fire via print), `Vector[String]`, `Dict[str, String]`, and `Vector[Vector[String]]` (nested). Together with Phase 16's move-zeroing, this completes the ownership story for collection elements (except struct elements with collection fields — tracked separately). Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `drop_collections.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 240 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Add HTTP redirect following to `std.http`. Extracted single-request logic into `http_single()`, wrapped `http_request()` with a redirect loop (max 10 hops). 301/302/303 switch method to GET and drop body; 307/308 preserve original method and body. Handles both absolute and relative (path-only) Location headers. Returns error on >10 redirects, returns response as-is if no Location header present. File: `lib/std/http.gg`. All 475 unit + 240 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Fix memory leak in all String-returning string methods and conversion functions. Added `gorget_string_adopt(char* s)` to c_runtime.rs — wraps a freshly-malloc'd `char*` into a `GorgetString` without copying (zero-copy ownership transfer). Replaced `gorget_string_new(gorget_string_X(...))` with `gorget_string_adopt((char*)gorget_string_X(...))` at all 17 sites: 14 string methods in `c_expr_methods.rs` (trim, strip, lstrip, rstrip, to_upper, to_lower, replace, substring, repeat, join, removeprefix, removesuffix, pad_left, pad_right) and 4 conversion functions in `c_expr_call.rs` (int_to_str, float_to_str, char_to_str, codepoint_to_str). Previously each call leaked the intermediate malloc'd `const char*` because `gorget_string_new()` malloc'd again and copied. Files: `c_runtime.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`. All 474 unit + 240 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Refactor: Centralize move-zeroing via semantic ownership data. Consolidated 6 scattered move-zero sites across 3 files into 3 centralized helpers in `c_expr_call.rs`: (1) `builtin_consuming_arg_indices()` — static lookup table replacing hardcoded knowledge in 4 collection method arms (Vector.push/set, Dict.put, Set.add); (2) `queue_method_arg_move_zeros()` — two-tier hook called at top of `gen_method_call`, first checking the static table for built-in methods, then falling back to `method_resolutions → FunctionInfo.param_ownerships` for user-defined methods with `Ownership::Move` parameters; (3) `queue_constructor_move_zeros_call_args()`/`queue_constructor_move_zeros_exprs()` — constructor helpers replacing inline loops in enum variant, struct literal, and struct/newtype-via-gen_call codegen. Threaded `method_resolutions: FxHashMap<usize, DefId>` from typechecker through `AnalysisResult` into `CodegenContext` (previously discarded after borrow checker). Changed `gen_method_call` and `gen_generic_method_call` signatures from `method_name: &str` to `method: &Spanned<String>` to preserve span for `method_resolutions` lookup. Fixed latent newtype bug: `MyNewtype(droppable_value)` now queues move-zeroing (struct/newtype constructor path in `gen_call` was missing it). Removed 4 inline move-zero blocks from `c_expr_methods.rs`. Files: `semantic/mod.rs`, `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_expr.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_generic.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`. All 474 unit + 240 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Convert `std.bytes` to file-based module + add Base64 codec. Migrated `std.bytes` from synthetic (Rust-generated AST) to file-based (`lib/std/bytes.gg`) with 11 `extern fn = "c_symbol"` declarations for existing C-backed functions. Added pure Gorget `base64_encode(Vector[uint8]) -> str` and `base64_decode(str) -> Result[Vector[uint8], str]` implementing RFC 4648. Updated bytes runtime detection in `codegen/mod.rs` to use `FunctionBody::Extern` matching instead of `Span::dummy()` (file-based modules have real spans). All variable names use `b64_` prefix to avoid `lookup_by_name_anywhere` cross-module collisions. Files: `lib/std/bytes.gg` (new), `src/stdlib.rs`, `src/codegen/mod.rs`, `tests/fixtures/bytes_ops.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 16: Enable Collection Buffer Drops with Move-Zeroing. Activated `DropAction::ArrayFree`, `MapFree`, `SetFree` in `drop_action_for_type()` for Vector/List/Array, Dict, HashMap, Set/HashSet. Added move-zeroing infrastructure: `queue_move_zero_if_droppable()` queues droppable variables for post-statement `memset(&var, 0, sizeof(var))`, making scope-exit drops no-op (`free(NULL)` safe per C standard). Zeroing at consumption sites: `.push()` arg, `.put()` value, `.set()` value, `.add()` arg (collection methods in `c_expr_methods.rs`); enum variant constructor args (`c_expr_call.rs`); struct literal field args (`c_expr.rs`). Extended return handler with `collect_consumed_identifiers()` recursive tree walker that finds all identifiers consumed by calls/constructors/variants in return expressions (handles `return Arr(items)`, `return Tbl(dict)`, etc.). Key safety gate: `is_owning_collection_init()` restricts collection drops to constructor-initialized variables only (avoids aliased-buffer double-frees from copies, field access, or function returns). Non-collection drops (Box, File, user Drop) unaffected. `DropAction::involves_collection()` method distinguishes collection-related drops from non-collection ones, including recursive struct field inspection. Extended `drop_collections.gg` test with nested vector push and enum variant wrapping. Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_expr.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `drop_collections.gg`. All 474 unit + 240 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Restrict `str` from `&`/`!` parameter modes. Added `InvalidParameterMode` error variant to `SemanticErrorKind` that rejects mutable borrows (`&str`) and moves (`!str`) at declaration time — `str` is Copy, pointer-sized, and immutable, so these modes are semantically meaningless. Validation via `validate_str_param_modes()` in `resolve.rs`, called from three sites: top-level functions, equip methods, and trait method declarations. 3 new unit tests. Files: `errors.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `traits.rs`. All 474 unit + 240 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 15: Box[T] Deep Drops + Collection Drop Infrastructure. Replaced `DropAction::BoxFree` with `BoxDrop { inner_drop: Option<Box<DropAction>> }` — when `Box[T]` goes out of scope, the inner type's destructor runs on `*var` before `free(var)`. E.g., `Box[Tracked]` where `Tracked` has `equip with Drop` now calls `Drop_for_Tracked__drop(&(*b))` then `free(b)`. Added three new `DropAction` variants (`ArrayFree`, `MapFree { mangled_name }`, `SetFree`) with full `emit_drop_entry()` and `emit_test_cleanup_for_action()` handlers — ready for activation when move-zeroing infrastructure exists. Collection drops are NOT yet registered in `drop_action_for_type()` because collections are non-Copy value types with shallow copy semantics: `.push(v)`, `.put(k,v)`, `.get()`, and constructors create shared-buffer copies that would cause double-free on scope-exit drops. The enabling work (move-zeroing after consumption sites, non-owning `.get()` returns, recursive return expression walking) is tracked in TODO.md. New integration test `drop_collections.gg` validates Box deep drops with `Tracked` struct's Drop printing before free. Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `drop_collections.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 471 unit + 239 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Fix `print()` format for trait object method return types. `print(traitObj.method())` now emits the correct printf format (`%s`/`%lld`/`%f`/`%s` for str/int/float/bool) instead of always falling back to `%lld`. Root cause: `resolve_expr_type_id()` in `c_expr.rs` couldn't resolve method return types for trait object receivers because `infer_receiver_type()` returns `"Unknown"` for them — no equip block matched. Fix: after the equip block search fails, use `resolve_trait_object_type(receiver)` to detect trait objects, then look up the method's return type from `TraitInfo.methods` in `self.traits.traits`. Also walks the `extends` chain for inherited methods. Files: `c_expr.rs`, `print_trait_object.gg`, `integration.rs`.
- [2026-02-18] Struct constructor str↔String field coercion. When a struct has a `String` field but a `str` literal is passed (e.g., `Holder("hello")`), the codegen now emits `gorget_string_new("hello")` instead of the bare string literal. Reverse coercion (String→str field) also works via `.data`. The fix lives in two codegen paths: (1) `Expr::StructLiteral` in `c_expr.rs` — the primary path after the `rewrite.rs` pass converts `Expr::Call` to `Expr::StructLiteral` for struct constructors; (2) `gen_call` in `c_expr_call.rs` — the fallback path for newtypes (which aren't rewritten). Both paths look up field types from `field_type_names` and apply `coerce_arg_to_str()` per field. Also added newtype inner types to `field_type_names` in `generate_c()`. New integration test `struct_string_coerce.gg`. Files: `codegen/c_expr.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `codegen/mod.rs`, `tests/fixtures/struct_string_coerce.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 471 unit + 238 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Native HTTP client + agentic loop. Replaced libcurl-backed `std.http.client` with: (1) `std.net.tls` — synthetic module wrapping OpenSSL for TLS socket connections (`TlsSocket` struct with `read`/`write`/`read_line`/`read_exact`/`close`); (2) `std.http` — pure Gorget HTTP/1.1 client (`lib/std/http.gg`, ~220 lines) implementing `http_get`/`http_post`/`http_put`/`http_delete` with URL parsing, chunked transfer encoding, header parsing, and `HttpResponse` struct; (3) `examples/agentic_loop.gg` — interactive chat with Anthropic/OpenAI APIs, showcasing the language. Also fixed `resolve_expr_type_id` for `Expr::Index` — string range slicing (`s[a..b]`) now correctly returns `string_id` (str) instead of `int_id` (char), enabling method chains like `.trim().to_lower()` on string slices. Added `Expr::Index` case to `infer_receiver_type()` and TlsSocket return types to MethodCall handler. Eliminated libcurl dependency — only OpenSSL remains (shared with `std.crypto`). Files: `c_runtime.rs`, `stdlib.rs`, `codegen/mod.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_types.rs`, `c_expr.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `main.rs`, `lib/std/http.gg`, `examples/agentic_loop.gg`. Deleted: `tests/fixtures/http_get.gg`, `http_headers.gg`. All 471 unit + 236 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 14: Automatic Drop Glue for Structs. Structs with non-Copy fields (String, Box, File, or nested droppable structs) now automatically drop their fields when going out of scope, even without an explicit `equip with Drop`. Added `DropAction::StructDrop { type_name, has_user_drop, field_drops }` variant encoding per-field cleanup. When a struct variable drops: (1) user's `drop()` method runs first (if any), (2) each non-Copy field is recursively dropped in reverse declaration order. `drop_action_for_type()` maps AST `Type` → `DropAction` (or None for Copy types), `compute_field_drops()` walks struct fields via `struct_fields` + `field_type_names` to find non-Copy fields. `maybe_register_droppable()` refactored to use `drop_action_for_type()`, eliminating duplicate Box/File/Named/String matching logic. Test-body cleanup (`__gorget_cleanup_push`) handled by `emit_test_cleanup_for_action()` which recursively emits per-field cleanup entries. Also hardened `gorget_string_free` with `cap > 0` guard — struct literals like `Holder("hello")` store string literal pointers in GorgetString.data with cap=0; calling `free()` on a string literal is UB. The cap guard makes this safe (same pattern as move zeroing where memset→0 makes cap=0). Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `drop_struct_fields.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 472 unit + 237 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Fix cross-module span collisions in resolution_map. Added `base_offset: usize` to Lexer so each module is lexed with a cumulative byte offset, making all spans globally unique across merged modules. Previously, `resolution_map: FxHashMap<usize, DefId>` used bare `span.start` byte offsets as keys — when multiple file-based stdlib modules were merged, their spans all started at byte 0, causing key collisions (wrong C types, unresolved methods). Fix: Lexer gets `new_with_offset()` constructor and `span()` helper that applies the offset to all 28 `Span::new` call sites; Parser gets `new_with_offset()` that threads offset to Lexer; ModuleLoader tracks `next_offset` and assigns unique ranges to each parsed module; `load_imports()` returns concatenated source text matching the span offsets for use by ErrorReporter and CodegenOptions. This also fixes `method_resolutions` and `expr_types` maps. Files: `lexer/mod.rs`, `parser/mod.rs`, `loader.rs`, `main.rs`. All 472 unit + 236 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Add `Measurable` trait + fix `len(x)` free function. Registered `Measurable` as built-in trait in `resolve.rs` and `traits.rs` with signature `int len(self)`. Fixed `len(x)` codegen in `c_expr_call.rs` — replaced broken `sizeof(x)/sizeof(x[0])` (C array idiom, wrong for all Gorget types) with delegation to `gen_method_call`, which routes through the existing dispatch chain: built-in types → `try_gen_builtin_method` (Vector/Dict/Set/str/String inline C), user equip types → trait method call. Added `"len"` to return type resolution in `c_expr.rs`. New integration test `measurable_trait.gg` exercises `len()` on Vector, str, and a user type equipped with Measurable. Updated language-reference.md: trait table row, `len` free function description, `#### Measurable` subsection. Files: `resolve.rs`, `traits.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr.rs`, `measurable_trait.gg`, `integration.rs`, `language-reference.md`. All 472 unit + 236 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 13: Block-Scoped Drops. Added `DropScopeKind::Block` and wrapped `if`/`elif`/`else` branches, `match` arms, `with` blocks, and `unsafe` blocks with push/pop Block scope. Previously, variables declared inside these blocks registered their drops in the enclosing scope (Function or Loop), but the C variables are block-scoped — causing the cleanup code to reference variables outside their C scope. Block scopes ensure cleanup is emitted inside the C block (before `}`), where variables are still in scope. Applied to both main codegen path (6 sites) and break-flag code path for for-else/while-else (4 sites). All existing scope-traversal helpers (`emit_cleanup_to` for break/continue/return) already iterate through intermediate scopes, so Block scopes between variables and their enclosing Loop/Function scope make cleanup more correct. Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `drop_block_scope.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 472 unit + 235 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Fix Drop trait ownership: changed `self_ownership: None` (immutable borrow) to `Some(Ownership::Move)` (consuming `!self`) in built-in trait registration (`traits.rs:216`). Updated test fixtures (`drop_raii.gg`, `drop_reassign.gg`) from `void drop(self)` to `void drop(!self)` to match the language reference. This ensures correct `void*` (non-const) vtable slots when traits extend Drop, and makes the C function signature non-const so destructors can modify the object.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 12: Drop Safety (Move Zeroing + Drop-on-Reassign). Two codegen fixes for memory safety: (1) **Move zeroing** — when `!var` (move) transfers ownership, `memset(&var, 0, sizeof(var))` is emitted after the statement, making the scope-exit drop a no-op (`free(NULL)` is safe per C standard). Prevents double-free when both source and destination survive to scope exit. Uses `pending_move_zeros: Vec<String>` on `CodegenContext` to collect moved variables during `gen_expr`, flushed via `flush_move_zeros()` after each statement type (Expr, Assign, CompoundAssign, Return, VarDecl). (2) **Drop-before-reassign** — when a non-Copy variable is reassigned, the old value is dropped before the new one is stored. Uses temp-variable pattern (`__drop_tmp_N = new_expr; drop(old); var = __drop_tmp_N`) to avoid use-after-free when RHS references the target. `find_drop_action()` helper searches the drop scope stack. Key insight: zeroing keeps the drop scope entry, so no add/remove tracking is needed — the zeroed value produces a no-op drop, and on reassignment the new value replaces it. Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_expr.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `drop_reassign.gg`, `drop_move_zero.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 472 unit + 234 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Documentation audit — fixed conflicts and filled gaps. language-reference.md: added missing keywords (directive, via, test/suite/assert), added Iterable/Default/From/TryFrom traits to §15.1 with examples, fixed @derive example and listed derivable traits, added 6 missing std.fs functions and codepoint_to_str, documented 11 missing stdlib modules (json, toml, xml, bytes, crypto, http.client, net.socket, gfx, sdl, ecs, ssh), added CLI features (--hot-reload, --shared, --show-borrows, -i, package commands), added directive hot-reload, fixed std.sync import example. language-design.md: fixed Displayable method to_string→display everywhere, updated test syntax @test void→test "name", fixed operator traits to use generic parameter pattern (not associated types), fixed auto array literal inference, marked 12+ unimplemented features (concurrency, @cfg, @deprecated, @bench, doc tests, Debuggable, Copy, Sendable, Syncable, BTreeMap/LinkedList/etc, std.sync/thread/async/regex/log/encoding). traits.rs: fixed stale 4-trait comment. TODO.md: logged Drop trait ownership bug.
- [2026-02-18] Lifetime inference Phase 11: Reassignment invalidation. When a non-Copy owning variable is reassigned, all existing references borrowing from the old value are now detected as dangling. New `reassignment_invalidated: FxHashMap<DefId, (String, Span)>` field in `BorrowChecker` tracks dependent variables whose borrow source was reassigned. Unlike `invalidated_origins` (which marks the source and would prevent new borrows), this marks the specific dependent variables — preserving the source's usability for new borrows after reassignment. Three insertion points: (1) `Stmt::Assign` walks `var_origins` for dependents when a non-Copy variable is reassigned and inserts them into `reassignment_invalidated`. (2) `check_use` checks the map before the existing `invalidated_origins` check, reusing `UseAfterSourceMoved` error. (3) When a dependent variable is itself reassigned, its entry is removed (fresh value). `BranchState` extended with `reassignment_invalidated` for correct save/restore/merge across branches (union semantics). 5 new unit tests (reassignment_invalidates_borrow, reassignment_new_borrow_ok, reassignment_reborrow_ok, reassignment_copy_type_ok, reassignment_transitive). 2 new integration tests: `lifetime_reassign.gg` (positive — reassignment then re-borrow), `lifetime_reassign_error.gg` (negative — borrow invalidated by reassignment). Files: `borrow.rs`, `lifetime_reassign.gg`, `lifetime_reassign_error.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 471 unit + 230 integration tests pass (1 pre-existing hot_reload platform issue).
- [2026-02-18] Fix multiple `From[T]`/`TryFrom[T]` impls per type (mangling collision). When a type had both `From[int]` and `From[str]`, both generated the same C symbol `From_for_Type__from`, causing a duplicate symbol error. Fix: encode trait generic type arguments into the mangled name — `From[int]` for UserId now produces `From__int64_t_for_UserId__from`, `From[str]` produces `From__const_char_ptr_for_UserId__from`. Non-generic traits (e.g., `Displayable`) are unchanged. Changes: (1) `mangle_trait_method` gains `trait_type_args: &[String]` param; (2) `function_signature`/`emit_function_prototype`/`emit_function_def` method_info tuple widened to include trait type args; (3) New helpers `impl_trait_type_args`, `impl_trait_type_args_raw`, `lookup_trait_type_args` for extracting and looking up trait type args (with base-name fallback for monomorphized generics); (4) `TraitRegistry::trait_impls` key widened from `(DefId, TypeId)` to `(DefId, TypeId, Vec<TypeId>)` to allow multiple impls of same parameterized trait; (5) All call sites (`From`, `TryFrom`, `Index`, `IndexMut`, `Iterable`, `Iterator`, `Equatable`, `Add`/`Sub`/`Mul`/`Div`/`Rem`/`Neg`, `Comparable`, `Default`, `Drop`, `Displayable`) updated to thread trait type args through. Files: `c_mangle.rs`, `c_item.rs`, `c_expr.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs`, `c_stmt.rs`, `traits.rs`, `from_trait_multi.gg`, `integration.rs`. 467 unit + 229 integration tests pass (1 pre-existing hot_reload platform issue).
- [2026-02-18] Borrow analysis diagnostics and origin tracking improvements. Three features: (1) **`--show-borrows` diagnostic flag** — `gg check --show-borrows` and `gg build --show-borrows` print the inferred `return_borrows_from` for every function, showing which parameters' data flows to the return value. Helps programmers understand what the compiler inferred and builds trust in the inference system. (2) **Enriched error messages** — `UseAfterSourceMoved` now shows a secondary label at the move site ("`source_name` moved here"); `DanglingReturn` shows where the local variable was declared ("will be dropped when function returns"). Uses `codespan_reporting` secondary labels for precise multi-span diagnostics. (3) **Reduced `Unknown` origin fallbacks** — `compute_expr_origin()` now explicitly handles 15+ expression types that previously fell to the catch-all `Unknown`: value-type literals (int/float/bool/char → Static), binary/unary ops (→ Static), Range (→ Static), Is (→ Static), comprehensions (→ Static), OptionalChain/MutableBorrow/Await/Spawn (→ propagate inner), Path (→ resolve like Identifier). Fewer `Unknown` origins means more precise tracking and fewer missed bugs. Also filed two future analysis items to TODO.md: demand-driven refinement and `@guarded` self-referential struct support. Files: `main.rs`, `errors.rs`, `semantic/errors.rs`, `semantic/borrow.rs`, `TODO.md`. All 466 unit + 228 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Lifetime inference Phase 10: Cross-function callable lifetime tracking. Extended lifetime tracking to closures received from function calls (e.g., `auto f = make_closure(v)` where `make_closure` returns `Callable[void()]`). Previously, `compute_function_return_borrows` (Pass 5a) gated on `is_reference_type`, excluding callable return types — so `compute_call_origin` returned `Unknown` and the closure variable got no origin in `var_origins`. Six changes: (1) Extended `compute_function_return_borrows` gate to also accept `is_callable_type`. (2) Added `Expr::Closure` arm to `trace_expr_to_params` with `trace_closure_body_to_params` / `trace_closure_body_stmts` recursive walkers that find outer function params captured in closure bodies (excludes closure's own params and nested closures). (3) Extended elision fallbacks (declaration/extern and body-analysis) to include callable-typed params. (4) Extended VarDecl handler to track callable-typed variables from any expression (not just direct `Expr::Closure`), using `is_callable_type` check with `Expr::Closure` fallback for `auto` vars. (5) Extended `Stmt::Assign` handler to insert fresh origins for callable-typed reassignments. (6) Extended `mark_pattern_origins` and `collect_captured_ref_origins` to include callable types. 4 new unit tests (cross_function_closure_source_moved, cross_function_closure_ok, closure_reassignment_tracks_origin, closure_pattern_binding_origin), 1 new integration test (lifetime_closure_cross_fn_error). Files: `borrow.rs`, `lifetime_closure_cross_fn_error.gg`, `integration.rs`.
- [2026-02-18] Lifetime inference Phase 9: Closure lifetime safety. Two new safety checks: (1) **Use-after-source-moved through closures** — closures capturing ref-type variables now have their origins stored in `var_origins`, so `check_use()` detects when a closure's captured reference source is invalidated. (2) **Dangling closure return** — `Stmt::Return` and expression-body function checks now cover callable types (via new `is_callable_type()` helper), catching closures that capture local references from being returned. Added `is_callable_type()` to `types.rs` (matches Function, CallableTrait, MutCallableTrait, ConsumeCallableTrait, BoxedCallable). VarDecl handler stores `compute_expr_origin()` for closure-valued variables when origin is non-Unknown. Also fixed `compute_expr_origin` for `Expr::Identifier`: callable-typed variables no longer return `BorrowOrigin::Local(def_id)` — they use their `var_origins` entry (if present) or `Unknown` (safe, no ref captures). This prevents false DanglingReturn on legitimate closure escaping (e.g., `return f` where `f` captures only value-type data). 5 new unit tests (closure_capture_source_moved, closure_return_captures_local, closure_return_captures_param_ok, closure_return_literal_ok, closure_no_ref_captures_ok), 2 new integration tests. Deferred: cross-function callable lifetime tracking (return_borrows_from for callable return types). Files: `types.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `integration.rs`, 2 fixture files. All 460 unit + 226 integration tests pass (1 pre-existing hot_reload platform issue).

- [2026-02-17] Lifetime inference Phase 8: Origin completeness sweep. Filled all remaining gaps in `compute_expr_origin()` and `collect_captured_ref_origins()` for comprehensive origin tracking. (1) **`merge_origins` helper** — extracted common "filter Unknown, return single or CallResult" pattern used by 6+ call sites (Expr::If, Expr::Match, Expr::Closure, Expr::StructLiteral, method call origin, NilCoalescing, collections). (2) **Match expression origins** — match arm bodies now contribute to origin tracking, mirroring Expr::If pattern. (3) **SelfExpr origin** — `self` in equip methods resolves to param 0 from `current_param_def_ids`. (4) **Transparent wrapper origins** — Move, Deref, Try, TryCapture, As propagate inner expression origin. (5) **NilCoalescing origin** — `lhs ?? rhs` merges both sides. (6) **Collection literal origins** — ArrayLiteral, TupleLiteral, DictLiteral propagate element origins. (7) **Closure capture walker completeness** — added Expr::Match, Expr::DictLiteral, ListComprehension, SetComprehension, DictComprehension to `collect_captured_ref_origins()`. 2 new unit tests (`match_expr_origin_use_after_move`, `try_expr_origin_propagation`). Files: `borrow.rs`. All 453 unit + 223 integration tests pass (1 pre-existing hot_reload platform issue).
- [2026-02-17] Implement `Deserializable` trait + `@derive(Deserializable)`: Counterpart to `Serializable` for deserialization. Added `Deserializer` trait (17 methods: `read_bool`, `read_int`, `read_float`, `read_str`, `is_null`, `is_string`, `is_struct`, `is_seq`, `begin_struct`, `has_field`, `field`, `end_struct`, `begin_seq`, `elem`, `end_seq`, `has_error`, `error`) and `JsonDeserializer` struct with tree-walking implementation using `Vector[Json]` stack for cursor management. `@derive(Deserializable)` generates **free functions** (not equip blocks) since Gorget has no `Self` type in traits: `Result[T, str] deserialize_T(Box[Deserializer] de)`. Struct deserialization reads fields by name; enum deserialization discriminates unit variants via `is_string()` + string matching, data variants via `is_struct()` + `has_field()` + externally-tagged `{"VariantName":[fields]}` format. Nested types use `?` for error propagation (`deserialize_T(de)?`). Added `field_read_decl()` helper mapping field types to read declarations: int→`read_int`, float→`read_float`, bool→`read_bool`, str/String→`read_str`, char→`read_str` + index, sized ints→`read_int` + cast, sized floats→`read_float` + cast, anything else→recursive `deserialize_T(de)?`. Renamed `parse_and_collect_equip_blocks` to `parse_and_collect_derived_items` to also collect `Item::Function`. 7 new derive unit tests, stdlib assertion updated (4 equip blocks, +1 struct). Integration test proves lossless roundtrip: serialize → JSON string → `json_parse` → deserialize → re-serialize → verify output matches for struct, enum unit variant, enum data variant, nested struct. Files: `lib/std/json.gg`, `src/semantic/derive.rs`, `src/stdlib.rs`, `tests/fixtures/deserializable.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 442 unit + 219 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Lifetime inference Phase 7: Method & loop origin tracking. Three improvements: (1) **Method call origin tracking** — `compute_method_call_origin()` now uses `return_borrows_from` data instead of always returning receiver origin. Method DefIds threaded from TypeChecker to BorrowChecker via `method_resolutions: FxHashMap<usize, DefId>` (keyed by method span start). (2) **Loop origin merging** — `Stmt::For`, `Stmt::While`, and `Stmt::Loop` now save/restore/merge origin state across iterations using the same `BranchState` mechanism as if/match branches. A source moved inside a loop body now correctly invalidates refs used after the loop. For-else merges body and else branch states. (3) **Method-call temporary borrow detection** — `is_temporary_borrow()` extended from `Expr::Call`-only to also handle `Expr::MethodCall`. `str v = obj.build()` where `build()` returns `String` now correctly emits `TemporaryBorrow`. Extracted shared logic into `is_temporary_from_function()`. **Bonus fix: TypeId deduplication** — discovered that `TypeTable::insert(ResolvedType::Defined(def_id))` created a new TypeId each time, causing `resolve_method()` to fail for user-defined struct methods (receiver TypeId never matched equip block's self_type TypeId). Added `TypeTable::defined_id(def_id)` with `defined_cache: FxHashMap<DefId, TypeId>` for dedup. Also added equip method collection to the resolve pass so equip methods get proper DefIds and `FunctionInfo` entries (previously skipped, causing DefId(0) fallback). **Also fixed:** `process_impl()` in traits.rs now uses `lookup_def_by_span` instead of `lookup` to find equip method DefIds. 7 new unit tests, 4 new integration tests (lifetime_method, lifetime_method_error, lifetime_method_temp_error, lifetime_loop_error). Files: `typecheck.rs`, `mod.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `types.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `traits.rs`, `integration.rs`, 4 fixture files. All 442 unit + 222 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Implement Default core trait: Added `Default` as a built-in trait with a static `default()` method (`has_self: false`) — the first static-method trait in Gorget. `@derive(Default)` generates equip blocks for structs where each field gets its zero/empty value: `0` for integers, `0.0` for floats, `false` for bools, `""` for str, `String()` for String, and `Type.default()` for nested user types. Manual `equip Type with Default:` also supported. Codegen intercepts `Type.default()` method calls (where Type is a struct/enum) and emits either inline C zero values for primitives or `Default_for_Type__default()` calls for user types. Generic path (`Type[T].default()`) handles collections: `Vector/List/Array` → `gorget_array_new()`, `Dict` → `GorgetDict__K__V__new()`, `HashMap` → `GorgetMap__K__V__new()`, `Option` → `Option__T__None()`. Note: primitive type defaults (`int.default()`, etc.) are not yet usable from user code because the parser treats type keywords as non-expressions — codegen handles them for future parser support. Enum derive deferred (no obvious default variant). Files: `resolve.rs`, `traits.rs`, `derive.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `default_trait.gg`, `integration.rs`. 4 new derive unit tests, 1 new integration test. All 441 unit + 219 integration tests pass (1 pre-existing hot_reload platform failure excluded).
- [2026-02-17] Fix Iterable dispatch through generic field access: `for eid in self.health:` where `health` is `SparseSet[Health]` (which implements `Iterable[int]`) was falling through to C array iteration. Two bugs: (1) `infer_type_name_from_expr()` returned empty string for FieldAccess on generic types because `ast_type_to_type_id()` can't resolve generic Named types — added FieldAccess fallback that extracts base name from AST type annotation in `field_type_names`. (2) The Iterable/Iterator for-loop codegen used `infer_type_name_from_expr()` (base name, e.g. "SparseSet") for function name mangling, but emitted functions use monomorphized names (e.g. "SparseSet__Health") — switched to `infer_receiver_type()` which returns the mangled name. Applied same fix to Iterator codegen path. Updated `examples/ecs/world.gg` to use `for eid in self.health:` pattern in `team_alive_count`, `remove_dead`, `print_state`. Files: `c_stmt.rs`, `examples/ecs/world.gg`. All 422 unit + 214 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Implement format-agnostic Serializable trait with `@derive(Serializable)`: Added serde-inspired serialization architecture to `std.json`. Two traits: `Serializer` (format backend with 12 methods: `write_bool`, `write_int`, `write_float`, `write_str`, `write_null`, `begin_struct`, `field`, `end_struct`, `begin_seq`, `elem`, `end_seq`, `result`) and `Serializable` (type description with `serialize(self, Box[Serializer] ser)`). `JsonSerializer` struct implements `Serializer` with JSON output. `@derive(Serializable)` generates `equip` blocks for structs (field-by-field serialization with type-appropriate write calls) and enums (unit variants as strings, data variants as externally-tagged `{"VariantName":[fields]}`). Nested struct serialization delegates via `.serialize(ser)`. Added `field_write_call()` helper that maps field types to serializer calls: int→`write_int`, float→`write_float`, bool→`write_bool`, str/String→`write_str`, char→`write_str(char_to_str(...))`, sized ints→`write_int(... as int)`, sized floats→`write_float(... as float)`, anything else→recursive `.serialize(ser)`. Fixed `extract_trait_object_construction` in `c_stmt.rs` — added fallback to `resolve_expr_type_id` for concrete type resolution from variable references (previously only handled struct constructor calls). This enables `Box[Trait] x = Box.new(variable)` pattern. 4 new derive unit tests, stdlib assertion updated (3 equip blocks). Integration test covers struct, enum unit variant, enum data variant, nested struct. Files: `lib/std/json.gg`, `src/semantic/derive.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `src/stdlib.rs`, `tests/fixtures/serializable.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 426 unit + 215 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Refactor types to use `Iterable[T]`: Split 4 types that conflated collection identity with cursor state into separate collection (Iterable) and iterator structs. **LinkedList** → `LinkedList` (4 fields, no cursor) + `LinkedListIter` (values, nexts, cursor). Removed `reset()` method and `equip LinkedList with Iterator[int]`. Added `equip LinkedList with Iterable[int]` with `iter(&self)` creating fresh iterators. Main updated: `list.iter().fold(...)`, `list.iter().map(...)` instead of `list.reset()` + direct calls. **Counter** → `Counter` (start, max) + `CounterIter` (current, max). `for x in c:` now uses Iterable dispatch. Adapter calls go through `.iter()`: `Counter(0, 5).iter().collect()`. **Range** → `Range` (start, stop, step) + `RangeIter` (current, stop, step). `for x in r:` uses Iterable; adapters use `.iter()`. - [2026-02-17] Lifetime inference Phase 6: Hardening. Four sub-features strengthening the borrow checker: (1) **Branch origin merging** — `var_origins`/`invalidated_origins` now saved/restored/merged across if/else/match branches alongside `VarState`. Added `BranchState` struct combining var states, origins, and invalidated sets with `save_branch_state()`/`restore_branch_state()`/`merge_branch_states()` methods. Rewrote 4 branch sites (Expr::If, Expr::Match, Stmt::If, Stmt::Match). Moving a source in one branch now correctly triggers `UseAfterSourceMoved` for ref-type vars after the merge point. (2) **Pattern binding origin tracking** — match arm bindings (`case Some(x):`) now inherit origins from the scrutinee via `mark_pattern_origins()`. Reference-type bindings get the scrutinee's origin, enabling use-after-source-moved detection through pattern deconstruction. (3) **Temporary borrow detection** — `str v = make_string()` where a ref-type is bound to a function returning an owning type now emits `TemporaryBorrow` error. `is_temporary_borrow()` checks: must be a Call, callee must have `FunctionInfo` with a body (`has_body` field), non-generic, owning return type, no `return_borrows_from`. Added `has_body: bool` to `FunctionInfo` to distinguish user-defined functions from bodyless declarations/externs. (4) **Closure capture origin tracking** — `compute_expr_origin()` for `Expr::Closure` now walks the closure body via `collect_captured_ref_origins()` to find ref-type captures from outer scope with tracked origins, returning their combined origin instead of `Unknown`. New error variant: `TemporaryBorrow { name, callee }`. 11 new unit tests, 3 new integration tests (lifetime_branch_error, lifetime_pattern_error, lifetime_temporary_error). Files: `borrow.rs`, `errors.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `integration.rs`, 3 fixture files. All 433 unit + 217 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Implement `Iterable[T]` built-in trait: Added `Iterable[T]` as a built-in trait with a single `iter(&self)` method that returns a concrete iterator. For-loops auto-desugar: `for x in collection:` calls `collection.iter()` then uses the Iterator protocol on the returned iterator. This separates "thing you iterate over" from "stateful cursor", allowing a collection to be iterated multiple times (each `for` loop gets a fresh iterator). Implementation: Registered `"Iterable"` in built-in trait scope (resolve.rs). Added `Iterable` to `register_builtin_traits()` with `iter(&self)` signature using `Ownership::Borrow` (traits.rs). Added `has_iterable_impl()` and `get_iterable_iter_info()` codegen helpers that extract elem type from trait generic args and iterator type from the `iter()` method's return TypeId (c_stmt.rs). Inserted Iterable dispatch branch in `gen_for_loop` before Iterator — emits `iter()` call to get temp iterator, then standard while/next/Option loop. Dispatch order: Range → string → array → map → set → **Iterable** → Iterator → C array. 2 new unit tests (`iterable_trait_impl`, `iterable_missing_iter_method`). Updated existing test assertions for new trait count. New integration test `iterable.gg` covering: collection + separate iterator structs, two consecutive for-loops over same collection (proves fresh iterator), for...else with empty iterable. Files: `resolve.rs`, `traits.rs`, `c_stmt.rs`, `iterable.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 412 unit + 212 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Desugar string interpolation to `format()` outside print() context: `"{variable}"` in non-print expression contexts (variable assignment, function args, return values) now desugars to `gorget_format()` instead of emitting literal `%s`. In `c_expr.rs`, `Expr::StringLiteral` dispatch checks for interpolation segments and routes to `gen_gorget_format_from_string_lit` (made `pub(super)`). The `print()` path stays optimized (direct `printf`, no allocation). Updated `lib/std/xml.gg` to use interpolation instead of string concatenation in 3 error-message sites. Fixed latent bug in `lib/std/json.gg` and `lib/std/toml.gg`: unescaped `{`/`}` in string literals (e.g., `"{}"`) were being parsed as empty interpolation — escaped to `\{\}` for correct literal brace output. Added integration tests for assignment-context and function-argument interpolation. - [2026-02-17] Lifetime inference Phase 5: Named borrow groups + outlives bounds. Functions can now declare named borrow groups on parameters (`live(a) str x, live(b) str y`) and ordering constraints (`where a outlives b`). Named groups make `return_borrows_from` more precise — instead of all live params, body analysis determines which params actually flow to the return value, so moving a non-return-contributing param's source doesn't trigger false positives. The `where a outlives b` bound is enforced at call sites: if group `a`'s argument source is moved while group `b`'s source is still alive, the compiler emits `OutlivesViolation`. Implementation: Extended `Param` with `live_group: Option<String>` field. Changed `WhereBound` from struct to enum with `Trait` and `Outlives` variants. Parser: `live(name)` syntax in `parse_param` (peek for `(`, parse identifier, expect `)`), `where X outlives Y` in `parse_where_clause` (check for `Keyword::Outlives` before falling through to `is`). Extended `FunctionInfo` with `param_live_groups: Vec<Option<String>>` and `outlives_bounds: Vec<(String, String)>`. Added `extract_outlives_bounds()` helper. Borrow checker: added `ActiveOutlives` struct tracking per-call-site group-to-source mappings, `record_call_outlives()` method (populates constraints from `Expr::Call` when callee has outlives bounds), `check_outlives_on_move()` (checks violations when a "longer" group source is moved while "shorter" group sources are alive). Phase 3 modified: when named groups present, falls through to body analysis for precision instead of using all live params. Formatter updated for `live(name)` and `WhereBound::Outlives`. 3 parser unit tests, 4 borrow checker unit tests. 2 new integration tests: `lifetime_groups.gg` (positive — named groups, both directions), `lifetime_groups_error.gg` (negative — outlives violation). Files: `ast.rs`, `parser/mod.rs`, `errors.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `formatter/mod.rs`, `stdlib.rs`, `lifetime_groups.gg`, `lifetime_groups_error.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 420 unit + 213 integration tests pass.

Created `src/semantic/rewrite.rs` — a post-resolution pass that walks the entire AST and converts `Expr::Call` nodes whose callee resolves to `DefKind::Struct` into `Expr::StructLiteral` nodes. Extracts bare `Spanned<Expr>` values from `CallArg` wrappers. Skips collection types (Vector, List, Array, Dict, HashMap, Set, HashSet, Box) which have special C codegen. Verifies `def.name == callee_name` to prevent false rewrites from derive-generated span collisions. Wired into `analyze()` between resolve and typecheck passes. Added `Expr::StructLiteral` handling in `extract_trait_object_construction`. Removed Phase 4 struct-as-Call workaround from `compute_call_origin()` in borrow.rs — the `compute_expr_origin()` StructLiteral arm now handles struct origin computation directly. 3 unit tests in rewrite.rs. All 413 unit + 211 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Lifetime inference Phase 4: Automatic struct borrowing. Structs with reference-type fields (`str`, `Slice[T]`, or other ref-type structs) are now automatically tracked as borrowing from the sources of their reference-type field values. No annotation required — the compiler sees the field types and infers. A `struct View { str name }` borrows from wherever `name` came from, and becomes dangling if that source is moved. Implementation: Added `compute_ref_type_structs()` (fixpoint iteration over module AST `StructDef` items to find structs with ref-type fields, including transitive cases), `compute_struct_field_ref_flags()` (per-struct boolean vec of which fields are ref types), and `is_ast_type_ref()` (AST-level ref type check using `ScopeTable::lookup_from_scope(ScopeId(0))` for reliable module-scope lookup) to `borrow.rs`. Extended `is_reference_type()` in `types.rs` to accept `ref_type_structs: &FxHashSet<DefId>` and check `ResolvedType::Defined`/`Generic` against it. Updated all 7 call sites. Extended `BorrowChecker` with `ref_type_structs` and `struct_field_ref_flags` fields. `compute_call_origin()` now intercepts struct constructor calls (which come through `Expr::Call`, not `Expr::StructLiteral`) and computes origin as the union of ref-type field arg origins. `is_var_reference_type()` updated to recognize struct types via `is_ast_type_ref`. Param tracking in `check_function` updated similarly. `trace_expr_to_params` extended for `StructLiteral` in the pre-pass. 6 new unit tests (struct_str_field_auto, struct_outlives_source, struct_from_literal_ok, struct_no_ref_fields_unaffected, struct_transitive_borrow, struct_mixed_fields). 2 new integration tests: `lifetime_struct.gg` (positive — literal source, param forwarding, mixed fields, nested structs), `lifetime_struct_error.gg` (negative — struct borrows from local that gets moved). Files: `types.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `lifetime_struct.gg`, `lifetime_struct_error.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 410 unit + 211 integration tests pass.

Added `xml_hex_to_int()` helper for hex string parsing. Decimal refs validated digit-by-digit before calling `parse_int()` to avoid panics on malformed input. Added `parse_int` and `chr` to import line. 3 new integration test cases in `xml_parse.gg`: decimal refs (`&#65;&#66;` → `AB`), hex refs (`&#x43;&#x44;` → `CD`), mixed entities/numeric refs (`&#60;b&#62;` → `<b>`). - [2026-02-17] Lifetime inference Phases 1–3: Compile-time origin tracking that prevents dangling references with zero runtime cost. **Phase 1 (Foundation):** Added `is_reference_type()` to `types.rs` — identifies `str` and `Slice[T]` as reference types needing tracking. Added `BorrowOrigin` enum (`Static`, `Param`, `Local`, `CallResult`, `Unknown`) to `borrow.rs` with `contains_local()`, `references_def()`, `local_names()` helpers. Extended `BorrowChecker` with `var_origins`, `invalidated_origins`, `current_return_type_id`, `current_param_def_ids` fields. `check_function` now sets up param origins at function entry. `VarDecl` tracks origins for reference-typed variables. `Return` validates that returned reference-type values don't contain local origins (`DanglingReturn` error). Expression-body functions validated similarly. `check_move` invalidates origins on move. `check_use` detects use of reference-typed variables after their source was moved (`UseAfterSourceMoved` error). `compute_expr_origin()` traces through identifiers, field access, indexing, calls, if/match branches, blocks. Only non-reference-type local variables produce `Local` origin — reference-type locals without tracked origins (e.g. pattern bindings from match arms) fall through to `Unknown` to avoid false positives. **Phase 2 (Cross-function):** Two-pass borrow checking architecture. Pass 5a (`compute_all_return_borrows`) computes `return_borrows_from: Vec<usize>` per function by walking return statements and tracing expressions back to parameter names. Elision fallback: single ref-type param → borrows from it; `self`/`&self` method → borrows from self. Pass 5b does the full borrow check with origin tracking, using precomputed `return_borrows_from` for cross-function propagation via `compute_call_origin()`. **Phase 3 (`live` annotations):** Added `param_is_live: Vec<bool>` to `FunctionInfo`, populated from `Param.is_live` (already parsed). In Pass 5a, explicit `live` annotations take precedence over body analysis — `live` params' indices directly become `return_borrows_from`. New error variants: `DanglingReturn`, `UseAfterSourceMoved`, `MissingLiveAnnotation` with Display impls. 11 new unit tests (return_str_literal_ok, return_str_from_param_ok, return_str_from_local_string, use_str_after_string_moved, cross_function_borrow_ok, cross_function_chain, cross_function_dangling, live_param_explicit, return_str_from_expression_body_local, str_from_param_through_local_ok, str_view_reassigned_ok). 3 new integration tests: `lifetime_basic.gg` (positive — literals, param forwarding, chaining, `live`), `lifetime_dangling_error.gg` (negative — return local String as str), `lifetime_use_after_move_error.gg` (negative — use str after source moved). Files: `types.rs`, `errors.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `mod.rs`, `lifetime_basic.gg`, `lifetime_dangling_error.gg`, `lifetime_use_after_move_error.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 404 unit + 209 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] Operator overloading via traits: Implemented 9 operator traits (`Add[Out]`, `Sub[Out]`, `Mul[Out]`, `Div[Out]`, `Rem[Out]`, `Neg[Out]`, `Comparable`, `Index[K,V]`, `IndexMut[K,V]`) enabling user-defined types to use `+`, `-`, `*`, `/`, `%`, unary `-`, `<`/`>`/`<=`/`>=`, `a[k]` read, and `a[k] = v` write syntax via equip blocks. Registered traits in scope (resolve.rs) and method signatures (traits.rs). Added `try_operator_trait_type()`, `gen_binary_op_trait_call()`, `gen_unary_op_trait_call()` codegen helpers (c_expr_generic.rs). Wired: binary arithmetic ops dispatch after string/vector concat (c_expr.rs), comparison ops dispatch via `compare()` with optional `lt`/`gt`/`lte`/`gte` overrides, unary Neg dispatch, compound assignment desugar `target = target.op(value)` (c_stmt.rs), Index read before C `[]` fallthrough, IndexMut write after map subscript write. Added `has_method_for_type()` to TraitRegistry. Fixed name collision: extended `ScopeTable::define()` to allow user definitions to shadow built-in traits (dummy-span Trait defs). `Comparable` returns `int` (neg/zero/pos); comparison operators derived from compare result. New test fixture `operator_overload.gg` covering Add, Sub, Neg, compound `+=`, all 4 comparisons, Index read, IndexMut write. Documented in language-reference.md (§15.1). - [2026-02-17] Unified lifetime keyword: removed `life` keyword, replaced with `live`-only system. `live` on params = return borrows from this param; `live` on struct fields = struct borrows through this field; `live(name)` = named borrow groups for multiple independent lifetimes. Added `outlives` keyword for lifetime bounds (`where X outlives Y`). Updated lexer (`Keyword::Life` → `Keyword::Outlives`), parser, formatter, both language docs (reference + design), and EBNF grammars. All 393 unit tests pass.
- [2026-02-17] ConsumeCallable single-call enforcement: When the borrow checker encounters `Expr::Call` where the callee is a `ConsumeCallable`-typed variable (including `ConsumeCallableTrait` and `BoxedCallable { kind: ConsumeCallable }`), the call is now treated as a **move** of that variable. First call marks the variable as `Moved` (succeeds); second call triggers `DoubleMove` error; use after call triggers `UseAfterMove`; call in a loop triggers `MoveInLoop`; call in one branch conservatively marks as `Moved`. Added `is_consume_callable_var()` helper method on `BorrowChecker`. Wired into `Expr::Call` handler — ConsumeCallable callee goes through `check_move()` instead of `check_expr()` to avoid double-reporting. 3 new unit tests (`consume_callable_double_call`, `consume_callable_single_call_ok`, `consume_callable_loop_error`). 3 new integration tests with fixtures: `consume_callable_once.gg` (positive — single call + param pass), `consume_callable_once_error.gg` (negative — double call), `consume_callable_loop_error.gg` (negative — call in loop). - [2026-02-17] Rename `moving` keyword to `consuming` and `MoveCallable` to `ConsumeCallable`: Comprehensive rename across the entire compiler pipeline. Lexer: `Keyword::Moving` → `Keyword::Consuming`, string match `"moving"` → `"consuming"`. Parser: all `Keyword::Moving` references updated in `mod.rs`, `expr.rs`, `stmt.rs`; comments and unit tests updated. Semantic: `ResolvedType::MoveCallableTrait` → `ConsumeCallableTrait`, `ClosureKind::MoveCallable` → `ConsumeCallable`; string literals `"MoveCallable"` → `"ConsumeCallable"` in type resolution; error messages updated (`"! or consuming"` replaces `"! or moving"`); borrow checker ownership mismatch strings updated. Codegen: `CallableKind::MoveCallable` → `ConsumeCallable`; all string matching in `c_item.rs`, `c_types.rs`, `c_stmt.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs` updated. `FnOnce` retained as backward-compat alias for `ConsumeCallable`. - [2026-02-17] `Box[Callable]` trait objects (Closures step 4): Made `Box[Callable[int(int)]]` resolve to a proper trait object type, enabling heap-allocated type-erased closures with vtable dispatch. Semantic: added `ResolvedType::BoxedCallable { kind: ClosureKind, inner: TypeId }` variant; `ast_type_to_resolved` detects `Box[Callable[sig]]`/`Box[MutCallable[sig]]`/`Box[MoveCallable[sig]]` and maps to BoxedCallable; `resolve_type_deep` recurses into inner; unification handles BoxedCallable ↔ BoxedCallable (same kind), BoxedCallable ↔ CallableTrait (auto-boxing), BoxedCallable ↔ Function (auto-boxing from bare closure); Call dispatch extracts inner Function for arg checking; `validate_closure_arg_kind` handles BoxedCallable.
